Fix: мерить U на входе ESC, сравнить с ESC из таблицы, проверить сечения.

Thrust stand: load cell + HX711, current, RPM, efficiency
Обороты вдвое выше ожидаемых или C_T скачет от точки к точке

Cause: оптодатчик ловит две метки или блики от лопастей, неверный TACH_PPR или делитель полюсов для eRPM.

Fix: одна матовая метка, экран от света, сверка с внешним тахометром.

Thrust stand: load cell + HX711, current, RPM, efficiency
Мотор продолжает крутиться, когда завис ПК или отвалился датчик

Cause: безопасность привязана к циклу чтения датчика или нет таймаута команды.

Consequences: неуправляемый проп 15" — травма.

Fix: отдельный таймер безопасности, таймаут 0.5 с, аппаратный выключатель питания ESC.

Thrust stand: load cell + HX711, current, RPM, efficiency
После броска самолёт задирает нос и сваливается, руль высоты в упоре вниз

Cause: ЦТ позади нейтральной точки (SM < 0) — частая ошибка, когда АКБ сдвинута назад ради «лёгкого хвоста» или летающее крыло отцентровано по четверти корневой хорды.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: CTUN.Pitch растёт против CTUN.NavPitch, выход руля высоты в RCOU на пределе, PIDP.I сильно отрицательный.

Fix: пересчитать NP, сдвинуть ЦТ вперёд на 5–10 % САХ, повторить балансировку.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
На крейсере постоянно нужен руль вверх, посадка «плюхом», не хватает выравнивания

Cause: тяжёлый нос, SM > 20 %.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: устойчиво положительный PIDP.I, руль высоты заметно выше SERVOn_TRIM.

Fix: сдвинуть ЦТ назад на 3–5 мм за итерацию до запаса 10 %, затем заново выставить триммер.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
Раскачка по тангажу в FBWA/AUTO, AUTOTUNE выдал очень маленькие PTCH_RATE_*

Cause: малый запас (2–5 %) — слабая собственная «пружина» по тангажу.

Fix: ЦТ вперёд на 2–3 % САХ, после изменения центровки или полезной нагрузки повторить AUTOTUNE.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
Летающее крыло тянет к земле и требует сильного «руля вверх» даже при правильном ЦТ

Cause: Cm0 профиля отрицателен, нет reflex или крутки.

Fix: поднять элевоны на 2–4 мм механически, а не триммером автопилота; при большой разнице перейти на reflex-профиль.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
Время полёта на 30–40 % меньше расчётного по поляре

Cause: C_D0 занижен (антенны, камера, щели) или крейсер выше V_md.

Fix: тест планирования, пересчёт C_D0 через cd0_from_glide(), снизить AIRSPEED_CRUISE к 1.25·V_s.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
SERVO_AUTO_TRIM «уехал» к пределу, а самолёт всё равно не держит ровный полёт

Cause: автотриммер маскирует центровку или перекос установки, а предел у него 20 % от центра.

Fix: вернуть триммеры в механическую нейтраль, проверить ЦТ и угол установки ГО, только потом включать автотриммер.

The plane: stability, CG, static margin, drag polar
При отрыве аппарат резко переворачивается

Cause: неверный порядок или направление мотора, либо перепутан знак столбца (в Betaflight знак pitch противоположен PX4/ArduPilot).

Fix: снять пропы, тест моторов по одному, проверка знаков наклоном рамы рукой (шаг 3 перехода на железо).

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors
На резких креновых манёврах рыскание «отпускает» и аппарат доворачивает

Cause: штатная десатурация — рыскание режется первым (PX4 MINIMUM_YAW_MARGIN, ArduPilot MOT_YAW_HEADROOM, Betaflight pidsum_limit_yaw).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: рыскание в насыщении на пиках крена, actuator_saturation ≠ 0.

Fix: поднять T/W или запас по газу; на ArduPilot поднять MOT_YAW_HEADROOM; на PX4 проверить control_allocator_status.unallocated_torque[2].

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors
Кувырок или «проваливание» при нулевом газе на снижении

Cause: airmode выключен, на малом газе крен ужимается (в шаге 3 — до 16 %).

Fix: MC_AIRMODE = 1 после настройки (PX4), ACRO_OPTIONS бит 0 (ArduPilot), AIRMODE (Betaflight); для PX4 airmode по рысканию требует тумблер арма.

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors
После замены рамы или наклона моторов рыскание осциллирует

Cause: нормировка PX4 сохраняет команду, а физическое усиление выросло (×1.84 при 10°).

Fix: снизить MC_YAWRATE_K/P пропорционально росту эффективности и заново настроить.

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors
Гекса после отказа мотора крутится по рысканию и сносится

Cause: PNPNPN без запаса в части направлений; CA_FAILURE_MODE = 0 или детектор не сработал (нет телеметрии тока ESC для FD_ACT_*).

Consequences: без обработки отказа PX4 продолжает распределять момент на мёртвый мотор, и ошибка растёт до потери управления.

Fix: T/W ≥ 3, проверить раскладку (PPNNPN), включить CA_FAILURE_MODE = 1 и проверить детектор в SITL.

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors
ArduPilot со скриптовой матрицей не армится

Cause: SCR_ENABLE = 0, скрипт не в APM/scripts/, init() раньше add_motor_raw, FRAME_CLASS ≠ 15.

Fix: сообщения скрипта в GCS, порядок вызовов, перезагрузка после смены FRAME_CLASS.

Mixer and control allocation: the matrix, saturation, PX4 Control Allocation, tilted motors

Integrator

The spectra of two logs can't be compared

Cause: different fs or nperseg, different flight modes.

Fix: the same parameters, the same mode (hover).

Python for flight data analysis
Step_metrics is empty

Cause: the log has no sharp stick movements, or the threshold doesn't match fs.

Fix: a flight with sharp stick stops; pick the threshold from a histogram of d setpoint.

Python for flight data analysis
The spectrum peaks are smeared

Cause: RPM changed within the window.

Fix: a steady segment, a spectrogram instead of Welch.

Python for flight data analysis
Pymavlink takes very long to read

Cause: a log of hundreds of MB, read in Python message by message.

Fix: filter with type=[...], use mavlogdump.py --format csv for a one-off export.

Python for flight data analysis
Peaks in the log don't match eRPM

Cause: wrong motor_poles, aliasing due to a low logging fs.

Fix: check the number of magnets, log at the full rate.

Signals and spectra: what the gyro sees
A peak remains after the RPM filter

Cause: it's a resonance (doesn't track throttle) or a harmonic above rpm_filter_harmonics.

Fix: spectrogram; a static notch or mechanics; more harmonics.

Signals and spectra: what the gyro sees
Mushy response, bounce-back grew after adding filters

Cause: cutoff frequencies too low — large delay.

Fix: raise fc, replace PT1 → notch where the noise is narrowband.

Signals and spectra: what the gyro sees
“noise” below 100 Hz grows during maneuvers

Cause: propwash; it can't be filtered.

Fix: mechanics, D-term, don't lower the filters.

Signals and spectra: what the gyro sees
A strong peak at 100–250 Hz in the gyro log.

Cause: a frame resonance/play.

Fix: check the mechanics and make sure the dynamic notch is active.

Blackbox
Noise above 250 Hz is not suppressed.

Cause: the RPM filter is disabled or bidirectional DShot is set up incorrectly.

Fix: check E = 0% in Motors, enable the RPM filter (it exists only on the gyro — in both 4.5 and 2025.12; there is no separate D-term RPM filter).

Blackbox
Spikes in 20–100 Hz.

Cause: insufficient P/D or excessive filtering (delay).

Fix: check the delay in PIDscope (or PIDtoolbox), adjust the filters and PIDs.

Blackbox
The drone became “mushy” after filtering.

Cause: the cutoff frequencies are too low.

Fix: raise the gyro lowpass cutoff, check the D-term lowpass and the delay.

Blackbox
A log with gaps/artifacts.

Cause: write speed/buffer.

Fix: use a good SD card, lower the logging rate if necessary.

Blackbox
Nothing is recorded to onboard flash with Blackbox enabled.

Cause: the memory is full.

Fix: Erase Flash, download the logs regularly.

Blackbox
Incorrect frequencies in the spectrum.

Cause: sections of different length are being compared.

Fix: mark equal intervals with I/O.

Blackbox
The log is cut short / “Log ends abruptly”

Cause: the flash is full or cannot keep up (DMA/speed), a weak SD card.

Fix: erase the flash, a lower blackbox_sample_rate, disable unneeded fields, an A1/U3 class card.

Blackbox
The log has no gyro before the filters

Cause: blackbox_disable_gyrounfilt, or an old version without the field.

Fix: enable the field (set blackbox_disable_gyrounfilt = OFF); on 4.5 debug_mode = GYRO_SCALED also works.

Blackbox
The aircraft slowly yaws in hover, and raising yaw P or I does not help

Cause: a tilted motor or a bent arm, different props in a pair.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: I_yaw consistently above 5 %, I_pitch and I_roll near zero, a constant axisI[2].

Fix: check the shaft axis of each motor relative to the frame, straighten or replace the arm, fit props from one batch.

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
One motor is noticeably hotter than the others after a flight

Cause: it constantly runs at a raised command (a weak motor, a damaged prop, the CG offset toward it).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the median of that motor[i] is higher, and the index pattern points to its corner.

Fix: swap the props, then swap the motors; with a pitch/roll-only pattern — move the battery.

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
The script prints no steady segment >= 10 s

Cause: the flight had no 10 s without throttle changes, or the aircraft is oscillating (gyro RMS > 20 °/s).

Fix: check blackbox_high_resolution in the header (with 1 — --hires); for a manual hover raise thr_span_max to 40–50; a separate flight with 20 s of hover; if it oscillates in hover too — deal with the oscillation first (bf-pid, bf-filters).

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
The motor percentages in hover are 3–4 points higher than expected

Cause: a wrong --motor-range, for example 48 instead of 158 with dynamic idle off.

Fix: take motorOutput from the header of that log (grep -a motorOutput).

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
The indices jump from flight to flight by more than 2 points

Cause: wind, different segments (hover and a pass), the battery shifts in flight.

Fix: one program, calm air, a strap and a stop for the battery, compare segments of one type.

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
After the swap test the aircraft spins right at takeoff

Cause: a prop was fitted upside down or on a motor of the other rotation.

Fix: disarm, compare the rotation with the Quad X diagram, match the prop to the motor direction.

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
On 2025.12 get dshot_idle_value reports that there is no such parameter

Cause: it was renamed in 2025.12.

Fix: get motor_idle, the same default, 550.

Mechanics before PID: motor balance from the log
The motors are hot after a short flight

Cause: the D term is noisy — the RPM filter is not working (no eRPM) or D is too high for a noisy aircraft.

Fix: dshot_bidir, the errors on the Motors tab, motor_poles; the D spectrum; reduce D or the D LPF; the mechanics.

Filters
The drone “rattles” in hover, a buzz is audible

Cause: a gyro peak gets through to the PID — a resonance or the RPM filter missing its target.

Fix: the spectrogram: sloped → RPM/polarity/poles; horizontal → notch + mechanics.

Filters
A screech and loss of control authority on a throttle punch

Cause: a resonance in the working RPM range, broadband noise at high eRPM.

Fix: a throttle sweep, find the frequency, a static notch; a higher dyn_notch_max_hz (up to 800) for fast props.

Filters
After tuning it became “mushy”, bounce-back grew

Cause: excessive filtering — the delay increased.

Fix: raise the slider multipliers and the D LPF; check whether there are unneeded static notches.

Filters
A “fuzzy” gyro on the spectrum, a wide band

Cause: the RPM telemetry is invalid (E > 0%), or mechanics (bearings, play).

Fix: the ESC firmware, dshot_bidir, the signal wires; inspect the motors.

Filters
The spectrum before the filters is clean, after — peaks at new frequencies

Cause: aliasing/a wrong logging rate, or a wrong motor_poles (the notches land off target and create artifacts).

Fix: log at the full rate, check the number of magnets, debug_mode = RPM_FILTER.

Filters
The RPM filter doesn't enable, the Motors tab shows no RPM

Cause: ESC firmware without bidir DShot (original BLHeli_S), dshot_bidir is off, the wrong protocol.

Fix: check the ESC firmware, enable bidir, if necessary — Bluejay/AM32.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
A high percentage of DShot errors

Cause: interference on the signal wires, a DShot speed too high for the wire length, a poor ground.

Fix: DShot300 instead of 600, short wires, a common ground, ferrites if needed.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
A motor twitches or stops on a sharp throttle input

Cause: loss of synchronization (desync).

Fix: raise the demag compensation (Bluejay Low → High, AM32 demag_comp 0 → 2 → 4), then the timing one step higher — Bluejay raises the timing by itself when the demagnetization metric is high; check the bearings, the windings and the length of the phase wires. If the motors are hot as a result — restore the timing and use a smaller prop.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
AM32 shuts off a motor in mid-flight while the battery is still alive — or, conversely, never shuts it off

Cause: the per-cell cutoff is enabled (low_voltage_cut_off = 1), and the cell count was calculated when the ESC armed as voltage / 3.70 V: a sagging 6S below 22.2 V is counted as 5S, the threshold is lower than intended; with a charged pack and a high threshold — an early cutoff under load.

Fix: on a copter with a flight controller — low_voltage_cut_off = 0, the flight controller watches the battery; if a cutoff is needed — arm only with a charged pack, or use mode 2 (an absolute threshold).

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
The aircraft arms, but the motors don't spin; Bluejay beeps briefly when throttle is applied, AM32 silently ignores the throttle

Cause: Bluejay EDT safety arm = 1 or AM32 input_type = 4, while the flight controller does not send command 13 (dshot_edt = OFF, no bidir DShot, or a separate ESC telemetry wire is enabled).

Fix: set dshot_edt = ON and dshot_bidir = ON in Betaflight, or disable EDT arming in the ESC.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
Power drops sharply in hot weather or in a long climb, the ESC is hot after landing

Cause: the thermal protection triggered; with AM32 the first degree above the threshold leaves about 25 % of the power.

Fix: watch the temperature via EDT; raise the threshold to the real operating temperature with a margin, lower the PWM frequency, add airflow.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
After a crash the motors are silent

Cause: beacon in Betaflight is off by default; Bluejay waits for beacon delay of 10 min; the battery flew off — the ESC and the flight controller have no power.

Fix: beacon RX_LOST, beacon delay 1–2 min; for searching without a battery — a standalone buzzer.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
Passthrough does not find the ESC

Cause: Configurator/the GCS is open, no battery power to the ESC, IOMCU outputs in ArduPilot, the protocol is not DShot.

Fix: close the other programs, connect the battery, move to FMU/AUX outputs, enable DShot.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
After flashing a motor doesn't spin or heats up at rest

Cause: a wrong target/layout.

Fix: reflash with the correct target through the bootloader; if the bootloader is damaged — only with a programmer.

ESC firmware: Bluejay, AM32, BLHeli_32 and ESC configuration
The motors “whistle” or hum in hover, and it goes away with throttle

Cause: rpm_filter_min_hz is above the motor frequency at low throttle.

Fix: compute the motor frequency from the log and lower the limit.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
Arming is blocked, the OSD shows DSHOT_TELEM

Cause: one of the ESCs does not send DShot telemetry (firmware without bidir, a wrong protocol).

Fix: check the ESC firmware and the errors in the Motors tab with the battery connected.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
Telemetry errors in Motors are above 1%

Cause: interference on the signal line, a DShot rate too high for the chosen PID loop.

Fix: use DShot300 on a 4k/3.2k loop, check the solder joints of the signal wires.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
You enabled bidir, and after a reboot it is OFF again

Cause: the board does not support the chosen timer/DMA scheme, and Betaflight silently reverted the value.

Fix: re-read get dshot_bidir and get dshot_bitbang, check against the board documentation.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
The OSD shows DSHOT_TELEM, and in dshot_telemetry_info one motor has NO DATA

Cause: eRPM does not arrive from this particular ESC: firmware without bidir, a broken or badly soldered signal wire.

Fix: the ESC firmware (Bluejay/AM32/BLHeli_32 ≥ 32.7), the signal solder joint; DShot300 as a test.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
E% is 0 on the bench, but the motors heat up in flight and the peaks in the spectrum “wander”

Cause: telemetry errors appear only under load (interference from the power wires). The DSHOT_TELEM flag does not catch them; it checks only for the presence of eRPM.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: debug_mode = DSHOT_RPM_ERRORS, debug[0…3] above 100 (1 %) in a punch-out.

Fix: DShot300, the signal wire next to ground and away from the power wires, a ferrite ring if needed (a course guideline).

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
The motor peaks in the spectrum are not where the frequencies from debug_mode = RPM_FILTER are

Cause: a wrong motor_poles: the notches are shifted by a factor of poles_true/poles_set (14 against 12 — by 17 %).

Fix: count the magnets on the bell again, set motor_poles = <number of magnets>.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
After rpm_filter_min_hz = 30 the noise went away, but the aircraft became “mushy” at low throttle, and propwash did not decrease

Cause: a notch of width f/5 at 30–60 Hz adds phase delay in the band where PID works.

Fix: raise min_hz to the minimum working frequency from the log; enable dynamic idle (bf-pid-reg-in-bf) so that the RPM does not drop below it.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
After telemetry was lost in flight the motors are suddenly hot

Cause: without fresh eRPM the RPM notches froze at the old frequencies (an inference from the code), and 1–2 dynamic notches do not cover 12 harmonics.

Fix: restore telemetry; if you fly without it, temporarily dyn_notch_count 4–5 (bf-filters).

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
E% grows at high throttle

Cause: long/noisy DShot lines, 600 kbit.

Fix: DShot300, shorter wires, ground next to the signal.

RPM filter: the case of the "whistling" 10"
Fast oscillations (20–60 Hz) when raising P

Cause: the phase margin is used up because of delay.

Fix: reduce the delay (filters/mechanics) or P.

Control theory: stability, margins, cascades
Slow oscillations at 0.5–3 Hz

Cause: I too high, or the outer loop faster than allowed.

Fix: reduce I / ANG_P, check for windup.

Control theory: stability, margins, cascades
Bounce-back despite a good response

Cause: low ζ — too little D or too much delay in the D filters.

Fix: raise D, raise the D-LPF, check the noise.

Control theory: stability, margins, cascades
Overshoot only on sharp stick moves

Cause: too much FF.

Fix: reduce FF / smooth FF.

Control theory: stability, margins, cascades
Autotune produced a strange outer loop

Cause: the inner loop wasn't tuned before it.

Fix: rate first, then angle.

Control theory: stability, margins, cascades
The frequency response points “jump around”

Cause: small amplitude relative to the noise, a short recording, wind or pilot intervention.

Fix: larger amplitude (without saturation), a longer recording, calm weather, hands off the sticks.

System identification: chirp, frequency response, a model for tuning
At high frequencies the phase drops off without limit

Cause: the real delay is larger than in the model, filters, frame resonance.

Fix: evaluate the model in the range where it applies; handle resonances separately (notch).

System identification: chirp, frequency response, a model for tuning
The model fits well, but the gains based on it make the vehicle oscillate

Cause: a “setpoint → output” estimate instead of “plant input → output” in a closed loop.

Fix: take the actual signal into the plant (the controller/mixer output) as the input.

System identification: chirp, frequency response, a model for tuning
The motors hit their limits during the chirp

Cause: amplitude too large, low thrust margin.

Fix: reduce SID_MAGNITUDE, check the hover throttle.

System identification: chirp, frequency response, a model for tuning
It won't enter SYSID mode

Cause: SID_AXIS = 0, parameters not refreshed, the vehicle is on the ground, or the switch isn't from a manual-throttle mode (for axes 14–19 — not from LOITER).

Fix: set the axis, refresh the parameters in the GCS.

System identification: chirp, frequency response, a model for tuning
The step response “jumps” between neighboring maneuvers

Cause: a short log, a smooth flying style, ANGLE mode.

Fix: direct metrics per maneuver, medians, ACRO for the test.

Data-driven PID tuning
The motors are hot after a profile with high D

Cause: D amplifies the residual noise.

Fix: restore D, improve the filters, then repeat.

Data-driven PID tuning
Propwash does not go away with D

Cause: the aerodynamics of a heavy drone under small props.

Fix: different props/weight, TPA, do not push D up.

Data-driven PID tuning
Two identical drones give different numbers

Cause: a de facto different flight program.

Fix: compare profiles within one series of flights with one program and one charge window, normalize by the amplitude.

Data-driven PID tuning
Yesterday's “best” profile is no better today

Cause: the decision was made on one pair of flights, and the difference was noise or wind.

Fix: an A/A test to estimate the metric noise, then 4–5 flights per arm in ABBA order.

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The script prints nan for one metric

Cause: a maneuver did not fit the rule: a descent shorter than 0.5 s, snaps weaker than 200 °/s, a punch-out below 1900, a hover shorter than 2 s; or the window @t0-t1 cut it off.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: a zero in place of the maneuver in the segments h/m/d/p line.

Fix: fly the program with a margin in duration, check the windows; with blackbox_high_resolution = 1 run with --hires.

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The switch gives a d_roll of 41 instead of 40 (position A) or 45 instead of 46 (position B)

Cause: the channel endpoints do not reach 1000/2000 µs, and C division drops the fractional part.

Fix: endpoints in the radio out to 988–1000 and 2000–2012 µs, a check with get d_roll in both positions.

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The PID profile cannot be switched from a switch in flight

Cause: 4.5 and 2025.12 have no such function; in select mode adjrange can do only the rate, OSD and LED profiles, HORIZON_STRENGTH and PID_AUDIO (in 2025.12 also LED_DIMMER).

Fix: separate flights with the profile chosen on the ground, or an absolute slot for one gain.

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The profile changes by itself after the battery is changed

Cause: auto_profile_cell_count ≠ 0 in one of the profiles.

Fix: 0 in all the profiles for the duration of the experiment, a check of the profile name in the OSD before takeoff.

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The effect is there on one aircraft and absent on a second identical one

Cause: the aircraft differ in mechanics or props more than the profiles do.

Fix: a crossover design, the effect within each aircraft; motor balance from the log (bf-motor-balance).

The A/B method: tuning as an experiment
The motors twitch and heat up with still sticks, and in Blackbox FF is noisy “grass”

Cause: gimbal noise or the noise of a 250–1000 Hz link gets into feedforward; on 2025.12 — a feedforward_smooth_factor that is too low or averaging = OFF in a config that was carried over; on 4.5 the default 25 is “per packet”, so at 500 Hz it gives only 0.67 ms, and averaging is off.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: debug_mode = FEEDFORWARD_LIMIT, the field debug[3] (FF ×0.01 before averaging, the axis gyro_filter_debug_axis, roll by default; in 4.5 — debug_mode = FEEDFORWARD, debug[3], ×0.1) jumps with an almost constant rcCommand.

Fix: restore the 2025.12 defaults (65 and 2_POINT; on 4.5 set them by hand for 500 Hz), feedforward_jitter_factor 10–12. A test flight with f_roll/f_pitch = 0: if it becomes clean, the problem is in FF.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
At the end of a flip or a roll the aircraft overshoots the maximum rate and bounces back

Cause: feedforward_max_rate_limit = 0 or a feedforward_boost that is too high: FF keeps pushing at the stop (the lab: 40,500 and 29,250 °/s² on the first two packets at the stop, F ≈ 668 and 483).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: on 4.5.3 in debug_mode = FEEDFORWARD debug[3] ≈ debug[2] at the stop; on 2025.12 in FEEDFORWARD_LIMIT debug[3] (FF ×0.01) does not fall when debug[2] (the setpoint) ≈ debug[1] (the max rate); the gyro is above the setpoint.

Fix: restore the limit of 90 (the default), boost — no higher than the default 15 (10–15 is the author's guideline).

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
On 4.5.3 with ELRS 500 Hz / F1000 smooth movements lag, and FF is “weak”

Cause: jitter 7 with a step of 1.5–3 units per packet cuts FF to 0.38–0.67.

Fix: raise f_roll/f_pitch or lower feedforward_jitter_factor to 5 (the 4.3 Tuning Notes allow 5 only for racing on a clean link; a lower jitter lets through more gimbal noise — for motors twitching with a still stick you go the other way, 10–12); check debug[0] in FEEDFORWARD_LIMIT on smooth movements.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
The stick is “mushy”, and the aircraft lags on fast movements

Cause: a low packet rate (50 Hz, an 18 Hz cutoff), a rc_smoothing_auto_factor that is too high, or a manual cutoff.

Fix: rc_smoothing_info; raise the packet rate, restore factor 30 and *_cutoff = 0.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
At 50 Hz or with rc_smoothing = OFF the aircraft shakes finely and the motors are noisy

Cause: setpoint steps once per 20 ms, and D and FF amplify each one.

Fix: rc_smoothing = ON; for an unstable Crossfire — a manual cutoff of 20–30 Hz.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
rc_smoothing_info shows NO SIGNAL or a rate far below the packet rate

Cause: no link, poor LQ, ELRS in a different mode, frequent interval outliers.

Fix: check the link and LQ in the Receiver tab, repeat 2 s after recovery.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
The last millimeters of stick travel change nothing, the full rate comes “before the stop”

Cause: CRSF outputs 988–2012, and rcCommand is limited to ±500.

Fix: rxrange <ch> 988 2012 for channels 0–3.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
After rates_type was changed the aircraft became uncontrollably slow or fast

Cause: the numbers were not converted: Actual 7/67 in Betaflight is an RC rate of 0.07.

Fix: convert the profile with the converter, then check the maximum in PID Tuning → Rateprofile Settings.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
The rate profile switch changes nothing in positions 5–6

Cause: rate_6pos_switch = ON, while there are only four profiles.

Fix: a 3-position switch, or ranges for the indices 0–3.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
thr_mid was changed, but the throttle behaves the same

Cause: 4.5 firmware and thr_expo = 0 — there the curve without expo is a straight line. In 2025.12 thr_mid has an effect without expo too, if it is not equal to thr_hover.

Fix: on 4.5 set thr_expo to 20–50; on 2025.12 check the thr_mid/thr_hover pair and the hover point in PID Tuning → Rateprofile Settings.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
A diff after the setup does not contain the rates of the other profiles

Cause: diff/diff rates print only the current profile.

Fix: back up only through diff all.

Rates, RC smoothing and feedforward: from the stick to the setpoint
The adjustment switch changes nothing

Cause: the slot is inactive (the range channel is outside start..end, the end is not included), the CLI is open, or the function is empty (32–35 in 2025.12, 31–34 in 4.5).

Fix: adjrange — check the line; look at the range channel in Receiver; close the CLI; check the function number against the table.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The wrong parameter changes (you expected ROLL_F, and YAW_F is being turned)

Cause: the number was taken from the enum or from the betaflight.com table for another version.

Fix: CLI number = position in defaultAdjustmentConfigs[]; ROLL_F = 27, YAW_F = 28 (in both 4.5 and 2025.12).

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The value grows by itself every half second

Cause: the step switch is off center or the channel is not centered (the center is not 1500, mid_rc is different).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: a series of INFLIGHT_ADJUSTMENT events at 500 ms intervals.

Fix: a momentary switch, center 1500 ± 20 in Receiver, the switch warning on the radio.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
After the slot is enabled the quad abruptly changes its behavior

Cause: absolute mode, the pot off center — the value jumped.

Fix: center the pot before enabling the slot; for two parameters on one pot — a middle selector position without a slot.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
After a reboot the settings came back

Cause: not saved; the pitch stick did not reach min_check; Save in the app without Refresh wrote the old values.

Consequences: the tuning is lost, and Blackbox shows values that are not in the config.

Fix: a stick save with one beep, or CLI save; check the stick endpoints.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The PID profile does not switch with a switch

Cause: there is no such function (neither in 4.5 nor in 2025.12).

Fix: change the profile with the sticks while disarmed, from the OSD menu or with profile; in flight only rates, OSD and LED are switched.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The vtx band slot does not take effect

Cause: the aircraft has already been armed since power-up (the locked latch until reboot).

Fix: change the band/channel before the first arm (on the bench, after a reboot); check that the slot ranges on one channel do not overlap.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The VTX power does not go up on the bench

Cause: vtx_low_power_disarm = ON or UNTIL_FIRST_ARM, or there is no VTX table.

Fix: get vtx_low_power_disarm, vtx_info, vtxtable; on the bench that is how it should be.

In-flight adjustments: the Adjustments tab, `adjrange` and the VTX on a switch
The FLIP OVER AFTER CRASH mode is not in the Modes tab

Cause: the motor protocol is not DShot.

Fix: DSHOT300/600; without DShot turtle is impossible (in both 4.5 and 2025.12).

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
Turtle is on, but on arming the motors spin forward or do not spin

Cause: the ESC does not execute DShot commands 20/21; the turtle switch was raised after ARM; the stick is in the 15 % deadband.

Fix: the order “turtle → ARM”, exit only by a disarm; check on the bench; update the ESC firmware (f-esc-firmware).

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
Dynamic idle “does not work” although dyn_idle_min_rpm = 30

Cause: no eRPM (dshot_bidir = OFF, an ESC without bidir) — the target is silently zeroed; the value is in another PID profile; feature 3D.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: debug DYN_IDLE [0] is always 0.

Fix: dshot_telemetry_info, profile, get dyn_idle.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
Launch control does not engage (no LAUNCH in the OSD)

Cause: stick arming; ANGLE/HORIZON; feature 3D; MOTOR_STOP without airmode; the start has already happened with launch_trigger_allow_reset = OFF.

Fix: arm with a switch in ACRO; toggle the mode switch or reboot.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
3D does not arm, or editing 3d_deadband_low/high changes nothing

Cause: the throttle is outside 1451–1549 µs; 3d_deadband_* apply only to PWM, and with DShot the split is fixed.

Fix: throttle to center; the idle with DShot is motor_idle (before 2025.12 — dshot_idle_value), and it applies in both directions.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
Angle is sluggish near stick center, Horizon does not level in steep turns

Cause: Angle follows the rates (16.6° at half stick by default); Horizon fades toward 75 % of stick and 135°.

Fix: raise roll_rc_rate or use a separate rate profile; for Horizon — horizon_limit_sticks > 100 or horizon_ignore_sticks = ON.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
set acro_trainer_angle_limit gives ###ERROR IN set: INVALID NAME, there is no ACRO TRAINER mode

Cause: a build without the ACRO_TRAINER option, or no accelerometer.

Fix: rebuild in Cloud Build with the option (bf-custom).

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
The camera menu does not open with the sticks

Cause: the aircraft is armed; STICK COMMANDS DISABLE; the throttle is not within 1350–1650; the camera did not respond at FC startup.

Fix: the RunCam Device port and TX↔RX; a larger rcdevice_init_dev_attempts; for the joystick — resource CAMERA_CONTROL and the camera_control_internal_resistance of your camera.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
It does not arm, the PARALYZE flag, the switch is already off

Cause: the mode is “sticky”.

Fix: reboot the FC.

The other Betaflight modes and features: turtle, launch control, 3D, acro trainer, Angle/Horizon, dynamic idle, camera control
The CLI does not respond, commands are not executed.

Cause: the CLI was not entered correctly (# was not sent), a port conflict, a wrong baud rate.

Fix: make sure the CLI tab is active, close other applications with access to the COM port, compare the MSP baud rate on the port (serial/the Ports tab) with the terminal baud rate (for USB VCP it does not matter).

CLI and remap
After pasting a backup the drone does not work correctly.

Cause: a firmware version mismatch.

Fix: compare the version from the backup with the current version, restore from diff all and not from dump all, check the critical settings by hand.

CLI and remap
also used by MOTOR errors appear during remapping.

Cause: a temporary overlap of resources during sequential assignment.

Fix: first clear the assignments (resource MOTOR N none), then assign the new ones and save with save.

CLI and remap
After remapping the motors do not work or work incorrectly.

Cause: a wrong pin, a timer/DMA conflict, no DShot support on the selected pin.

Fix: run resource show and timer show, check them against the board pinout and the functions the pins support.

CLI and remap
The save command hangs or the controller does not reboot.

Cause: an unstable USB connection, power, a corrupted configuration.

Fix: reconnect power and USB, check the cable, and if it happens again run defaults and restore step by step.

CLI and remap
The BUILD KEY cannot be obtained or the URL returns an error.

Cause: an old Betaflight version (< 4.4, before Cloud Build), the key was not copied completely, a wrong URL.

Fix: make sure the version is no lower than 4.4, copy the key again, and if necessary try the alternative endpoint .../<BUILD_KEY>/log.

CLI and remap
The script “lost” the drone after save

Cause: after the reboot the COM port got a different number.

Fix: find the port again by craft_name through MSP_NAME, not by number.

CLI automation over serial
A written value is different after the reboot

Cause: Betaflight corrected an incompatible combination in validateAndFixConfig (d_max < D, GPS-RESCUE without GPS, bidir without DShot).

Fix: re-read the related parameters, check them against the table of fixes above and against the board and firmware documentation.

CLI automation over serial
After pasting a diff some lines gave INVALID NAME errors

Cause: the diff is from another firmware version.

Consequences: if a batch was opened with batch start, the first save will refuse and nothing will be saved (the changes stay in RAM until reboot; a repeated save will save them); without a batch everything is applied except the failing lines.

Fix: carry settings over section by section, checking the parameter names in the help of the current version.

CLI automation over serial
Two drones respond to the same craft_name

Cause: identical names (a cloned config).

Fix: rename right away, set craft_name = ... + save; keep MSP_UID next to the name.

CLI automation over serial
save noreboot rebooted the board

Cause: firmware 4.5.3 or older: the argument is not read.

Fix: determine the version over MSP before writing, and on 4.5.3 always wait for a reboot.

CLI automation over serial
After exit noreboot the aircraft does not arm

Cause: 2025.12 keeps the CLI arming disable flag until reboot.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: status → Arming disable flags: CLI.

Fix: reboot the board before flying; use MSP or STX/ETX for reading values.

CLI automation over serial
A diff all backup is cut off in the middle

Cause: reading “until # ” fired on the comment line # master at a chunk boundary; or a fixed timeout is shorter than the output time on a large F7/H7 config.

Fix: the end of the output is the prompt plus 0.3 s of silence; check that the file ends with the save line.

CLI automation over serial
status → GPS: NOT CONNECTED or NOT ENABLED

Cause: feature GPS is off; TX/RX are not crossed over; the module has no power (on USB some boards do not power the GPS pad); the GPS function is on the wrong UART; SoftSerial.

Fix: feature GPS, serial with function 2 (GPS) on the right UART, a continuity check, power from a battery without propellers.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
It does not arm, the GPS flag in status

Cause: Rescue is configured (the procedure or the mode), and there is no fix or fewer satellites than gps_rescue_min_sats; or Rescue is configured with feature GPS off — then the flag stays forever.

Consequences: an arm without home gives RESCUE N/A — on failsafe the aircraft will not fly home.

Fix: wait under an open sky, watch the OSD and the GPS tab; do not lower gps_rescue_min_sats below 8 and do not enable gps_rescue_allow_arming_without_fix just to be able to arm.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
Few satellites or a slow fix, while with the VTX off everything is fine (question No. 17)

Cause: interference from the VTX or the digital air unit raises the noise floor; a cold start is especially sensitive (ephemeris decoding).

Fix: the “VTX off/on” bench test, the module farther from the VTX antenna and higher, the antenna facing the sky, turn the VTX on after the fix, a battery or capacitor on V_BCKP for a hot start.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
The M10 does not deliver 10 Hz, the NAV interval in debug_mode = GPS_CONNECTION is more than 100 ms

Cause: the factory GPS+GAL+BDS set of the MAX-M10S is limited to 3 Hz (datasheet).

Fix: gps_update_rate_hz = 3, or through u-center the GPS+GAL set (10 Hz) saved to BBR.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
An M10 on Betaflight 4.4 and earlier works poorly or erratically

Cause: 4.4 does not know the M10 and sends only the old CFG messages; the GPS tab documentation requires 4.5 for the M10.

Fix: update to 2025.12 (or at least to 4.5, if the board is not supported by anything newer).

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
An M10 with ROM SPG 5.00 firmware (MON-VER) loses accuracy with a strong signal

Cause: known limitation 14 from the 5.00 release notes — the “balanced” power mode. Betaflight (verified on 4.5-maintenance) sends M9+ only CFG-PM-OPERATEMODE = FULL, and CFG-PMS only to M8 modules.

Fix: u-center → CFG-PMS full power saved to BBR, or a module with SPG 5.10.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
The home arrow points the wrong way right after takeoff

Cause: there is no compass, and the GPS heading has not been learned yet (it needs a speed above 1 m/s, the nose tilted forward, a roll below 12°, the yaw stick centered).

Fix: a straight pass of 30–50 m at more than 2 m/s, then check the arrow; if it has not settled — land and reboot.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
Home turned out to be at the landing point, not the takeoff point

Cause: gps_set_home_point_once = OFF and a disarm in the field — the new arm overwrote home.

Fix: gps_set_home_point_once = ON for flights with intermediate landings, the first arm at the launch site.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
gps_ublox_use_galileo = ON, but there are no Galileo (E) satellites on the GPS tab — or they are there with OFF

Cause: Galileo is changed only through CFG-GNSS; the step is skipped with SBAS ≠ NONE and Galileo OFF; the M10 documents only VALSET.

Fix: check the satellite list on the GPS tab; if necessary set CFG-SIGNAL in u-center and save to BBR.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
GPS loss in flight, status shows a rate of 115200, the NAV intervals jump around in the logs

Cause: frame loss at 115200 (documentation).

Fix: serial … 57600 … for GPS, check the load per step 3.

GPS in Betaflight: M8/M10 modules, configuration, accuracy and the home point
The CLI has no servo/smix commands, and there is no Servos tab

Cause: a Cloud Build without the SERVOS option (USE_SERVOS is only in the non-cloud block of common_pre.h).

Fix: rebuild with SERVOS, restore diff all.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The servo does not move, and resource looks correct

Cause: SERVO 1 is empty (servoDevInit stops at the first empty one), the feature is not enabled or the mixer has no servos, the pin is still held by MOTOR n.

Fix: resource show — the owner of the pin must be SERVO; assign from SERVO 1 in order; resource MOTOR n NONE; feature SERVO_TILT or CHANNEL_FORWARDING.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
After a servo was moved two motors do not spin or twitch

Cause: the servo pad is on a channel of a motor timer — servoDevInit() overwrote the DShot time base.

Fix: timer show, move the servo to a timer without motors (on the SpeedyBee F405 V4 — TIM2/M7).

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
smix was entered, but nothing happens on a quad

Cause: smix rules are applied only in CUSTOMAIRPLANE/CUSTOMTRI; a rule with rate 0 terminates the table.

Fix: for a gimbal — SERVO_TILT and servo … rate; for an airplane — mixer CUSTOMAIRPLANE, the rules in order from 0.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The camera is stabilized too strongly or in the opposite direction

Cause: rate 100 gives 20 µs/°; the sign is wrong.

Fix: rate = 5 × (the servo's µs/°), the sign — from a tilt test.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The servo hits the stop and hums at the ends of the forward channel

Cause: CHANNEL_FORWARDING outputs RC as is, and the servo min/max do not apply.

Fix: limit the travel on the radio (the channel endpoints) or switch to SERVO_TILT with the forward field.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The flight controller reboots when the servo moves

Cause: the servo is on the flight controller's 5 V, a sag at the stall current.

Consequences: in flight — a disarm and a crash.

Fix: a separate BEC, a common ground, a capacitor; check the stall current.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The camera buttons through CAMERA_CONTROL stopped working after the servo was set up

Cause: CAMERA_CONTROL in HARDWARE_PWM on the same timer (on the SpeedyBee F405 V4 — TIM12).

Fix: resource CAMERA_CONTROL NONE or set camera_control_mode = SOFTWARE_PWM (available on F405), or the servo on another timer.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
An analog servo heats up and hums

Cause: servo_pwm_rate is above its specification.

Fix: return to 50 Hz; 200–333 Hz — only for digital servos per the datasheet.

Servos, PWM and timers: `resource SERVO`, `servo`, `smix`, SERVO_TILT and channel forwarding
The strip does not light up

Cause: the firmware has no LED_STRIP (the feature is cleared after a reboot), the first led line is zero, the pin has no DMA/timer, LEDLOW is on.

Fix: feature, led, resource, timer show, dma show, the Modes tab; rebuild with the option.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
After the strip was connected the ESC beeps “no signal” and a motor does not spin

Cause: the LED is on a motor's timer, or it took the TIM1/TIM8 that bitbang needs.

Fix: timer show; move the LED to another pad or change dshot_bitbang_timer; check the motors without props.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
Flicker, random colors, only the first one lights up

Cause: 3.3 V against 0.7·5 V, a long data wire, no common ground.

Fix: a common ground, a resistor at the strip, a 74AHCT125 buffer or a diode in the strip's positive lead.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
Green lights up red; a “mess” from the second LED on

Cause: ledstrip_grb_rgb (RGB/GRB) or an RGBW strip.

Fix: one LED with C:6; red — change the order; a “mess” — GRBW.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
Only part of the strip lights up

Cause: a zero led in the middle, or more than 32 without LED_STRIP_64.

Fix: find 0,0::C:0 in led.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
The FC reboots or the video drops out on white

Cause: sag of the 5 V BEC.

Fix: a measurement under load; a separate BEC with a common ground; brightness 30.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
Turned the radio off — the aircraft is silent

Cause: less than failsafe_delay has passed, or less than 5 s since power-up; the RX_LOST condition is not met (subsection “RX_LOST…”: USB, no arm in 4.5); beeper -RX or -RX_LOST; BEEPER MUTE; -ON_USB on USB power.

Fix: wait 2 s, beeper, Modes; disconnect USB and restart the FC from the battery, test after an arm without props.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
After an impact the aircraft lies intact, the battery is in place, but it is silent (4.5)

Cause: the FC rebooted from the impact or a bouncing connector — WAS_EVER_ARMED is cleared, and RX_LOST does not sound in 4.5.

Fix: rxfail <3 + n> s 2000 on the BEEPER channel (AUXn) or a move to 2025.12; during the search — turn on the radio and the BEEPER switch.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
The board without the radio beeps short 0.1/0.1 s beeps right away

Cause: rxfail … s on the BEEPER channel — that is by design; or the BEEPER switch in h is stuck on.

Fix: turn the radio on; if it is a nuisance on the bench — the BEEPER MUTE switch, not removing rxfail.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
rxfail 10 a → ###ERROR IN rxfail: PARSING FAILED###

Cause: the a mode exists only for channels 0–3.

Fix: for AUX — h or s <µs>.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
The motors do not beep on signal loss

Cause: beacon is off by default; not DShot; < 1.2 s after the disarm; the FC sees USB (2025.12, see the RX_LOST condition); in 4.5 only beacon RX_SET is enabled while RX_LOST is sounding; in 2025.12 the RX_SET beacon from rxfail does not work without the link.

Fix: beacon RX_LOST, get motor_pwm_protocol, a test from the battery.

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon
The beeper sounds continuously from power-up

Cause: a wrong beeper_inversion / beeper_od.

Fix: toggle it, save, check against the board config (get beeper).

LEDs, the beeper and beacons: LED_STRIP, `led` / `color` / `mode_color`, beeper, DShot beacon

State estimation and navigation

The OSD horizon is tilted at rest

Cause: accelerometer bias / calibration on an uneven surface.

Fix: 6-position calibration on a level surface.

IMU sensors: physics, errors, calibration
Heading drift after compass calibration

Cause: calibration near metal/indoors, wrong COMPASS_ORIENT.

Fix: calibrate in the field, check the orientation, is |B| constant?

IMU sensors: physics, errors, calibration
EKF variances grow after warm-up

Cause: temperature drift of the gyro bias.

Fix: INS_TCAL, heating, let the board warm up before arming.

IMU sensors: physics, errors, calibration
VIBE.Clip > 0

Cause: the accelerometer saturates from vibration/impacts.

Fix: mechanics, damping, accel range.

IMU sensors: physics, errors, calibration
The heading is wrong when tilted

Cause: the compass isn't tilt-compensated (no attitude estimate) or soft iron.

Fix: full calibration with the matrix, check that attitude is being computed.

IMU sensors: physics, errors, calibration
The OSD horizon drifts after hard maneuvers and slowly comes back

Cause: the gyro accumulated error, Kp is small — slow recovery.

Fix: normal for Acro; for Angle — raise Kp moderately, check vibrations.

Attitude: Euler angles, quaternions, complementary filter
Loiter moves in a spiral, the heading in the OSD doesn't match the real one

Cause: yaw drift / a bad compass.

Fix: compass, GPS yaw, GSF; check for interference.

Attitude: Euler angles, quaternions, complementary filter
Your own filter diverges from ATT by 5–10° during maneuvers

Cause: no compensation for centripetal acceleration.

Fix: add correction from GPS velocity or reduce trust in the accel when |a| ≠ g.

Attitude: Euler angles, quaternions, complementary filter
NaN in the quaternion

Cause: normalization skipped or acc = 0.

Fix: check norms, guard divisions.

Attitude: Euler angles, quaternions, complementary filter
XKF4.SH > 1 on takeoff

Cause: baro in the prop wash/airflow; the configured R (EK3_ALT_M_NSE) is too small to cover this error.

Fix: cover the baro with foam, EK3_ALT_M_NSE, a rangefinder as the height source near the ground.

The Kalman filter in plain terms, and the EKF
Position innovations grow, then “EKF variance” and a failsafe

Cause: GPS glitch/multipath, or the compass is lying and the model drifts off.

Fix: GPS.NSats/HDOP, XKF4.SM; EKF sources, GPS check.

The Kalman filter in plain terms, and the EKF
Your own filter diverges (P grows without bound)

Cause: all measurements are rejected by the gates after a systematic error.

Fix: temporarily widen the gates / reset to the measurement on a prolonged failure (like an EKF reset).

The Kalman filter in plain terms, and the EKF
A lane switch in the log

Cause: one IMU is noisier (vibration) — the core using it accumulated error.

Fix: mechanics, check the IMU; fine if it's rare.

The Kalman filter in plain terms, and the EKF
A long satellite search after power-on

Cause: cold start, the antenna under carbon, interference from USB/the camera.

Fix: a mast, a ground plane, see the EMI module; give it 1–2 minutes.

GNSS: how GPS works, errors, RTK
HDOP > 2 with 15 satellites

Cause: satellites on one side (a wall, a roof).

Fix: open sky; don't arm in GPS modes.

GNSS: how GPS works, errors, RTK
Position jumps by 10–30 m in a city

Cause: multipath.

Fix: higher above the frame, a helical antenna, don't trust Loiter near buildings.

GNSS: how GPS works, errors, RTK
“GPS glitch” in ArduPilot messages

Cause: a position/velocity jump beyond the EKF gate.

Fix: the GPS/XKF4 log, the interference source; if it repeats — the failsafe settings.

GNSS: how GPS works, errors, RTK
RTK doesn't reach Fixed

Cause: too far from the base, a poor RTCM link, a single-frequency receiver.

Fix: check the RTCM stream, the distance, the antennas on both ends.

GNSS: how GPS works, errors, RTK
Pre-arm "EKF compass variance"

Cause: interference from the power wiring, a poor calibration, the compass in the GPS module mounted rotated.

Fix: calibrate in a clean location, move the compass away, check its orientation.

EKF and navigation
In LOITER the vehicle slowly drifts or circles ("toilet bowling")

Cause: a heading error (compass) with a good GPS.

Fix: check the difference between the compass heading and the GPS heading in the log, recalibrate.

EKF and navigation
A sudden lurch sideways in position modes

Cause: a GPS glitch accepted by the filter.

Fix: look at ERR/GPS in the log, HDOP and the satellite count, the GPS antenna.

EKF and navigation
EKF failsafe and a landing in the middle of a flight

Cause: variances above the threshold because of vibration, the compass, or the GPS.

Fix: analyze the log (next module); do not raise FS_EKF_THRESH blindly.

EKF and navigation
PreArm: AHRS: not using configured AHRS type on a copter

Cause: AHRS_EKF_TYPE = 0 or 2 on a build without EKF2; on a plane, EKF3 is unhealthy and DCM is active while the compass is in use.

Fix: AHRS_EKF_TYPE = 3, EK3_ENABLE = 1, reboot; on a plane, find out why EKF3 is unhealthy (XKF4).

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
EKF variance and Land although all the variance bars in the GCS are green

Cause: the EKF has no horizontal position: GPS dropped out, or the active source set has no position.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: XKF4.SS without bits 3 and 4, GPS.Status dropped, variances near 0.

Fix: find out why the source disappeared (GPS power, connector, the EK3_SRCn set) and leave the threshold alone; add a backup set with flow or VIO (step 4).

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
EKF3 lane switch in flight with no visible cause

Cause: one IMU is noisier: vibration, clipping, heating.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: XKF4.SV/SH of one core grow and those of the other do not; VIBE.Clip on that IMU.

Fix: mechanics and damping (ap-logs); occasional switches are normal, frequent ones are not.

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
AHRS: DCM active in the GCS on a plane

Cause: EKF3 is not fusing GPS or has lost position with a working 3D fix, or it has entered constant position mode.

Consequences: position-controlled modes run on raw GPS, with no gates against glitches.

Fix: XKF4.SP, GPA.HAcc, glitches; disable the fallback (AHRS_OPTIONS bit 0) only deliberately.

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
A compass-less copter does not arm in Loiter, and after a takeoff in AltHold it yaws around and will not enter Loiter

Cause: the GSF yaw is not aligned: the vehicle is hovering or flying slower than 1–2 m/s.

Fix: straight flight for 5–10 s at 3–5 m/s until EKF3 IMU0 yaw aligned using GPS; watch for XKY0.YCS < 5°.

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
EKF3 IMU0 emergency yaw reset in flight

Cause: the compass yaw went wrong (interference from current), GSF rescued the yaw and marked the compass as failed.

Fix: ap-sensors (CompassMot, moving the compass away); remember that there are no more than EK3_GSF_RST_MAX = 2 resets per flight.

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
A plane with no GPS returns to the "wrong place", tens of meters off

Cause: there is no airspeed sensor, or the EKF is rejecting it (bit 17 in XKF4.SS).

Fix: fit and calibrate a sensor (ap-sensors); dead reckoning with it drifts about 12 m a minute in a 7 m/s wind against 96 m without it.

EKF3, EKF2 or DCM: how ArduPilot estimates attitude and position, and which to choose
Altitude “sags” on takeoff and jumps on landing

Cause: prop wash on the baro.

Fix: foam, relocate the baro, a rangefinder as POSZ near the ground.

Altitude and position without GPS: baro, rangefinder, optical flow
Loiter without GPS drifts on flow

Cause: poor texture/lighting, the flow scale isn't calibrated, no rangefinder.

Fix: calibrate FXSCALER, add lighting, texture; a rangefinder is mandatory.

Altitude and position without GPS: baro, rangefinder, optical flow
The rangefinder shows max over a field

Cause: the surface doesn't reflect (water, black asphalt), range exceeded, angle.

Fix: a different type (radar over water), a realistic RNGFND1_MAX_CM.

Altitude and position without GPS: baro, rangefinder, optical flow
“EKF needs position” and Loiter won't engage

Cause: no POSXY/VELXY source (GPS disabled, flow not set up).

Fix: check EK3_SRC1_VELXY, the flow status in OF.

Altitude and position without GPS: baro, rangefinder, optical flow
Altitude drifts by 10 m over half an hour while parked

Cause: weather/temperature drift of the baro.

Fix: that's normal; the relative takeoff altitude is reset at arming.

Altitude and position without GPS: baro, rangefinder, optical flow
“EKF variance” and a switch to Land for no visible reason

Cause: compass/GPS disagreement — interference or multipath.

Fix: XKF4.SM/SP at the moment of the event; onboard EMI; yaw sources.

Navigation and link resilience to interference
Satellites drop out only over a specific spot

Cause: external interference or shielding (power lines, buildings, an industrial facility).

Fix: mark the zone, don't fly GPS modes there; C/N0 telemetry.

Navigation and link resilience to interference
GPS shows excellent accuracy, but Loiter drifts off

Cause: bad data with a “good” fix (spoofing or a receiver error), or the compass.

Fix: EKF innovations, comparison with baro/flow, land in AltHold.

Navigation and link resilience to interference
On GPS loss the vehicle sagged/shot off abruptly

Cause: the failsafe action doesn't fit the scenario, the pilot isn't ready for AltHold.

Fix: revisit FS_EKF_ACTION, train the manual modes.

Navigation and link resilience to interference
RC drops out when a neighbor switches on their radio

Cause: an overloaded band / identical binding phrase.

Fix: frequency plan, a different band, a second receiver.

Navigation and link resilience to interference
Fix застрял на 5 (float), fixed не наступает

Cause: база дальше 50–100 км, нет 1230 (GLONASS float), антенна ровера без ground plane / под пропами, многолучёвость.

Fix: ближайший mount point, проверить состав RTCM в окне RTK (должны идти 1005 + MSM + 1230), поднять антенну, отключить GLONASS в GPS1_GNSS_MODE на пробу.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
Fix 6 есть, но позиция в Loiter уходит на метры

Cause: база после survey-in с ошибкой 2 м — это относительная точность; либо перезапущенный survey-in дал другую точку.

Fix: для абсолютной точности — известные координаты базы; дом/миссию задавать уже при fixed.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
GPS2_RAW.yaw = 65535 при fix 6

Cause: длина линии по GPSx_POS расходится с измеренной > 20 %, антенны ближе 5 см, разная высота антенн, RELPOSNED не приходит (нет 4072.0 на UART2, не тот GPS_DRV_OPTIONS).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: GPS[1].Yaw = 0, GPA[1].YAcc = 0, EKF курс медленно дрейфует.

Fix: перемерить офсеты до фазовых центров, проверить кроссовер UART2 и бод (38400 для 1 Гц, 460800 для 5 Гц), GPS_AUTO_CONFIG = 1.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
GPS2_RAW.yaw = 0

Cause: ровер не сконфигурирован как 18/23/25, или ровер и база перепутаны (RELPOSNED отдаёт только ровер).

Fix: GPS2_TYPE = 18, база 17; на PX4 — GPS_UBX_MODE 2 у базы и 1 у ровера.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
Курс по GPS отличается от компаса на 90° или 180°

Cause: перепутаны знаки GPS1_POS_Y/GPS2_POS_Y (на PX4 — неверный GPS_YAW_OFFSET).

Fix: база слева → GPS1_POS_Y отрицательный; повернуть рейку и сверить направление изменения.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
В логе RTCMFD растёт, RTCMFU стоит

Cause: телеметрия теряет фрагменты GPS_RTCM_DATA (низкий бод, перегруженный канал), sequence id не сходится.

Fix: 57600+ на телеметрии, MSM4 вместо MSM7, снизить частоту базы до 1 Гц, или радиомодем в UART2 ровера.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
EKF3 «yaw inconsistent» / XKF4.SM > 1 при EK3_SRC1_YAW = 3

Cause: компас врёт (силовые провода), а GPS-курс верный — конфликт источников.

Fix: compassmot или перенос компаса; на стенде временно EK3_SRC1_YAW = 2, сравнить XKF3.IYAW.

RTK/PPK: base and rover, NTRIP, moving baseline, GPS yaw
PreArm: AHRS: waiting for home при работающем источнике

Cause: не задан origin — без GPS его некому поставить.

Fix: SET_GPS_GLOBAL_ORIGIN с GCS («Set EKF origin here») или из моста сразу после старта потока; AHRS_OPTIONS биты 3 и 4 (с 4.7).

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
PreArm: Need Position Estimate или VisOdom: not healthy

Cause: поза не доходит (не тот порт или sysid, пауза больше 300 мс); ODOMETRY с frame_id не MAV_FRAME_LOCAL_FRD; VISO_TYPE = 0; EK3_SRC1_POSXY не 6.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: сообщений VISP нет или в них Ign = 1.

Fix: LOG_DISARMED = 1, найти VISP; проверить SERIALx_PROTOCOL = 2 (в MAVLink 1 нет covariance и reset_counter) и frame в сообщении.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
Сразу после взлёта аппарат уходит по расходящейся спирали, XKF4.SP 5–7, failsafe молчит

Cause: перепутаны оси или знак (ENU вместо NED, yaw с другим нулём).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: VISP.PX/PY растут в сторону, перпендикулярную или противоположную XKF1.PN/PE; сбросы EKF на источник каждые 7 с.

Consequences: в SITL 650 м за 70 с; в зале — стена через секунду.

Fix: Stabilize и посадка; проверка осей руками без пропов.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
В Loiter аппарат раскачивается, перелетает точки, просаживает высоту; SP при этом в норме

Cause: задержка источника больше VISO_DELAY_MS (или GPS1_DELAY_MS для GPS_INPUT, EK3_FLOW_DELAY для потока).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: XKF3.IPN/IPE растут на манёвре и падают на висении, по форме повторяют скорость.

Fix: измерить полную задержку, записать в VISO_DELAY_MS, перезагрузить; больше 250 мс — чинить конвейер (провод вместо Wi-Fi), а не параметр.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
Рывок на 1–3 м посреди висения, затем затухающие колебания

Cause: источник скачком сменил позу (loop closure, перекалибровка, потеря и новый захват тела) без reset_counter.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: VISP.PX/PY ступенькой, XKF4.SP > 1 на 1–2 с, VISP.Rst не изменился.

Fix: мост увеличивает reset_counter; для GPS_INPUT и /ap/tf — не допускать скачков или перейти на VISION_POSITION_ESTIMATE.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
По потоку Loiter медленно ползёт, по кругу или в одну сторону

Cause: это свойство — позиции нет, интегрируется скорость; сильнее при неверном FLOW_FXSCALER, FLOW_ORIENT_YAW, ошибке дальномера. Срыв на высоте или скорости — дальномер вне диапазона (RFND.Stat не 4) или скорость выше (EK3_FLOW_MAX − 1) · h.

Fix: калибровка масштаба; лидар с запасом по дальности; принять дрейф 1–3 % пути или добавить источник позиции.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
UWB-позиция прыгает на 0.5–2 м у стен, высота гуляет

Cause: отражения, якорь без прямой видимости; якоря в одной плоскости.

Fix: якоря выше голов и на разной высоте; EK3_SRC1_POSZ = 1; площадку внутрь прямоугольника якорей.

Without GNSS: optical flow, UWB, motion capture and external navigation in the EKF
PreArm: waiting for terrain data, TERRAIN_REPORT.pending не уменьшается

Cause: GCS не поддерживает TERRAIN_DATA или без интернета; TERRAIN_SPACING не тот, что у сервера.

Fix: Mission Planner/QGC/MAVProxy (module load terrain) с интернетом или тайлы на SD в APM/TERRAIN; TERRAIN_SPACING = 100.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
PreArm: terrain data expired, possible errors после обновления на 4.7

Cause: DAT-файлы от старого сервера (до 24.02.2026).

Fix: удалить APM/TERRAIN, скачать заново.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
«Failsafe: Terrain data missing» и RTL в AUTO, хотя тайлы загружены

Cause: источник — дальномер (WP_RFND_USE = 1), вышел за RNGFND1_MAX или потерял отражение (вода).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: RFND.Stat 3 (OutOfRangeHigh) или 1 (NoData) за 5 с до ERR Subsys 23 ECode 1 либо в момент «Mission: N WP»; MODE.Rsn = 11.

Fix: WP_RFND_USE = 0 для высоты выше дальности датчика; радар над водой.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
Дизарм через секунды после арма в AUTO с «Failsafe: Terrain data missing»

Cause: на земле дальномер читает меньше RNGFND1_MIN (статус 2, OutOfRangeLow) и считается нездоровым.

Fix: RNGFND1_MIN ниже высоты установки датчика (документация: 5 см).

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
В Loiter над склоном высота над землёй падает, пилот не трогал газ

Cause: дальномер вышел из диапазона, surface tracking отпустил цель в баро-высоту; или SURFTRAK_MODE = 0.

Fix: ниже RNGFND1_MAX с запасом H / cos θ; TERR_BRK_ENABLE = 1.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
На подъёме AGL ниже цели на 10–20 м, аппарат замедляется

Cause: уклон × скорость больше WP_SPD_UP; WP_TER_MARGIN тормозит.

Fix: WP_SPD вниз или WP_SPD_UP вверх; цель выше на крутых участках.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
RTL летит в гребень между аппаратом и домом

Cause: RTL_ALT_TYPE = 0 и RTL_ALT_M ниже гребня над домом; или тип 1, но «RTL: no terrain data, using alt-above-home».

Fix: RTL_ALT_M ≥ превышение гребня + 30 м; rally-точки за гребнем.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety
Над лесом дальномер показывает 20 м, база — 45 м

Cause: это не ошибка: дальномер видит кроны, SRTM — усреднённую поверхность.

Consequences: миссия по базе пройдёт на 20 м над кронами при цели 45.

Fix: цель по базе ≥ 60 м над лесом или дальномер как источник.

Terrain following: terrain data, rangefinder, TERRAIN_*, safety

Autonomy

PreArm: ... and the drone does not arm

Cause: an unfinished calibration or a wrong parameter; the message text names the subsystem.

Fix: read the whole message and remove the cause; do not disable the checks (ARMING_SKIPCHK).

Introduction to ArduPilot
Nothing changed after you changed a parameter

Cause: the parameter requires a reboot.

Fix: reboot the flight controller and read the value back.

Introduction to ArduPilot
LOITER/RTL are unavailable

Cause: no GPS fix, or the EKF is not ready.

Fix: wait for a 3D fix, check the EKF status in the GCS.

Introduction to ArduPilot
Lua scripts do not run on the flight controller

Cause: a board with 1 MB of flash or less (F405, F411), so the firmware has no scripting; or not enough memory.

Fix: check the board's flash size; scripts need more than 1 MB, that is, 2 MB (F427/F429, F7, H7).

Introduction to ArduPilot
"PreArm: Compass not calibrated / inconsistent"

Cause: the calibration was not done, or the internal and external compasses disagree (interference).

Fix: calibrate outdoors, and disable the internal compass when you have an external one on a mast: COMPASS_USEn = 0 for the priority slot it is in (COMPASS_USE2 is the second by priority, not necessarily the internal one; check COMPASS_PRIOn_ID against the compass list in the GCS).

Introduction to ArduPilot
"PreArm: Check fence" / "Need 3D Fix"

Cause: the geofence is enabled and the GPS has no fix.

Fix: wait for a fix (GPS_HDOP_GOOD), or temporarily fly without the geofence, in modes that need no position only.

Introduction to ArduPilot
sim_vehicle.py: command not found

Cause: the profile was not re-read after installation.

Fix: . ~/.profile or a new terminal.

SITL: a drone without a drone
The script hangs on wait_heartbeat

Cause: a wrong address or port, or SITL is not running.

Fix: check that SITL is running and the port number.

SITL: a drone without a drone
arm throttle is refused with PreArm

Cause: the EKF has not converged yet after startup.

Fix: wait 10–20 s after startup, read the PreArm message.

SITL: a drone without a drone
The drone behaves strangely after experiments

Cause: changed parameters are still in effect.

Fix: restart with -w to reset.

SITL: a drone without a drone
Sim_vehicle.py does not start, or "waf configure" errors

Cause: the dependencies are not installed.

Fix: Tools/environment_install/install-prereqs-ubuntu.sh -y, log in again, git submodule update --init --recursive.

SITL: a drone without a drone
You changed a value in a .parm file, but SITL still has the old one

Cause: the previous value is saved in eeprom.bin in the working folder, and it overrides --defaults.

Fix: start with -w or delete eeprom.bin; check that your file is last in --defaults.

SITL: a drone without a drone
The arducopter binary is running, but nothing happens and the log is empty

Cause: SERIAL0 is opened as tcp:0:wait, so the firmware is waiting for a client on 5760.

Fix: connect MAVProxy or your own script to tcp:127.0.0.1:5760.

SITL: a drone without a drone
A scenario with --speedup 10 "waits" 10 times longer than it should, or timeouts fire early

Cause: the intervals are measured on the computer's clock, not on time_boot_ms.

Fix: do all waiting in firmware time, like sim_t() in step 5; measure the real speedup.

SITL: a drone without a drone
RTL does not climb to RTL_ALT_M; the vehicle lands from its current altitude

Cause: the vehicle is close to home, and the RTL_CONE_SLOPE = 3 cone limited the altitude to d · 3.

Fix: this is by design; to test the climb, fly farther than RTL_ALT_M / 3 or set RTL_CONE_SLOPE = 0.

SITL: a drone without a drone
The GCS does not connect to SITL

Cause: the ports are in use, or MAVProxy is not forwarding.

Fix: --out=udp:127.0.0.1:14550 explicitly, check ss -lunp.

SITL: a drone without a drone
Mission Planner does not see the board after flashing a Betaflight board

Cause: a .apj or a .hex without the bootloader was flashed, or the board is not in DFU.

Fix: DFU (BOOT + USB), arducopter_with_bl.hex, STM32CubeProgrammer; from then on only .apj.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
The RC channels do not move although the receiver shows a link

Cause: the wrong SERIALn (hwdef), RC_PROTOCOLS without bit 9, RX/TX not crossed, a UART without DMA on F4/F7.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: RCIN sits at constant values.

Fix: check the board's UART table, try RC_PROTOCOLS = 1, receiver RX to board TX.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
"Bad DShot" / the motors do not respond after MOT_PWM_TYPE = 6

Cause: no reboot, the output is on IOMCU MAIN (DShot only on AUX without BRD_IO_DSHOT), DShot and PWM are mixed in a timer group.

Fix: reboot, motors on DShot-capable outputs, uniform groups.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
At liftoff the copter rolls sharply or spins

Cause: a motor is in the wrong position or spins the wrong way, the props are swapped.

Consequences: a flip within the first 0.5 s, broken props.

Fix: throttle to zero, disarm; repeat Motor Test with the props off and compare with the frame diagram.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
After arming one motor does not spin or stutters

Cause: MOT_SPIN_ARM is below the starting threshold of that motor; with PWM, the ESCs are not calibrated.

Fix: find the threshold in Motor Test, MOT_SPIN_ARM = threshold + 0.02; ESC calibration for PWM.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
The OSD voltage differs from the multimeter by 0.3–0.5 V

Cause: the default BATT_VOLT_MULT is for a different sensor.

Fix: new = old × V_multimeter / V_GCS, verify at two states of charge.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
The flight controller counted 15–20 % fewer mAh than the charger put back

Cause: a wrong BATT_AMP_PERVLT or a zero offset.

Consequences: BATT_LOW_MAH triggers late and the vehicle lands on an empty battery.

Fix: first BATT_AMP_OFFSET from the zero reading, then the scale from the charger mAh.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
In SITL SIM_RC_FAIL = 1 does not trigger the failsafe

Cause: the GCS or a script is sending an RC override, which counts as live RC.

Fix: remove the override (it times out after RC_OVERRIDE_TIME = 3 s) and repeat.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
In AltHold the copter lands by itself or falls

Cause: the throttle is below the deadband (in SITL, a timed-out throttle override), a wrong MOT_THST_HOVER, Z vibration.

Fix: center the throttle before switching, MOT_HOVER_LEARN = 2, VIBE.VibeZ < 30.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
"PreArm: Compass not calibrated" or toilet bowling in Loiter

Cause: calibration indoors, interference from power wires, a wrong orientation of the external compass.

Fix: calibrate outdoors, run CompassMot, disable the internal compass.

ArduPilot from scratch: Copter with Mission Planner / QGC
The goggles of an HD system (HDZero, Walksnail, DJI O3/O4) show video but no OSD.

Cause:

Fix:

OSD and video in ArduPilot: MAX7456, MSP DisplayPort (DJI, Walksnail, HDZero), OSDn screens, VTX
There is analog video but no OSD at all.

Cause:

Fix:

OSD and video in ArduPilot: MAX7456, MSP DisplayPort (DJI, Walksnail, HDZero), OSDn screens, VTX
The analog OSD is visible, but the bottom items are gone.

Cause:

Fix:

OSD and video in ArduPilot: MAX7456, MSP DisplayPort (DJI, Walksnail, HDZero), OSDn screens, VTX
Letters, foreign symbols, or question marks instead of icons.
