LEDs and buzzer in ArduPilot: NTF_*, NeoPixel/ProfiLED, buzzer, DShot beacon
SITL doesn't see Gazebo, the vehicle “hangs” on the ground

Cause: GZ_SIM_SYSTEM_PLUGIN_PATH not set, the plugin isn't built, the frame lacks gazebo-, or the model isn't JSON.

Fix: check the variables, the plugin build, the sim_vehicle.py arguments.

Simulation in Gazebo: worlds, sensors, a camera in ROS 2 and scenario tests
The world can't find models

Cause: GZ_SIM_RESOURCE_PATH not set.

Fix: add the models and worlds paths.

Simulation in Gazebo: worlds, sensors, a camera in ROS 2 and scenario tests
The simulation runs slower than real time

Cause: cameras and lidars on a weak machine, a small physics step, rendering in a window.

Fix: a headless server, fewer sensors and lower resolution, --headless-rendering for cameras.

Simulation in Gazebo: worlds, sensors, a camera in ROS 2 and scenario tests
Ros_gz_bridge is running, but the topic isn't in ROS 2

Cause: wrong topic name or type, mismatched ROS 2 and Gazebo versions.

Fix: gz topic -l and gz topic -i, the exact name and type, the recommended version pair.

Simulation in Gazebo: worlds, sensors, a camera in ROS 2 and scenario tests
A vision algorithm works great in simulation and poorly in the field

Cause: a perfect image with no noise, blur or lighting changes.

Fix: sensor noise in the SDF, varied lighting and textures, testing on recorded real data.

Simulation in Gazebo: worlds, sensors, a camera in ROS 2 and scenario tests
AUTO does not start, the vehicle sits there

Cause: the mission is not loaded, NAV_TAKEOFF is not the first command, or the vehicle is not armed.

Fix: read the mission back from the vehicle, check the first command and the GCS messages.

Missions and planning
The vehicle flies 100 m higher than expected

Cause: absolute altitude instead of relative.

Fix: check the frame of every waypoint in the mission file.

Missions and planning
RTL returns to a place where the pilot is not

Cause: home is the arming location.

Fix: reset home before takeoff after moving.

Missions and planning
The mission upload hangs

Cause: items sent without waiting for the requests, or a wrong seq.

Fix: answer MISSION_REQUEST(_INT) by number, check MISSION_ACK.

Missions and planning
In Auto the vehicle went into the ground or into a climb after a change of site

Cause: absolute altitudes in the mission.

Fix: relative or terrain altitudes; check the elevation profile in the GCS.

Missions and planning
After the last waypoint the vehicle hovers in place in AUTO

Cause: there is no NAV_RETURN_TO_LAUNCH/NAV_LAND at the end of the mission; at the end of the list Copter switches to holding position within AUTO.

Consequences: hovering until the battery failsafe.

Fix: make the last command RTL or LAND; right now, switch to RTL.

Missions and planning
A DO_JUMP loop is flown one time more or fewer than you expected

Cause: param2 is the number of repeats, not passes; N repeats = N + 1 passes; −1 is forever.

Fix: count the passes from Mission: N Jump k/N in the GCS messages.

Missions and planning
DO_CHANGE_SPEED "has no effect" on the leg you wanted

Cause: a DO command runs when the previous NAV command completes, so it affects the leg after the waypoint it follows.

Fix: place it before the NAV waypoint of the leg that needs the new speed; verify from the speed in the log.

Missions and planning
After a landing in the mission the vehicle does not disarm or carries on

Cause: MIS_OPTIONS bit 2 (ContinueAfterLand), LAND_*.

Fix: set it deliberately; test in SITL.

Missions and planning
The vehicle weaves around the line

Cause: short lookahead (L, NAVL1_PERIOD), position noise, poorly tuned roll.

Fix: increase the lookahead, check the inner loops, filter the position.

Guidance and trajectories: pure pursuit, L1, S-curves, minimum snap
Route corners are cut heavily

Cause: a lookahead or rounding radius that is large relative to the segment length.

Fix: a shorter lookahead or radius, longer segments, waypoints at the corners.

Guidance and trajectories: pure pursuit, L1, S-curves, minimum snap
The copter jerks at the start and end of every segment

Cause: acceleration steps — a trajectory without a jerk limit (your own generator, position targets only).

Fix: S-curves, a jerk limit, velocity and acceleration feedforward.

Guidance and trajectories: pure pursuit, L1, S-curves, minimum snap
A vehicle controlled by the companion lags in turns

Cause: only position targets are sent.

Fix: send velocity and acceleration in SET_POSITION_TARGET_LOCAL_NED.

Guidance and trajectories: pure pursuit, L1, S-curves, minimum snap
Strong wind pushes the plane off the line

Cause: the wind is close to the airspeed, and guidance can't hold the line.

Fix: a higher cruise speed, a route that accounts for the wind; in PX4 — look at the NPFG behavior.

Guidance and trajectories: pure pursuit, L1, S-curves, minimum snap
The log ends in the air

Cause: loss of board power.

Fix: POWR.Vcc before the end of the log, the soldering of the power connectors, the BEC.

ArduPilot logs: flight analysis
VIBE is high and Clip is increasing

Cause: unbalanced props or motors, a hard-mounted FC.

Fix: balancing, dampers, a frame check, before any filters.

ArduPilot logs: flight analysis
DesRoll and Roll diverge, one motor is at maximum in RCOU

Cause: a failed or weak motor or ESC, overloading.

Fix: check the motor, the propeller, the ESC; compare the motor outputs in hover.

ArduPilot logs: flight analysis
ERR 16/17 before a crash

Cause: bad EKF variances from the compass, GPS, or vibration.

Fix: XKF4 at that moment, then find the cause as in the previous module.

ArduPilot logs: flight analysis
A script does not start and the GCS is silent

Cause: SCR_ENABLE is not set or there was no reboot, or the file is in the wrong folder.

Fix: check the parameter and the scripts/ path relative to the folder SITL was started from.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
A pre-arm error about script memory

Cause: SCR_HEAP_SIZE is too small for the loaded scripts.

Fix: raise SCR_HEAP_SIZE or remove scripts you do not need.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
All scripts stopped working in the middle of a flight

Cause: the Lua engine died (Lua: Engine Error …, then Scripting: stopped) or scripting was restarted; an error in one script removes only that script.

Fix: read the GCS messages, fix the script, restart scripting (a reboot, MAV_CMD_SCRIPTING, RCx_OPTION = 316, SCR_ENABLE 0 → 1).

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
find_channel_for_option returns nil

Cause: no channel has the required RCx_OPTION assigned.

Fix: assign the option to a channel with a switch.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
Arming is rejected with PreArm: Scripting: ./scripts/<name>.lua:<line>: …

Cause: a script failed with a runtime error and was removed; the firmware will not let you fly without it.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the Lua: … message repeats in the GCS every 10 s (DEBUG severity, up to 10 times).

Fix: fix the line from the message, then reboot or send MAV_CMD_SCRIPTING with param1 = 3; leave bit 4 of SCR_DEBUG_OPTS alone if the script is a safeguard.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
add_table failed when a script starts

Cause: the table key or the prefix is already taken by another script, or the prefix is longer than allowed.

Fix: give the script its own param:add_table key and a unique prefix; check param show <prefix>*.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
A script is "slow": return update, 100, but it reacts noticeably less often

Cause: the delay is a minimum, not a period; many scripts or a heavy call stretch the queue.

Fix: measure the real period with millis() (≈ 113 ms for 100 ms in the run); do not do time-critical work in Lua.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
A script does not start, "Lua: … out of memory"

Cause: SCR_HEAP_SIZE is too small, or the board has no spare RAM.

Fix: increase the heap (200–300 kB on an H7, a course guideline), check whether expansion is disabled with bit 6 of SCR_DEBUG_OPTS, simplify the script.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
"<name>.lua exceeded time limit"

Cause: a heavy loop in a single update call.

Fix: split the work across calls; raise SCR_VM_I_COUNT with care.

Lua: scenarios and fault injection
The vehicle loses altitude in ALT_HOLD for no apparent reason

Cause: vibration; the EKF stops trusting the accelerometer.

Fix: look at VIBE and clipping in the log, balance the props and motors.

Heavy-lift platforms: X8 and octo
A constant yaw rotation in hover

Cause: a tilted motor or different props in the coaxial pairs.

Fix: check motor alignment and that the props are the same model and pitch.

Heavy-lift platforms: X8 and octo
Hover throttle (MOT_THST_HOVER, CTUN.ThO) above 0.5

Cause: overloaded once the coaxial loss is counted: this is a fraction of thrust, and 0.5 is T/W 2 (f-physics, "Hover throttle"; the same limit is used in des-sizing).

Fix: recalculate T/W with the coaxial correction, reduce weight.

Heavy-lift platforms: X8 and octo
After a motor failure the vehicle spins and loses control

Cause: not enough thrust margin or the wrong motor order.

Fix: repeat the test in SITL, check Motor Test and the thrust margin.

Heavy-lift platforms: X8 and octo
The plane drops its nose and sinks in a turn

Cause: a stall; the airspeed in the turn is below V_s/√cos φ.

Fix: a higher AIRSPEED_MIN, limit the roll (ROLL_LIMIT_DEG), a higher cruise speed.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
Twitchy in pitch, does not hold trim

Cause: an aft CG, a small margin.

Fix: move the CG forward by 2–5% MAC, do a dive test.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
It loses altitude while gaining airspeed in AUTO

Cause: TECS_SPDWEIGHT close to 2 with weak throttle, or TECS_CLMB_MAX set too high.

Fix: a real TECS_CLMB_MAX from a full-throttle test, SPDWEIGHT 1.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
The airspeed reads nonsense on the ground

Cause: the tube is not calibrated, is covered, or has condensation.

Fix: ARSPD_AUTOCAL, warm it up, blow it out; the airspeed pre-arm check.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
In FBWA or AUTO the wing will not bank more than 25–35° at a normal airspeed

Cause: AIRSPEED_STALL = 0, so STALL_PREVENTION treats AIRSPEED_MIN as the stall speed: below ≈ 1.19·AIRSPEED_MIN the roll is limited to less than 45° (Attitude.cpp:719–746).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: CTUN.NavRoll sits at a single value below ROLL_LIMIT_DEG, and the airspeed is close to AIRSPEED_MIN.

Fix: set AIRSPEED_STALL = the measured stall speed (slightly above), check the sensor calibration; there is no need to disable STALL_PREVENTION.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
Without an airspeed sensor the wing alternately slows to a stall and speeds up in turns when it is windy

Cause: without airspeed TECS holds altitude only, and throttle follows pitch from TRIM_THROTTLE (AP_TECS.cpp:930–958); TRIM_THROTTLE is below the real cruise throttle.

Fix: set TRIM_THROTTLE from a log of level flight, with a higher cruise speed as a margin for wind; for AUTO and VTOL, fit a sensor (ARSPD_USE = 1).

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
The sensor is connected and ARSP is in the log, but TECS behaves as if it were not there

Cause: ARSPD_USE = 0, so the airspeed is only logged.

Fix: ARSPD_USE = 1 after checking the readings on the ground and calibrating.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
The autopilot does not apply throttle on a hand launch

Cause: TKOFF_THR_MINACC is not reached, or the delay.

Fix: a harder throw, a lower threshold, check arming and the mode.

Wing aerodynamics and plane flight modes
In MANUAL the surfaces respond to the sticks correctly, in FBWA the plane rolls over or pitches up

Cause: the direction was fixed through RCn_REVERSED or reversing in the transmitter instead of SERVOn_REVERSED; the left and right elevons are swapped; a wrong AHRS_ORIENTATION.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: ATT.Roll moves away from ATT.DesRoll while RCOU of the output grows in the same direction.

Consequences: loss of the plane in the first seconds of FBWA, RTL or AUTOTUNE.

Fix: MANUAL at once and land; on the bench, check 2 against the table, then check 1.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The elevons lack roll travel and the plane turns sluggishly

Cause: MIXING_GAIN = 0.5, so one demand gives half of the travel.

Fix: raise it to 0.7–1.0 or set MIXING_OFFSET to 20–50; check that the servo does not bind at full roll with full pitch.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The failsafe triggers with the throttle stick down

Cause: the idle pulse width is not above THR_FS_VALUE.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: "Throttle failsafe on" with RCIN.C3 around 940–950.

Fix: idle 40–50 µs above the threshold, recalibrate the radio.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The transmitter is off, yet the plane keeps flying with the sticks at neutral and does not return

Cause: THR_FAILSAFE = 0 or 2; on loss the receiver holds the last values with the throttle above THR_FS_VALUE; FS_SHORT_ACTN = 3 together with FS_LONG_ACTN = 2.

Fix: THR_FAILSAFE = 1, and "no pulses" or throttle below the threshold in the receiver; check on the ground with the propeller off.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The mission is aborted and the plane returns although FS_LONG_ACTN = 0 is set

Cause: FS_SHORT_ACTN = 1 put AUTO into CIRCLE, and the long event from CIRCLE gives RTL.

Fix: FS_SHORT_ACTN = 0 if the mission has to continue without a link.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The OSD shows airspeed, but TECS holds airspeed poorly and the throttle moves only with pitch

Cause: ARSPD_USE = 0.

Fix: ARSPD_USE = 1 after checking the zero and calibrating ARSPD_RATIO.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
"PreArm: Airspeed: 1 need pre-flight calibration" or an airspeed of 3–5 m/s on the ground

Cause: the zero was taken in wind with the tube uncovered, or the sensor had not warmed up.

Fix: cover the tube, repeat the zero calibration (the preflight calibration command in the ground station), blow into it to check.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
"Airspeed sensor 1 failure. Disabling" in flight

Cause: a difference from GPS speed greater than ARSPD_WIND_MAX or a failed EKF3 check: the tube came off or took in water, or ARSPD_RATIO is wrong.

Fix: you can continue, since the firmware has switched to the estimate from GPS and wind; after landing check the tubing and the scale.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The motor started right after arming in TAKEOFF

Cause: TKOFF_THR_MINACC = 0 and TKOFF_THR_MINSPD = 0.

Consequences: a hand injured by the prop.

Fix: 15 and 4 for a throw; arm only just before the throw.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
The motor stays silent after the throw

Cause: no 3D fix; the throw was weaker than TKOFF_THR_MINACC; the speed did not reach TKOFF_THR_MINSPD within 0.1 s after the pause; after a rudder arm the rudder was not returned to center ("Takeoff waiting for rudder release"); the plane was pitched up more than 45°.

Fix: wait for GPS, throw more level and harder, lower TKOFF_THR_MINACC to 10–12.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
In the RTL turn the plane loses altitude and drops a wing

Cause: AIRSPEED_MIN is below 1.2 × V_s, STALL_PREVENTION = 0, or with no sensor TRIM_THROTTLE is too low.

Fix: recalculate V_s for the real mass, raise AIRSPEED_MIN and TRIM_THROTTLE, reduce ROLL_LIMIT_DEG.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
RTL loiters over home until the battery is flat

Cause: that is by design: RTL does not land.

Fix: a landing sequence with DO_LAND_START and RTL_AUTOLAND, or FS_LONG_ACTN = 5 and BATT_FS_CRT_ACT = 7 (AUTOLAND), or land manually.

ArduPlane from scratch: servos, control surfaces, airspeed, wing failsafe
After Q_ENABLE = 1 the station shows no Q_A_* parameters at all

Cause: the parameter list was not refreshed, or there was no reboot.

Fix: reboot the flight controller, Refresh Params; then check the number of Q_* parameters (up to 231 in 4.7.2 with the subgroups enabled).

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
After enabling QuadPlane a servo on outputs 5–8 hums, or a motor on that output spins with no command

Cause: the output function was 0, and the firmware assigned Motor1…Motor4 there.

Consequences: a motor on a servo channel is an injury on the bench.

Fix: remove power, build the output map, set the functions of all outputs explicitly before Q_ENABLE (ap-quadplane).

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
Arming is refused with "Q_ASSIST_SPEED is not set"

Cause: the default value of 0.

Fix: 3 m/s below the minimum airspeed you fly at (5 m/s below without a sensor), or −1.

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
Arming is refused with "tiltrotor setup not complete, reboot"

Cause: Q_TILT_ENABLE = 1 without a reboot.

Fix: reboot; if both tilt and tailsitter are enabled, keep one.

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
A roll oscillation at cruise does not change however much you adjust Q_A_RAT_RLL_*

Cause: you are adjusting the copter half, and at cruise the lift motors are off.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: there are no QTUN records at the moment of the oscillation.

Fix: restore Q_A_RAT_* and tune RLL_RATE_* with AUTOTUNE (ap-plane-tuning).

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
A loaded "copter" parameter file was only partly applied

Cause: the names ATC_*, PSC_*, MOT_* do not exist in ArduPlane.

Fix: rename them to Q_A_*, Q_P_*, Q_M_* and treat the values as starting points only.

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
In QSTABILIZE you cannot roll the aircraft more than 45° although Q_A_ANGLE_MAX is larger

Cause: in the Q modes the stick is also limited by the fixed-wing limits ROLL_LIMIT_DEG and PTCH_LIM_*_DEG.

Fix: bit 14 of Q_OPTIONS uses Q_A_ANGLE_MAX exclusively, if you really need that.

QuadPlane inside: where the plane is and where the copter is
A motor spins up with no command during a check

Cause: the motor function is assigned to the wrong output, or a servo signal is going to an ESC.

Fix: cut the power immediately, rebuild the output map with the props off.

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
A servo hums and drives against its stop at power-up

Cause: the output is running a motor protocol (DShot or a high PWM rate).

Fix: check the timer groups and the output rates.

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
On switching to MANUAL the motors tilt back instead of forward

Cause: the tilt servo reversal is not set.

Fix: SERVOn_REVERSED, then check in both modes.

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
The aircraft holds yaw poorly in hover

Cause: Q_TILT_YAW_ANGLE is too small, or the left and right tilt servos are swapped.

Fix: check functions 75/76 and the direction of deflection on a yaw command.

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
A sag and a nose drop after the forward transition

Cause: AIRSPEED_MIN is below the real V_s, Q_TRANSITION_MS is too short.

Fix: AIRSPEED_MIN ≥ 1.2–1.3 V_s from the calculation, Q_ASSIST_SPEED not above AIRSPEED_MIN, transition higher.

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
The VTOL motors kick in during level flight

Cause: Q_ASSIST_SPEED is above the airspeed the mode holds (FBWB/CRUISE at low throttle hold AIRSPEED_MIN), or the airspeed reading is wrong.

Fix: calibrate the airspeed sensor; set Q_ASSIST_SPEED 3 m/s below AIRSPEED_MIN (5 m/s below without a sensor), as the parameter description advises, but not below V_s (ap-vtol-modes).

QuadPlane and tilt-rotor VTOL
After switching to FBWA the VTOL motors do not shut down and "Transition airspeed reached" does not appear

Cause: the airspeed does not reach max(AIRSPEED_MIN, Q_ASSIST_SPEED); the airspeed sensor is stuck, or there is neither a sensor nor GPS (no airspeed estimate).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: QTUN.Trn = 0 all the time; ARSP.Airspeed does not grow while GPS.Spd does.

Fix: QHOVER; check the pitot tube and ARSPD_USE; set Q_TRANS_FAIL and ARSPD_WIND_MAX.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
A roll or an "uncontrolled" turn at the very start of a transition (4+1)

Cause: both controllers work during AIRSPEED_WAIT, so a reversed aileron or elevator shows up only now; forward-motor torque; climbing on the VTOL motors.

Fix: check the surface directions in FBWA on the bench (ap-setup-plane); bit 0 of Q_OPTIONS (Level Transition); transition with throttle > 50 % to accelerate.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
A sink and a nose drop right after "Transition done"

Cause: AIRSPEED_MIN is below 1.2 V_s, or the sensor reads high (the pitot tube is in the wash of the VTOL props), or Q_TRANSITION_MS is too short.

Fix: recalculate V_s (aero-wing), move the tube, compare ARSP.Airspeed with GPS.Spd on opposite headings; increase Q_TRANSITION_MS to 5000–8000.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
In cruise, "Transition started airspeed …" every few seconds

Cause: Q_ASSIST_SPEED is above the airspeed the mode holds (CRUISE/FBWB at low throttle hold AIRSPEED_MIN).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: QTUN.Ast with bit 2 (airspeed).

Fix: Q_ASSIST_SPEED = AIRSPEED_MIN − 3 m/s, not below V_s.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
"Angle assist r=… p=…" in an ordinary turn

Cause: ROLL_LIMIT_DEG is smaller than the bank actually used (for example in AUTOTUNE), and the attitude error exceeds Q_ASSIST_ANGLE.

Fix: make ROLL_LIMIT_DEG consistent with your turns; if needed, raise Q_ASSIST_ANGLE or Q_ASSIST_DELAY.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
On switching to QHOVER in cruise the vehicle pitches up sharply and gains 15–20 m

Cause: normal braking on the wing: pitch up to Q_A_ANGLE_MAX over Q_BACKTRANS_MS.

Consequences: busting the airspace ceiling or entering cloud.

Fix: transition at a lower speed and with altitude to spare; for a smoother entry, increase Q_BACKTRANS_MS.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
A VTOL landing in a mission overshoots the point ("VTOL Overshoot")

Cause: the real deceleration is weaker than Q_TRANS_DECEL, a tailwind, or an approach loiter radius smaller than the stopping distance.

Fix: reduce Q_TRANS_DECEL (for example 2 → 1.5); Q_FW_LND_APR_RAD ≥ V²/(2·Q_TRANS_DECEL) (course rule); approach into the wind.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
Arming is refused with "VTOL land too short, min X m"

Cause: the landing point is too close to the preceding NAV_WAYPOINT.

Fix: move the preceding waypoint farther than 0.75·V²/(2·Q_TRANS_DECEL), or set TECS_LAND_ARSPD below cruise.

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
RTL loiters over home and does not land

Cause: Q_RTL_MODE = 0 and no RTL_AUTOLAND with a landing sequence.

Fix: Q_RTL_MODE = 1 (or 2 for an approach into the wind).

VTOL modes and transitions in ArduPilot
AutoTune aborts with "AutoTune: Failed to level, please tune manually" or does not start

Cause: wind, high vibration, a poor initial tune.

Fix: calm air, a notch, a manual tune, AltHold stable for ≥ 1 min.

ArduPilot tuning: AUTOTUNE, filters, ESC-based notch
Hot motors after AutoTune

Cause: high D with a noisy gyro.

Fix: notch and filters, AUTOTUNE_MIN_D, then repeat.

ArduPilot tuning: AUTOTUNE, filters, ESC-based notch
The throttle-based notch misses with a payload

Cause: INS_HNTCH_REF was set for a different weight.

Fix: recalculate REF or use ESC telemetry mode.

ArduPilot tuning: AUTOTUNE, filters, ESC-based notch
A peak is still in the spectrum after the notch

Cause: a frame resonance (not a motor) or a harmonic outside HMNCS.

Fix: a fixed INS_HNTC2_* notch on the resonance; HMNCS = 7.

ArduPilot tuning: AUTOTUNE, filters, ESC-based notch
A mushy response with a good tune

Cause: input shaping (ATC_INPUT_TC is high, ATC_ACC_*_MAX is low).

Fix: reduce TC, raise ACC and RATE_MAX.

A switch does nothing, and there is no AUXF record.

Payloads in ArduPilot: camera, gimbal, relays, drop and winch — wiring and parameters
In FBWA the plane rolls over immediately

Cause: a reversed surface in stabilization (wrong servo direction or SERVOn_REVERSED).

Fix: the "tilt it by hand, the surfaces oppose" check before every first flight after a change.

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
AUTOTUNE never finishes

Cause: the reversals are not sharp enough, the airspeed is below AIRSPEED_MIN, RLL2SRV_RMAX is too low.

Fix: sharper and to full deflection, more airspeed, check the rate limits.

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
Altitude oscillates by ±10–20 m in LOITER

Cause: an undertuned pitch loop, TECS_TIME_CONST too low, wrong TECS_CLMB_MAX/SINK_*.

Fix: pitch first, then TECS_TIME_CONST +1, remeasure the limits.

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
It loses airspeed in a climb

Cause: TECS_CLMB_MAX is higher than the actual climb rate, throttle at THR_MAX.

Fix: lower TECS_CLMB_MAX to the measured value, check at the end of the battery.

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
It weaves after every turn

Cause: NAVL1_PERIOD is too low or roll is poorly tuned.

Fix: NAVL1_PERIOD +1…2, check roll (PIDR).

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
The landing digs in or overshoots

Cause: barometer altitude error, a late flare, a high approach airspeed.

Fix: a rangefinder for landing, LAND_FLARE_ALT/SEC, TECS_LAND_ARSPD, an approach into the wind.

ArduPlane tuning: wing AUTOTUNE, TECS, L1 navigation, landing
Nothing happens when you switch off the transmitter

Cause: the receiver holds the last values (no "no pulses" or failsafe setting in the receiver), FS_THR_ENABLE = 0.

Fix: set the receiver to "no pulses" or to a failsafe throttle value below FS_THR_VALUE; enable the failsafe.

Fault tolerance: failsafe, geofence, flight FMEA
RTL flew into a tree

Cause: RTL_ALT_M is below the obstacles.

Fix: set RTL_ALT_M from the tallest obstacle plus a margin; make the altitude fence consistent with it.

Fault tolerance: failsafe, geofence, flight FMEA
The low battery failsafe triggered right after takeoff

Cause: the threshold ignores sag; BATT_LOW_TIMER is too short.

Fix: a threshold based on voltage under load, a 10 s timer, or a mAh threshold.

Fault tolerance: failsafe, geofence, flight FMEA
In Auto, on RC loss, the vehicle went to RTL in the middle of the mission

Cause: FS_OPTIONS bit 0 is not set, which is expected and correct without an authorization.

Fix: leave it; with an authorized BVLOS operation, enable it deliberately.

Fault tolerance: failsafe, geofence, flight FMEA
It will not arm, with PreArm: Fence requires position or PreArm: Fence enabled, need position estimate

Cause: the circle or polygon fence is enabled (FENCE_TYPE bit 1 or 2) and the GPS has no fix.

Fix: wait for a fix, or fly with the fence only when you have GPS.

Fault tolerance: failsafe, geofence, flight FMEA

Engineer: code on board

The aircraft “freezes” or crashes when the companion hangs

Cause: the companion does what the FC should do (stabilization, direct motor commands).

Fix: move the critical parts to the FC, leave the companion only advice via GUIDED.

Companion: why, and where the boundary is
The FC doesn't see the companion

Cause: wrong port, protocol or baud rate; TX/RX swapped.

Fix: SERIALx_PROTOCOL, SERIALx_BAUD, check the heartbeat via MAVProxy.

Companion: why, and where the boundary is
The companion reboots under throttle

Cause: power from a weak BEC or a voltage sag.

Fix: a separate BEC/regulator with current headroom.

Companion: why, and where the boundary is
wait_heartbeat() doesn't return

Cause: wrong address/port, the wrong dialect, UART baud rate.

Fix: check the port, the baud (57600 or 115200 on telemetry), that SITL is running.

MAVLink and MAVSDK
The command “succeeded”, but the vehicle did nothing

Cause: COMMAND_ACK wasn't checked, the command was rejected.

Fix: read the ACK and STATUSTEXT.

MAVLink and MAVSDK
Telemetry arrives rarely

Cause: default stream intervals.

Fix: MAV_CMD_SET_MESSAGE_INTERVAL for the messages you need.

MAVLink and MAVSDK
CRC errors, “unknown” messages

Cause: different versions of the definitions on the two sides.

Fix: update pymavlink/MAVSDK, use the same dialect (ardupilotmega).

MAVLink and MAVSDK
Heartbeat comes through, but one particular message doesn't arrive

Cause: different message definitions → CRC_EXTRA mismatch, frame dropped.

Fix: compare CRC_EXTRA in both libraries; rebuild all sides from the same XML.

MAVLink internals: frame, CRC, dialects, signing
Your own parser loses some COMMAND_LONG/SET_POSITION_TARGET

Cause: a len == sizeof(struct) check, while MAVLink 2 truncated the zeros.

Fix: pad the payload with zeros to the full size.

MAVLink internals: frame, CRC, dialects, signing
After enabling signing, QGC doesn't connect

Cause: the GCS doesn't have the key, and the channel doesn't accept unsigned packets.

Fix: enter the same key in the GCS; temporarily allow unsigned packets over USB.

MAVLink internals: frame, CRC, dialects, signing
The loss counter shows 50 % with a good radio link

Cause: two senders with the same sysid/compid on one channel.

Fix: a unique compid for each script/component.

MAVLink internals: frame, CRC, dialects, signing
The mission is “uploaded,” but the vehicle has one or two fewer items

Cause: the client sent items back to back without waiting for requests; some were lost, the ACK wasn't checked.

Fix: reply only to MISSION_REQUEST_INT; require MISSION_ACK ACCEPTED; download and compare.

MAVLink microservices: commands, parameters, missions, streams
PARAM_SET “went through,” but the value didn't change

Cause: the PARAM_VALUE echo wasn't checked; the value is out of range or the type encoding is wrong.

Fix: compare the echo with the value; account for C-cast vs bytewise.

MAVLink microservices: commands, parameters, missions, streams
A command is sometimes executed twice

Cause: a retry without incrementing confirmation on a non-idempotent command.

Fix: confirmation++ on retry; check the state before retrying.

MAVLink microservices: commands, parameters, missions, streams
Telemetry over the radio “stutters” after the script connects

Cause: the script requested high rates via SET_MESSAGE_INTERVAL on the radio link.

Fix: request streams only on your own link; calculate the budget.

MAVLink microservices: commands, parameters, missions, streams
A MAVSDK script hangs at startup

Cause: waiting for health without a timeout on a vehicle without GPS/home; the wrong connection address (udpin vs udpout, syntax version).

Fix: asyncio.wait_for; check the address and the MAVSDK version.

Libraries on top of MAVLink: pymavlink, MAVSDK, MAVROS: what to use and when
PX4 exits Offboard immediately

Cause: setpoints weren't sent before offboard.start(), or they're sent less often than 2 Hz.

Fix: send an initial setpoint before starting; a separate task at 10–20 Hz.

Libraries on top of MAVLink: pymavlink, MAVSDK, MAVROS: what to use and when
upload_mission sometimes fails with a timeout over the radio

Cause: losses on a slow link, too few retries by default.

Fix: upload over a fast link/through the companion; look at the tlog to see which item was lost.

Libraries on top of MAVLink: pymavlink, MAVSDK, MAVROS: what to use and when
Two scripts interfere with each other

Cause: both occupy the same port or the same compid.

Fix: a router with separate endpoints, different compids.

Libraries on top of MAVLink: pymavlink, MAVSDK, MAVROS: what to use and when
The companion loses the link when the radio screen changes

Cause: sending only in refresh().

Fix: a shared tick() in refresh() and background().

ESP32 and peripherals
Lines arrive truncated

Cause: serialRead is called less often than data arrives, and the buffer overflows.

Fix: read in every tick(), shorten the messages, lower the rate.

ESP32 and peripherals
The access point came up in flight

Cause: protection on only one side.

Fix: check arm both in Lua and in the firmware; heartbeat timeout → FLIGHT.

ESP32 and peripherals
The ESP32 reboots when Wi-Fi turns on

Cause: a supply voltage sag (peaks of 300–500 mA).

Fix: a 470 µF capacitor on 3.3 V, a BEC/LDO with headroom, don't power it from a radio pin without checking the current.

ESP32 and peripherals
The Lua on the radio doesn't see the ESP32's replies

Cause: the AUX port isn't in Lua mode, baud rate, TX/RX.

Fix: Hardware → AUX = Lua, setSerialBaudrate, an analyzer on the line.

ESP32 and peripherals
The period jitter jumps up to ms

Cause: a higher-priority task/ISR does long work; busy-wait; printf in a critical task.

Fix: priorities, short ISRs, output in a separate task via a queue.

Embedded: interrupts, timers, DMA, RTOS
The ESP32 reboots with “Task watchdog got triggered”

Cause: a task doesn't yield the core (no vTaskDelay/blocking wait).

Fix: wait for events instead of polling; vTaskDelay(1) in background loops.

Embedded: interrupts, timers, DMA, RTOS
The data in the DMA buffer is “stale” on F7/H7

Cause: the data cache.

Fix: non-cacheable memory or invalidate after reception.

Embedded: interrupts, timers, DMA, RTOS
An ISR causes a crash

Cause: a non-ISR-safe call (printf, malloc, a plain xQueueSend) or code not in IRAM on the ESP32.

Fix: the FromISR versions, IRAM_ATTR, minimal work.

Embedded: interrupts, timers, DMA, RTOS
Betaflight: CPU load > 90%, skipped tasks

Cause: an F4 at 8 kHz with all features on.

Fix: 4 kHz, disable what you don't need, check tasks in the CLI.

Embedded: interrupts, timers, DMA, RTOS
UART receives garbage

Cause: baud rate/inversion/no common ground; overrun without DMA.

Fix: check with an analyzer, DMA reception, framing/overrun counters.

UART, I2C, SPI, CAN at the signal and driver level
An I2C transaction “hangs”

Cause: the slave holds SDA; clock stretching not supported.

Fix: timeouts in the driver, bus recovery, drop to 100 kHz.

UART, I2C, SPI, CAN at the signal and driver level
SPI returns 0xFF/0x00

Cause: CS does not go low, CPOL/CPHA mode, too fast.

Fix: scope on CS/SCLK, mode per the datasheet, WHO_AM_I test.

UART, I2C, SPI, CAN at the signal and driver level
A CAN node goes bus-off

Cause: no terminators, mismatched baud rates, H/L swapped.

Fix: 120 Ω at both ends, one baud rate, error counters.

UART, I2C, SPI, CAN at the signal and driver level
The sensor works on the bench but not on the aircraft

Cause: interference on I2C from power wiring, wire length.

Fix: shorter/twisted/shielded wires, SPI or CAN instead of I2C.

UART, I2C, SPI, CAN at the signal and driver level
MAVProxy doesn't see a heartbeat

Cause: the Linux console holds the port, the wrong baud rate, TX/RX swapped.

Fix: raspi-config, compare with SERIAL2_BAUD, check the wires.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
Garbage in the data at 921600

Cause: long wires, interference, a bad GND.

Fix: shorten, shield or drop to 57600.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
A second script can't open the port

Cause: /dev/serial0 is taken by the first one.

Fix: a MAVLink router, clients over UDP/TCP.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
The Pi reboots under throttle

Cause: weak power.

Fix: a BEC with headroom, a capacitor, a separate line from the power bus.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
Garbage or no data on /dev/serial0

Cause: the console on the UART, the mini-UART with a floating baud rate, Bluetooth on the same port.

Fix: raspi-config (console off, UART on), disable-bt, 921600 only on the full UART.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
The service restarts every few seconds with no errors in the log

Cause: WatchdogSec is set, but the script doesn't send WATCHDOG=1 (or the unit isn't Type=notify).

Fix: sd_notify in the main loop, or remove WatchdogSec.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
The algorithm is slower onboard than on the bench

Cause: throttling due to temperature or undervoltage.

Fix: vcgencmd get_throttled, a heatsink and airflow, a BEC with headroom.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
After a hard power cut the Pi doesn't boot

Cause: the SD card file system is corrupted.

Fix: Overlay File System or logs on a separate medium, Storage=volatile for the journal.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
The script works over SSH and “dies” after disconnecting

Cause: it isn't a service.

Fix: a systemd unit with Restart=always.

Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot
Nano выключается или перезагружается при старте инференса, в простое работает

Cause: питание через micro-USB или длинный тонкий провод; на входе меньше 4.75 В под током 1.5–3 А.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: NVAL CC_PWR_W обрывается на пике, heartbeat компонента 191 пропадает.

Fix: гнездо или пины 2/4 с перемычкой J48 по назначению, пара 20 AWG до 30 см, BEC 5.0–5.25 В на 5 А; измерить напряжение на гребёнке под нагрузкой.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
Гнездо питания подключено, плата не стартует

Cause: нет перемычки J48 — вход переключён на micro-USB.

Fix: поставить перемычку; проверить полярность штекера (плюс в центре).

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
К/с детектора падает через 5–10 минут, ошибок нет

Cause: программный троттлинг около 97 °C (Nano) или 99 °C (Orin Nano), закрытый корпус, вентилятор не включён.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: CC_GPU_MHZ падает при CC_GPU около 99 %, CC_TEMP у порога.

Fix: радиатор с обдувом, jetson_clocks --fan на стенде, ниже режим nvpmodel, меньше модель или разрешение.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
Killed при сборке engine или при запуске второго процесса с моделью

Cause: кончились 4 ГБ общей памяти, рабочий стол занимает сотни мегабайт.

Fix: headless-режим, swap на время сборки engine, одна модель, размер n/s.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
На /dev/ttyTHS1 мусор или «Permission denied»

Cause: консоль nvgetty держит порт; пользователь не в dialout; скорость не совпадает с SERIAL2_BAUD.

Fix: выключить nvgetty, добавить группу, сверить скорость; проверить петлёй 8–10.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
Heartbeat полётника есть, а NVAL в логе нет

Cause: лог не пишется без арминга (LOG_DISARMED = 0); мост шлёт с чужим sysid; на порту не MAVLink 2.

Fix: LOG_DISARMED = 1 на время стенда, source_system равен sysid аппарата, SERIAL2_PROTOCOL = 2.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
nvv4l2h264enc не находится или не открывает устройство на Orin Nano

Cause: у Orin Nano нет NVENC.

Fix: x264enc tune=zerolatency speed-preset=ultrafast и учесть около двух ядер CPU в бюджете.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
pip install ultralytics на Nano не ставится или тянет несовместимый PyTorch

Cause: Ubuntu 18.04 с Python 3.6 и CUDA 10.2 — колёс под них нет.

Fix: контейнер ultralytics/ultralytics:latest-jetson-jetpack4; engine собирать на самой плате.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
Engine, собранный на другом Jetson или ПК, не загружается

Cause: engine привязан к GPU и версии TensorRT.

Fix: переносить ONNX, собирать engine на целевой плате.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
Сервис перезапускается каждые 10 с

Cause: tegrastats не запущен или не найден — нет строк, нет WATCHDOG=1.

Consequences: дыры в NVAL каждые 10 с — по ним это видно и после полёта.

Fix: journalctl -u cc-health, запустить скрипт руками, проверить путь к tegrastats.

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
После жёсткого отключения плата не грузится

Cause: повреждена файловая система microSD.

Fix: выключать командой до снятия батареи, логи — на отдельный раздел или накопитель, журнал systemd в RAM (модуль «Raspberry Pi + ArduPilot»).

Jetson Nano as a companion: what it can do, what it can't, and how to get the most out of it
A script on the companion gets no replies to its commands

Cause: it doesn't send a heartbeat — the autopilot doesn't know the route to its compid; or the compid collides with another one.

Fix: a 1 Hz heartbeat from the script, a unique compid.

MAVLink routing and transport: radio, 4G, multiple GCSs
Two GCSs “fight”: modes jump, parameters get overwritten

Cause: both have sysid 255; or both send RC override.

Fix: different sysids; control rights for only one; in ArduPilot MAV_GCS_SYSID/SYSID_MYGCS for failsafe.

MAVLink routing and transport: radio, 4G, multiple GCSs
Over 4G telemetry arrives with a delay of seconds and then “catches up”

Cause: TCP over a lossy link.

Fix: UDP; if needed, TCP only for MAVFTP/parameters.

MAVLink routing and transport: radio, 4G, multiple GCSs
A port with a peripheral floods the radio with traffic

Cause: all packets from that port are forwarded to the others.

Fix: disable forwarding for the port (MAVn_OPTIONS bit 1 / SERIALn_OPTIONS bit 10), a filter in the router.

MAVLink routing and transport: radio, 4G, multiple GCSs
After enabling signing the GCS “sees” the aircraft, but commands don't go through

Cause: the key on the GCS does not match the autopilot's key.

Fix: select the same key in the GCS; if it is lost — change or erase the key over USB.

Remote ID and vehicle security: broadcast, MAVLink signing, firmware protection
Signing is enabled, but someone else's GCS still controls the aircraft

Cause: the radio port runs MAVLink 1, the command came over USB, or through a node that signs for everyone (a router with the key).

Fix: SERIALn_PROTOCOL = 2 on the radio ports, check the routing chain, the key only for those who control the aircraft.

Remote ID and vehicle security: broadcast, MAVLink signing, firmware protection
The aircraft won't arm with a Remote ID error

Cause: the module is not connected or has not received the pilot's position, no link with the GCS, no operator ID set.

Fix: check the link with the module, the OPEN_DRONE_ID_SYSTEM data from the GCS, the module's parameters.

Remote ID and vehicle security: broadcast, MAVLink signing, firmware protection
The companion's telemetry is visible to everyone on the network

Cause: output to a broadcast address, ROS 2 discovery across the whole network.

Fix: the GCS address instead of broadcast, a firewall, limited discovery, SROS2.

Remote ID and vehicle security: broadcast, MAVLink signing, firmware protection
After an update the firmware does not start on a secure bootloader

Cause: the firmware is not signed or is signed with a different key.

Fix: sign it with your key by the Secure Firmware procedure.

Remote ID and vehicle security: broadcast, MAVLink signing, firmware protection
SITL does not see the rangefinder even though packets are arriving

Cause: not rebooted after RNGFND1_TYPE; the orientation does not match RNGFND1_ORIENT; values outside min/max.

Fix: reboot; ORIENT 25 ↔ PITCH_270; check the units (cm versus m).

Your own MAVLink component: sensor, parameters, messages
The rangefinder “flickers” healthy/unhealthy

Cause: the send rate is low or unstable (a sleep in the receive loop).

Fix: send on a timer at ≥ 10 Hz, non-blocking reception.

Your own MAVLink component: sensor, parameters, messages
QGC does not show the component's parameters

Cause: no heartbeat from the component, or the replies go out with the autopilot's compid.

Fix: heartbeat; reply with your own compid and respect target_component in the request.

Your own MAVLink component: sensor, parameters, messages
“broken” frames arrive from the ESP32 from time to time

Cause: two tasks write to the UART at the same time.

Fix: a single sender task with a queue, or a mutex on the UART.

Your own MAVLink component: sensor, parameters, messages
The gimbal doesn't respond to commands from the GCS

Cause: wrong MNT1_TYPE, a MAVLink gimbal has no component 154, the port isn't in MAVLink/Gimbal mode, the gimbal mode is RC instead of MAVLink.

Fix: MAVLink inspector, MNT1_DEFLT_MODE, the port protocol, a reboot after changing the type.

Gimbals and payload: MAVLink Gimbal v2, camera, drop
The camera swings off to the side when the vehicle turns

Cause: yaw is in follow mode, not lock.

Fix: the YAW_LOCK flag, Mount Lock from the radio (163), or ROI.

Gimbals and payload: MAVLink Gimbal v2, camera, drop
Radio and companion commands “fight”

Cause: two clients without an explicit control takeover.

Fix: MAV_CMD_DO_GIMBAL_MANAGER_CONFIGURE, a single point of control, an explicit switch on the radio.

Gimbals and payload: MAVLink Gimbal v2, camera, drop
Fewer shots than expected

Cause: CAM1_TYPE doesn't match the wiring, CAM1_TRIGG_DIST isn't set, the camera can't keep up (the interval is shorter than the write time).

Fix: check the type and the CAM messages in the log, increase the distance or reduce the speed.

Gimbals and payload: MAVLink Gimbal v2, camera, drop
After the payload drop the vehicle climbs sharply

Cause: the step change in mass, a drop in a mode without altitude hold.

Fix: drop in a hover in an altitude-holding mode, with altitude to spare; check the response in SITL.

Gimbals and payload: MAVLink Gimbal v2, camera, drop
The vehicle does not respond to targets

Cause: not in GUIDED, not armed, or type_mask ignores the fields you need.

Fix: check the mode and arming in the heartbeat, the mask, and STATUSTEXT.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
It flies up instead of down (or the other way around)

Cause: the sign of Z in NED.

Fix: Z is positive down; a climb is a negative Z.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
It hovers after a couple of seconds although a command was sent

Cause: the velocity target was sent once, and the timeout fired.

Fix: send it regularly (5–10 Hz).

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
It stops at a boundary before reaching the target

Cause: the geofence or the WP_* limits.

Fix: check FENCE_* and the FC messages; this is protection, not a bug.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
The companion script receives only HEARTBEAT, with no position or mode

Cause: ArduCopter 4.7: the MAVn_* streams default to 0; the script listens to the port directly, with no station to request the streams.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the router's tlog has only a HEARTBEAT once a second from sysid 1.

Fix: MAV_CMD_SET_MESSAGE_INTERVAL for the messages you need after every connection, or set MAVn_* for your channel (with a reboot).

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
After the update to 4.7 the parameters SR2_*, SYSID_THISMAV, SYSID_MYGCS have "disappeared"

Cause: the 4.7 rename: MAVn_* (the MAVLink channel number, not the SERIAL number), MAV_SYSID, MAV_GCS_SYSID.

Fix: look for the new names; count the channel number in the order of the MAVLink ports (the console is MAV1).

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
Random command and mission timeouts, lost packets with 50 Hz telemetry

Cause: the companion port is at 57600 (the SERIAL1/2 default), with the link load above 50%.

Fix: SERIALn_BAUD = 921, calculate the load (frame bytes × rate), and at 1500 use RTS/CTS.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
The companion went silent in the middle of a flight, and the vehicle kept flying to the point

Cause: the last target was a position, which has no GUID_TIMEOUT; the silence is often caused by a power sag on the companion.

Fix: in modes where a companion failure must stop the vehicle, send velocity targets; a separate BEC; if needed, a Lua watchdog on the companion's HEARTBEAT.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
The GCS failsafe does not trigger although the ground station is gone, or it triggers with the companion alive

Cause: sysid confusion: the failsafe watches only the sysids in MAV_GCS_SYSID…_HI, and the companion's heartbeat (sysid 1) does not reset it; a station with a nonstandard sysid outside the range.

Fix: check the station's sysid and MAV_GCS_SYSID; keep the companion on the vehicle's sysid, compid 191.

Companion + ArduPilot: control from code
In the GCS one vehicle “jumps” between two places

Cause: two vehicles with the same sysid.

Fix: unique MAV_SYSID/MAV_SYS_ID, reboot, --auto-sysid in SITL.

Multiple vehicles: addressing, separation, formation and a supervisor
A command went to the wrong vehicle or to all of them

Cause: target_system left at default or 0, the script took target_system from the first heartbeat.

Fix: an explicit sysid in every command, check for COMMAND_ACK from the right sysid.

Multiple vehicles: addressing, separation, formation and a supervisor
The vehicle doesn't go into failsafe when it loses its GCS

Cause: it's being kept alive by the heartbeat of another station or the supervisor.

Fix: MAV_GCS_SYSID (before 4.7 — SYSID_MYGCS), separate sysids for helper scripts.

Multiple vehicles: addressing, separation, formation and a supervisor
All vehicles on RTL converge over home

Cause: the same altitude and return point.

Fix: different RTL altitudes and rally points, land one at a time.

Multiple vehicles: addressing, separation, formation and a supervisor
The follower in FOLLOW stays in place

Cause: FOLL_SYSID not set, the leader is farther than FOLL_DIST_MAX, the leader's positions don't reach the follower.

Fix: check the parameters and that the leader's GLOBAL_POSITION_INT is routed to the follower.

Multiple vehicles: addressing, separation, formation and a supervisor
Cloud Build fails with a size error

Cause: too many options for the board's flash (F411/F722 — 480 KB in FLASH1).

Fix: open Show Log., find the message about the FLASH1 overflow; remove what is unused (the second OSD, extra protocols, GPS without a GPS).

Your own firmware builds
build request error (please check build configuration, e.g. custom defines)

Cause: the server rejected the request itself; most often Custom Defines are to blame.

Fix: one name per single space, without USE_, without commas; check the name against the sources of the required version. An unknown name usually does not break the build, it just does not give you the feature.

Your own firmware builds
After a custom build a feature “is there” but does not work

Cause: the option needs a pin that was not assigned through resource; or the feature is enabled but the code is not in the build (LED_STRIP).

Fix: get by the name of the feature's settings and help by the command name; if INVALID NAME — rebuild with the option; otherwise resource, reboot, status.

Your own firmware builds
After pasting diff all the settings were not saved, and the output has ERRORS WERE DETECTED

Cause: the batch has lines for options that are not in the new build; a batch with an error blocks save.

Fix: read all the ###ERROR lines, make sure they are only the deliberately removed options, type save by hand.

Your own firmware builds
The analog OSD disappeared after a rebuild

Cause: only OSD_HD was built — the MAX7456 driver is stripped out.

Fix: add OSD_SD (or both).

Your own firmware builds
The motors do not respond to DShot, get dshot_bidir → INVALID NAME

Cause: a cloud build without DSHOT in Motor Protocol; motor_pwm_protocol = DSHOT600 is accepted all the same, but there is no DShot code. The same with the receiver: serialrx_provider = CRSF is accepted even without SERIALRX_CRSF in the build.

Fix: rebuild with DSHOT; after flashing, get dshot_bidir must answer with a value.

Your own firmware builds
The Configurator has no GPS / OSD / LED Strip / Servos tab

Cause: the option is not in the build — the Configurator hides the tab based on the option list.

Fix: Setup → Firmware info → Options; rebuild.

Your own firmware builds
ArduPilot waf configure does not find the board

Cause: a typo in the name, missing submodules.

Fix: ./waf list_boards, git submodule update --init --recursive.

Your own firmware builds
After a custom build some parameters disappeared

Cause: that is exactly the effect of a disabled subsystem.

Fix: check against the build record; if the parameter is needed — bring the feature back.

Your own firmware builds
I can't find where a parameter is used

Cause: the name in the code differs from the name in the GUI (Betaflight: pidProfile()->pid[PID_ROLL].D; ArduPilot: _pid_rate_roll from ATC_RAT_RLL_*).

Fix: search by @Param/PG_REGISTER/parameter_names.h, then by the struct field.

How to read firmware source code: Betaflight and ArduPilot
The SITL build fails

Cause: wrong dependencies/toolchain.

Fix: Tools/environment_install/install-prereqs-ubuntu.sh (ArduPilot), make arm_sdk_install/make SITL per the Betaflight README.

How to read firmware source code: Betaflight and ArduPilot
The patch does not affect behavior in SITL

Cause: you changed the wrong path (a different controller/mode), or the value is overridden by a parameter.

Fix: printf/send_text at the point of change, check that the code actually executes.

How to read firmware source code: Betaflight and ArduPilot
“the theory doesn't match” the code

Cause: that is normal — scaling, filters, order of operations; sometimes it is a bug.

Fix: record the discrepancy in the report with a quote; if you suspect a bug — an issue with a reproduction in SITL.

How to read firmware source code: Betaflight and ArduPilot
There are requirements, but the tests are “by feel”

Cause: the acceptance criteria are not numeric.

Fix: rewrite them with numbers and a method; a requirement without a method is not a requirement.

Systems engineering: requirements, FMEA, documentation, tests
After a firmware update everything broke

Cause: no regression and no rollback.

Fix: a tag before the update, a regression suite, a rollback procedure in the documentation.

Systems engineering: requirements, FMEA, documentation, tests
The project handover took weeks

Cause: the knowledge is in someone's head, not in the package.

Fix: a handover test, ADRs, an interface table.

Systems engineering: requirements, FMEA, documentation, tests
The FMEA was written once and went stale

Cause: no process for updating it after incidents.

Fix: an incident review ends with a row in the FMEA and a change to a mitigation.

Systems engineering: requirements, FMEA, documentation, tests
Diff all differs completely every time

Cause: order/comments/date in the export.

Fix: normalize and sort before committing.

CI and versioning of configs and code
SITL in CI does not start/times out

Cause: the build is slow, no --no-mavproxy/headless, ports.

Fix: build cache or a prebuilt image, headless launch, wait for heartbeat with a timeout.

CI and versioning of configs and code
The test passes even though failsafe is broken

Cause: the assert checks the wrong thing (mode is not read) or the test does not wait.

Fix: verify that the test fails on a broken config (mutation test).

CI and versioning of configs and code
I don't remember which config I flew with that time

Cause: no tags/releases.

Fix: a tag on every flight config, a log attached to the release.

CI and versioning of configs and code
Удостоверение пилота есть, работы нет

Cause: допуск без ниши и навыка обработки данных.

Fix: выбрать нишу (инспекция, картография, агро) и сделать одну работу с измеренной точностью: proj-photogrammetry (RMSE), proj-inspection.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Портфолио — скриншоты и видео без файлов

Cause: нет воспроизводимого артефакта.

Fix: к каждой работе — конфиг, исходный лог, README с числом результата; другой человек должен повторить вывод без тебя.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Issue закрыли как «support», PR висит без ответа

Cause: вопрос по настройке в трекере ошибок; PR без шагов воспроизведения и свидетельств теста; несколько изменений в одном PR.

Fix: вопросы — в Discord или на форуме проекта; баг-репорт с шагами и логом; один PR — одно изменение, с графиком до/после из SITL.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Покрытие роли 80 %, а интервью провалено

Cause: модули пройдены, но нет своей работы, которую можно объяснить с числами.

Fix: по таблице типов вопросов — одна своя работа на каждый тип с числом из своего лога.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Удостоверение «внезапно» недействительно

Cause: истёк срок (5 лет в ЕС и Великобритании) или пропущен recurrent-курс FAA (24 календарных месяца).

Fix: дата окончания — в журнал оператора; в Чехии продление открывается за 180 дней.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Зарплатные цифры из разных источников расходятся в разы

Cause: опросы школ и блогов, разные роли под одним названием.

Fix: брать только официальную статистику с годом и страной (BLS — май 2025); нет такой — не опираться на число.

A career in drones: roles, skills, certificates, portfolio
Stripes of under-application on the field, while in the log spraying is “on” the whole time

Cause: the pump is saturated: speed × SPRAY_PUMP_RATE ≥ 100 %; by default that is 10 m/s.

Fix: recalculate SPRAY_PUMP_RATE for the rate and the pump flow, reduce the pass speed, check with water by the procedure from the practice.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows
Stripes of double rate and gaps spaced one pass apart

Cause: the track error is comparable to the swath width — flying without RTK FIX, or the ZIGZ_SIDE_DIST spacing does not equal the real swath.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: GPS.Status less than 6 (6 — RTK Fixed, 5 — RTK Float), GPA.HAcc on the order of meters.

Fix: RTK (nav-rtk), the pass spacing — from the swath measured at your height.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows
The NDVI of one field “changes” from flight to flight

Cause: different lighting without radiometric calibration.

Fix: calibration by the camera manufacturer's procedure, surveying at the same time of day.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows
The temperature of an overheated contact in the report is lower than that of the neighboring one

Cause: the target is smaller than 3 × 3 px or the emissivity is wrong; according to FLIR that is a shift of 2 °C and more.

Fix: fly closer than D_max = L·f/(3p), set the emissivity of the material, eliminate reflections.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows
The delivery aircraft lands on reserve before returning

Cause: the radius was calculated without the hover and winch or without the reserve; a headwind on the way back.

Fix: t/(1 − reserve) with the wind per ops-weather; with t at the limit of 20–40 min — a VTOL.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows
At the show rehearsal two aircraft come closer than the spacing

Cause: no RTK corrections on some of the aircraft, an error of 2–3 m against a 3 m spacing.

Fix: check the RTK fix of each one before takeoff, increase the spacing or do not take off; the check of spacing and speeds is Safety Check in Skybrush Studio.

Application segments: agriculture, inspection, delivery, shows

Perception and intelligence

Latency grows over time

Cause: the algorithm is slower than the FPS — the buffer queue piles up.

Fix: BUFFERSIZE 1, take the latest frame, lower the resolution/FPS, skip frames.

Cameras and video streaming on the companion
“jello”/bending during motion

Cause: rolling shutter + vibration.

Fix: damp the camera, global shutter, shorter exposure.

Cameras and video streaming on the companion
The stream stutters in QGC

Cause: bitrate above the link throughput, packet loss without keyframes.

Fix: lower bitrate, more frequent iframes, check Wi-Fi/the link budget.

Cameras and video streaming on the companion
The CSI camera isn't detected

Cause: ribbon cable/connector, length, overlay not enabled.

Fix: rpicam-hello --list-cameras (before Raspberry Pi OS Bookworm — libcamera-hello), a shorter ribbon cable, the dtoverlay config.

Cameras and video streaming on the companion
Auto exposure “flickers” when the vehicle tilts

Cause: AE reacts to the sky/ground.

Fix: fixed exposure/gain or a limited range.

Cameras and video streaming on the companion
Ground points are mirrored relative to the heading

Cause: the camera and body frames are mixed up (image x ≠ body y) or the roll sign is wrong.

Fix: test “object on the right of the frame at yaw = 0 → positive E”; synthetic round-trip.

Camera geometry: from a pixel to a point on the ground
The error grows toward the edges of the frame

Cause: distortion isn't removed, or the calibration is for a different resolution.

Fix: check the calibration resolution, apply undistort to the points.

Camera geometry: from a pixel to a point on the ground
During maneuvers the point “wanders” by meters, in hover it's stable

Cause: frame and attitude are out of sync.

Fix: hardware timestamp, ATT interpolation, global shutter or a lower angular rate.

Camera geometry: from a pixel to a point on the ground
Systematic range error on a slope

Cause: altitude relative to the takeoff point instead of height above terrain.

Fix: rangefinder or DEM; intersect the ray with the terrain instead of a plane.

Camera geometry: from a pixel to a point on the ground
Calibration RMS > 1 px

Cause: blurry frames, too few tilted views, a board that isn't flat, stabilization or autofocus turned on.

Fix: reshoot with a shorter exposure, a rigid board, EIS/AF off.

Camera geometry: from a pixel to a point on the ground
30 fps on a file, 8 fps from the camera

Cause: capture/conversion on the CPU, frame copies.

Fix: a per-stage profile, hardware preprocessing, a lower resolution.

Computer vision: from OpenCV to neural networks
MAP 0.9 on validation, but in the field it doesn't see anything

Cause: validation from the same distribution as training; the field is different.

Fix: held-out frames from other conditions, collect more data.

Computer vision: from OpenCV to neural networks
False positives on shadows/glare

Cause: too few negative examples.

Fix: add “empty” frames with shadows to training, temporal filtering.

Computer vision: from OpenCV to neural networks
Accuracy dropped badly after INT8

Cause: poor quantization calibration.

Fix: a calibration set from your own data, FP16 as a compromise.

Computer vision: from OpenCV to neural networks
The object ID keeps jumping

Cause: no tracker or weak matching.

Fix: ByteTrack, detection on every frame for tests.

Computer vision: from OpenCV to neural networks
30 frames/s on the bench, 12 after 10 minutes in flight

Cause: throttling in an enclosure without airflow.

Fix: airflow from the props or a fan, a heatsink, a temperature and clock log, the power mode.

Onboard ML: budget, quantization, evaluation, maintenance
After INT8 the model “went blind” at dusk

Cause: PTQ calibration on daytime frames only.

Fix: a calibration set covering all conditions; QAT; a check by slice.

Onboard ML: budget, quantization, evaluation, maintenance
Excellent test metrics, lots of misses in the field

Cause: a split by frame instead of by flight; the test isn't from your own conditions.

Fix: a held-out set split by flight and conditions, slices by distance.

Onboard ML: budget, quantization, evaluation, maintenance
Occasional 300–500 ms frames among 40 ms ones

Cause: the garbage collector, writes to the memory card, CPU contention, an unsupported layer on the CPU.

Fix: the engine profiler, core isolation, writing in a separate thread, p99 in the budget.

Onboard ML: budget, quantization, evaluation, maintenance
After a model update, investigating a flight is impossible

Cause: the model and threshold version isn't written to the log.

Fix: the version in the metadata and in the log of every flight, keep the previous version for rollback.

Onboard ML: budget, quantization, evaluation, maintenance
The EKF does not accept VIO (XKF4.SP > 1 constantly)

Cause: camera coordinate frame/orientation, latency, scale.

Fix: VISO_ORIENT/POS, DELAY_MS, compare VISP with XKF1 axis by axis and sign by sign.

Visual navigation: optical flow, VIO, SLAM overview
The position drifts while stationary

Cause: VIO without texture/light, bad camera–IMU calibration.

Fix: lighting/texture, recalibration, time offset.

Visual navigation: optical flow, VIO, SLAM overview
The position jumps during a turn

Cause: tracking loss during fast rotation (rolling shutter).

Fix: global shutter, limit the yaw rate, shorter exposure.

Visual navigation: optical flow, VIO, SLAM overview
After losing VIO the vehicle flew away

Cause: the companion kept sending a “frozen” pose.

Fix: tracking status → stop publishing; FS_EKF_ACTION; backup source set.

Visual navigation: optical flow, VIO, SLAM overview
PX4 rejects odometry

Cause: frame (ENU vs NED), EKF2_EV_CTRL not set, no timestamp.

Fix: MAVROS frame_id, EV parameters, timesync.

Visual navigation: optical flow, VIO, SLAM overview
PreArm: GPS 2: not healthy, арминг отказан, хотя GPS2_RAW.fix_type = 3

Cause: GPS_INPUT приходит реже 5 Гц (например, по одной сырой привязке в 0.5–5 с); средний интервал ≥ 215 мс.

Fix: слать сглаженную оценку фильтра на 5 Гц; на земле — известную точку взлёта с fix_type = 3 (иначе GPS 2: Bad fix).

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
На зрении аппарат сползает вбок на прямых, привязки подряд отбрасываются воротами узла, затем EKF variance и LAND

Cause: курс EKF ушёл на градусы, а VO поворачивает сдвиг кадра по нему; по скорости такая ошибка не наблюдаема.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: разность XKF1.Yaw − SIM.Yaw 5° (на железе — против курса из привязок), GPS[1] отстаёт поперёк пути всё сильнее.

Fix: поправка курса из остаточного поворота гомографии по каждой прошедшей проверку привязке, даже если ворота позиции её отвергли.

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
GPS Glitch or Compass error ровно на входе в участок без текстуры

Cause: узел поднял speed_accuracy выше 1.5 м/с — EKF3 считает такой приёмник негодным для полёта (возврат при < 1.0).

Fix: пока оценка годна — sAcc ≤ 1.0; когда нет — честный fix_type = 1, а не «плохая, но 3D».

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
Аппарат улетает на сотни метров, при этом GPS2 fix = 3, h_acc 40–120 м, EKF без ошибок

Cause: узел ослеп и шлёт счисление с постоянной скоростью; EKF идёт за выдумкой, а ворота скорости заперлись на устаревшем значении и не пускают ожившую VO.

Consequences: в SITL 1.5 км от маршрута за 150 с.

Fix: 2 с без VO → fix_type = 1 и переопределение по всей карте; ворота размыкаются после 3 отказов подряд; геозона и FS_EKF_ACTION = 1.

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
VO на прямой даёт постоянный процент ошибки с первого метра

Cause: неверная AGL (баро над точкой взлёта вместо высоты над рельефом), неверный f после смены разрешения.

Fix: дальномер; сверить масштаб гомографии привязки — он должен быть 1.00 ± 0.03.

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
Привязка с σ 0.5–0.7 м ошибается на 3–5 м

Cause: inliers сбились в угол кадра у края однородной зоны (берег, граница поля); RMS мал, геометрия слабая.

Fix: ворота 3σ по инновации, порог inliers, требование разброса inliers по кадру; не поднимать вес привязки ниже σ = 0.5 м.

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
На снижении ниже 40–45 м оценка «плывёт», аппарат уводит от точки

Cause: след кадра меньше окна признаков карты; в стенде — синтетическая текстура, увеличенная в 4–6 раз.

Consequences: в SITL без запасного источника — 50 м на высоте 20 м и 204 м к касанию.

Fix: ниже рабочей высоты — набор EK3_SRC2 с оптическим потоком и дальномером или ручная посадка.

Navigating by terrain with a camera: frame vs map, visual odometry and return without GNSS
Holes and “smeared” areas in the orthophoto

Cause: insufficient overlap or uniform texture (field, water, forest).

Fix: overlap of 85/75 and more, higher altitude, fly at a different time of day.

Mapping: surveying, georeferencing, photogrammetry
The map is shifted by several meters

Cause: georeferencing from the onboard GNSS only, without RTK/GCPs.

Fix: GCPs with RTK coordinates or PPK; assess against checkpoints.

Mapping: surveying, georeferencing, photogrammetry
The log has fewer shots than there are files on the card (or vice versa)

Cause: missed triggers, frames taken in turns, no feedback.

Fix: CAM1_FEEDBAK_PIN, DO_SET_CAM_TRIGG_DIST only on the survey lines, CAM_MAX_ROLL.

Mapping: surveying, georeferencing, photogrammetry
Blurry frames on one direction of the survey lines

Cause: a tailwind increases ground speed, the shutter speed is too slow.

Fix: survey lines across the wind, a faster shutter, a lower speed.

Mapping: surveying, georeferencing, photogrammetry
An elevation model with “steps” along the survey lines

Cause: frame time and position out of sync, rolling shutter at speed.

Fix: coordinates from CAM with feedback, the rolling shutter option in ODM, a lower speed.

Mapping: surveying, georeferencing, photogrammetry
The vehicle flies a spiral over the target or moves away from it

Cause: the angle_x/angle_y axes/signs are mixed up, or PLND_YAW_ALIGN isn't accounted for.

Fix: in SITL move the target north; the vehicle must go north. Check each axis separately.

Precision landing on a marker: ArUco, LANDING_TARGET, PLND
Oscillation over the target that grows during descent

Cause: camera latency without an exposure timestamp, or a wrong PLND_LAG.

Fix: time_usec = exposure time on the companion's monotonic clock; measure the constant camera → autopilot latency and set it in PLND_LAG.

Precision landing on a marker: ArUco, LANDING_TARGET, PLND
The landing is abandoned in the last meter

Cause: the marker doesn't fit in the frame, there's no nested marker; PLND_ALT_MIN = 0 or the rangefinder is lying.

Fix: a nested marker, check RFND, set PLND_ALT_MIN.

Precision landing on a marker: ArUco, LANDING_TARGET, PLND
The target isn't accepted, there's no PL in the log

Cause: PLND_TYPE isn't 1, the messages come from the wrong system, no rangefinder.

Fix: check the sender's sysid/compid, a rate of ≥ 10 Hz, that a rangefinder is present.

Precision landing on a marker: ArUco, LANDING_TARGET, PLND
The vehicle doesn't react to an obstacle even though the companion is sending data

Cause: PRX1_TYPE isn't 2, wrong frame or angle_offset, rate < 5 Hz, sysid mismatch.

Fix: check PRX in the log and the Proximity tab in Mission Planner — are the sectors visible.

Depth and obstacle avoidance: sensors, OBSTACLE_DISTANCE, algorithms
The vehicle is “afraid” of a direction where there's nothing

Cause: 0 or a garbage value in the sector instead of 65535; reflections from the frame and landing legs.

Fix: 65535 for “no data”, mask out your own airframe, min_distance.

Depth and obstacle avoidance: sensors, OBSTACLE_DISTANCE, algorithms
Avoidance works in Loiter but not in a mission

Cause: OA_TYPE = 0, or Dijkstra selected without a polygon geofence.

Fix: BendyRuler for sensors; Dijkstra — only for geofences.

Depth and obstacle avoidance: sensors, OBSTACLE_DISTANCE, algorithms
In PX4 the vehicle won't fly backward or sideways

Cause: no data in those sectors with CP_GO_NO_DATA = 0 — that's by design.

Fix: add sensors or deliberately allow movement without data.

Depth and obstacle avoidance: sensors, OBSTACLE_DISTANCE, algorithms
It stops too late

Cause: the delay was underestimated, v_max for the sensor range was exceeded.

Fix: measure the delay, limit the speed in the mode, increase the margin.

Depth and obstacle avoidance: sensors, OBSTACLE_DISTANCE, algorithms
The vehicle oscillates around the target

Cause: K_p is too high for the latency.

Fix: lower K_p, reduce the latency, target prediction, acceleration limiting.

Tracking and target-based control
Slowly catches up with a moving target

Cause: P only, so there's a steady-state velocity error.

Fix: FF on the target velocity from the tracker, a moderate I with anti-windup.

Tracking and target-based control
Jerks on every detection

Cause: commands from the raw detector without a tracker/filter.

Fix: tracker, command filter, acceleration limiting.

Tracking and target-based control
After losing the target the vehicle flew off on the last command

Cause: no watchdog, the autopilot held the last velocity until GUID_TIMEOUT.

Fix: watchdog 0.5 s → zero velocity; timeout → Loiter.

Tracking and target-based control
Commands aren't accepted

Cause: not in GUIDED/Offboard, setpoint rate too low, type_mask.

Fix: check the mode, ≥ 10 Hz, the mask; look at the GUIDED messages/COM_OF_LOSS_T.

Tracking and target-based control
Точка на земле «плавает» на 0.5–1 м в такт крену, хотя цель неподвижна

Cause: ориентация берётся из последнего ATTITUDE, а не интерполируется на метку экспозиции; метка кадра — время после cap.read().

What you see in the log/on the instrument: ошибка коррелирует с угловой скоростью, а не с углом.

Fix: метка буфера камеры + TIMESYNC, интерполяция; остаточный сдвиг подобрать на стенде качанием рамы.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
После перекрытия или тени аппарат уверенно летит к камню, тени, чужой машине

Cause: переприхват по пикселям без проверки на земле; ворота по размеру слишком широкие или кадр растянут полным undistort.

Fix: оценка по срезу «после потери»; undistort только точки; радиус переприхвата в метрах вокруг прогноза; после таймаута — защёлка до команды оператора.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
Команда отстаёт на секунду и больше, контур раскачивается

Cause: очередь кадров в драйвере или GStreamer; инференс медленнее камеры.

Fix: drop=true max-buffers=1; детекция раз в 2–3 кадра с трекером между; пересчитать K_p по измеренной T_d.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
Companion завис, аппарат ещё три секунды летит с последней скоростью

Cause: это штатное поведение GUID_TIMEOUT; watchdog был в том же процессе, что и завис.

Consequences: 18 м пути при 6 м/с.

Fix: GUID_TIMEOUT 0.5–1 с; watchdog отдельным процессом или systemd; геозона включена.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
Запрошено 6 м/с — летит 6 м/с при WP_SPD = 3

Cause: WP_SPD не ограничивает скорости SET_POSITION_TARGET_LOCAL_NED (измерено на ArduCopter 4.6.3, код 4.7.2 тот же).

Fix: лимит v_max на companion, проверка в SITL до полёта.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
После таймаута аппарат в LOITER снижается

Cause: стик газа внизу или пульта нет.

Fix: пилот держит газ в середине; без пилота — BRAKE или нулевая скорость в GUIDED.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
«Plnd: Frame not supported» либо PX4 пишет «Landing target: coordinate frame … unsupported»

Cause: ArduPilot с position_valid = 1 принимает только MAV_FRAME_BODY_FRD, PX4 — только MAV_FRAME_LOCAL_NED.

Fix: frame под стек; для ArduPilot проще углы angle_x/angle_y и distance.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command
FOLLOW включился, аппарат стоит на месте

Cause: цель дальше FOLL_DIST_MAX; FOLL_SYSID не совпадает с отправителем; сообщения реже раза в 3 с.

Fix: проверить sysid и частоту потока; FOLL_POS_P по умолчанию 0.1 даёт медленное сближение.

Onboard computer vision end to end: calibration → detection → tracking → geolocation → command

Elective: INAV

It does not arm although everything is configured

Cause: no GPS fix with navigation modes configured, or the compass is not calibrated.

Fix: read the reason in the OSD/configurator, wait for the fix, calibrate the compass.

Introduction to INAV
In POSHOLD it circles (“toilet bowl”) or drifts away

Cause: the compass — calibration, alignment, interference from the power wires.

Fix: recalibrate, move it onto a mast, check align_mag.

Introduction to INAV
RTH heads the wrong way

Cause: home was recorded in the wrong place (armed somewhere else) or a safehome was used.

Fix: check the home point in the OSD after arming and the list of safehomes.

Introduction to INAV
In MANUAL the fixed wing is uncontrollable

Cause: reversed servos or the wrong mixer preset.

Fix: check the control surface directions on the bench.

Introduction to INAV
After an INAV upgrade some settings “disappeared”

Cause: renamed settings in the diff were not applied (the red ### ERROR: Invalid name lines), and untouched settings got the new defaults.

Fix: read the CLI output while loading the diff, correct the lines using the “Moving from 8.0 to 9.1” table and the Settings.md of the new version, recalibrate.

Introduction to INAV
No fix or few satellites

Cause: the antenna is obstructed, interference from the VTX/power wires, the wrong port or baud rate.

Fix: go through the procedure from the “GPS receiver setup” section: status (GPS=OK), gpssats with and without the VTX, the constellations and gps_ublox_nav_hz for the module.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
In POSHOLD the aircraft drifts away in a spiral

Cause: a compass heading error.

Fix: calibration, align_mag or, for a module at an angle, align_mag_roll/pitch/yaw (Alignment Tool), remove the interference.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
After a landing and a repeat arm RTH heads somewhere other than where you first took off today

Cause: inav_reset_home = EACH_ARM (home is the point of the last arm), or HOME RESET was activated in flight.

Fix: for flights with restarts in the field — FIRST_ARM (default); before takeoff check the distance to home in the OSD.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
RTH climbs “into the sky”

Cause: nav_rth_alt_mode = EXTRA with a large nav_rth_altitude, or MAX after a high flight.

Fix: choose AT_LEAST and a sensible altitude.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
On RTH it landed somewhere other than home

Cause: a safehome was used.

Fix: check the list and safehome_usage_mode.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
ALTHOLD is enabled and the aircraft sags/jumps

Cause: nav_mc_hover_thr is far from the real hover throttle; nav_mc_althold_throttle = STICK with the stick off center.

Fix: set hover_thr from the OSD/log, use MID_STICK or HOVER.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
RTH aborted into a landing midway

Cause: nav_rth_abort_threshold — the distance to home was increasing (a wrong compass/home).

Fix: compass calibration, check the home point before takeoff (OSD).

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
The navigation modes “flicker” and drop out

Cause: the position estimate is invalid (eph > inav_max_eph_epv).

Fix: GPS quality (satellites, HDOP), the antenna, interference; do not raise the threshold “to make it work”.

GPS, compass and RTH in INAV
SITL prints [SOCKET] Unable to bind socket

Cause: port 5760/5761 is taken by another SITL (INAV or Betaflight) or by an old process.

Fix: close the process; the Configurator connects to UART1, so an error on UART2 does not matter.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
After save MULTISHOT is set instead of DSHOT
