Cause: this is SITL or a target without USE_DSHOT; config.c replaces the protocol.

Fix: in SITL this is normal; on a real board check the target and the motor output.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
It does not arm, status has the RX flag

Cause: no receiver frames — the wrong UART, serialrx_provider, no bind.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the flag clears as soon as frames arrive (step 4–5).

Fix: the Receiver tab, Ports, repeat the bind.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
In Receiver the throttle moves a different channel, the aircraft does not arm with the THR flag

Cause: the wrong map (TAER instead of AETR).

Fix: map AETR for CRSF, check against the Receiver bars.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
On the first arm the aircraft flips over or spins in place

Cause: the motors are in the wrong order or spin the wrong way for Quad X.

Consequences: broken props and frames; this is caught on the bench in 5 minutes.

Fix: remove the props, motor test, Motor Mixer Wizard, reverse in the ESC.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
The OSD shows “battery empty” much earlier or later than it really is

Cause: vbat_scale or current_meter_scale is not calibrated; scale was corrected the wrong way.

Fix: voltage — old × V_multimeter / V_INAV; current — old × mAh_INAV / mAh_charger.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
The idle current in the OSD is 0.3–1 A with the motors off instead of the real value

Cause: current_meter_offset is not set.

Fix: new = old + (I_INAV − I_ammeter) × scale, recheck after changing scale.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
When the radio is turned off the aircraft lands on the spot instead of RTH

Cause: failsafe_procedure = LAND (default), no GPS fix or compass, failsafe_min_distance applied, or the aircraft is closer than nav_min_rth_distance (5 m).

Fix: check the procedure and the sensors, repeat the test farther than 50 m.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
When ALTHOLD or POSHOLD is enabled the aircraft sags or jumps

Cause: nav_mc_hover_thr (1300 by default) is far from the real hover throttle.

Fix: take the hover throttle from the Blackbox log of the first hover.

INAV from scratch: a GPS quadcopter
After selecting a sensor status has the HWFAIL flag, arming is disabled

Cause: the sensor is selected but not found or has not replied for longer than 500 ms; baro_hardware = AUTO or pitot_hardware = AUTO on a board without the sensor; the port function is not assigned for a serial rangefinder.

Fix: the value NONE for a missing sensor; the Rangefinder or Optic flow function on the right UART; for MSP — check that packets are arriving.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
The SURFACE mode is not in the list

Cause: the platform is an airplane; the barometer or the rangefinder is not detected.

Fix: SURFACE exists only on a multirotor with both sensors.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
In SURFACE the aircraft descends by itself, “(!SURFACE UNRELIABLE!)” in the OSD

Cause: the altitude is greater than inav_max_surface_altitude; the tilt is greater than half the cone (only 13.5° for the VL53L1X); the surface does not reflect; the target from the throttle stick is below 10 cm.

Consequences: a descent of up to 2 m/s with the throttle at idle.

Fix: turn SURFACE off with the switch, lower nav_max_terrain_follow_alt, fly more smoothly or use a sensor with a wide cone.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
The rangefinder shows the distance in the OSD, but AGL does not use it

Cause: no healthy barometer; the reading after tilt compensation is greater than inav_max_surface_altitude; the reliability has not yet reached 0.75.

Fix: check logic operands 35 and 36; raise inav_max_surface_altitude to the real range of the sensor.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
POSHOLD on the flow drifts or cannot be enabled

Cause: inav_allow_dead_reckoning = OFF; no rangefinder, or its reliability is below 0.33; the flow quality is below the threshold (a smooth floor, darkness); a wrong align_opflow or opflow_scale.

Fix: calibrate the scale by rocking; check the sign of the displacement in the FLOW_RAW debug mode from docs/Blackbox.md.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
The flow calibration ran, but opflow_scale did not change

Cause: the sum of the body rates did not exceed 3600 — too little rocking, or the quality was below the threshold.

Fix: rock more vigorously, 20–40 °/s, above a contrasting surface.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
Arming is disabled after power-up, the CAL flag in status does not clear

Cause: the pitot zero is being taken; in wind the pressure spread is above the threshold, and the calibration restarts.

Fix: cover the pitot probe at power-up.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
The airspeed on the ground is not zero, or in flight it disagrees with the ground speed

Cause: the zero was taken in wind; a wrong pitot_scale; a leaking hose.

Consequences: with apa_pow > 0 a speed error directly changes the PID gains.

Fix: reboot with the probe covered; a there-and-back pass in calm air and a scale correction.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
The Multi Function Status OSD element shows the warning “PITOT FAIL” (without this element in the layout there is no indication: the speed on the screen simply becomes the computed one)

Cause: the reading went outside 30–200 % of the virtual airspeed — a blocked tube, a hose that came off; or the wind estimate is far from the truth.

Fix: land; INAV is already computing the speed from GPS and wind.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
A temperature element blinks at a normal temperature

Cause: the threshold was written in degrees, not in tenths: 60 is 6.0 °C.

Fix: temp_sensor with the thresholds ×10.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
Without a barometer the altitude in ALTHOLD wanders by meters

Cause: that is the accuracy of the GPS altitude, and there is no other source.

Fix: install a barometer; changing inav_default_alt_sensor will not add a source.

Additional INAV sensors: rangefinder and SURFACE, optical flow, pitot, temperature
After moving settings from Betaflight the CLI answers set roll_rate = 670 with an error

Cause: INAV rates are set in tens of °/s, range 4…180.

Fix: 670 °/s is roll_rate = 67; work out expo again with the rc_curves.c formula.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
profile 2 — the CLI does not know such a command

Cause: the guide was written for old versions.

Fix: control_profile 2; for the battery and mixer profiles — battery_profile, mixer_profile.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
PIDs set in the CLI went back to other values after a reboot

Cause: ez_enabled is on, and EZ Tune recalculates the PIDs, rates and filters at every boot.

Fix: change the ez_* sliders or set ez_enabled = OFF, and then tune by hand.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
The RPM filter is enabled, but the motor peaks are still in the gyro spectrum, and the motors are hot

Cause: no port with function 262144, or the ESC does not send telemetry; a wrong motor_poles.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: gyroPeakRoll[0] moves together with the throttle, gyroADC is noisy.

Fix: check the RPM with the OSD element “Motor RPM from ESC telemetry” (the Outputs tab of Configurator 9 does not show RPM) and the ESC temperatures in status; until it is fixed turn rpm_gyro_filter_enabled off and keep the dynamic notch.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
At mid throttle the aircraft flies normally, but in a sharp climb or at full throttle it starts to shake and flips

Cause: tpa_rate is greater than 100 — above a throttle of breakpoint + (2000 − breakpoint) · 100 / tpa_rate the TPA factor is negative, and P and D work with the opposite sign.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: at a high rcCommand[3] the sign of axisP is opposite to the sign of the error axisRate − gyroADC, and the oscillation grows.

Consequences: a flip and a crash at full throttle, that is, on takeoff or when avoiding an obstacle.

Fix: on the ground set tpa_rate to no more than 100 (0 to start with); check that the value was not carried over from an airplane profile.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
After set ez_filter_hz above 255 stabilization behaves unpredictably: jitter, jerks, the aircraft does not hold level

Cause: ez_filter_hz = 256…260 — a Smith predictor delay from 40 ms to infinite with a buffer of 65 samples (32 ms at a looptime of 500): the write goes past its end (certainly at 258–260) and corrupts the memory of the PID controller; 261…300 — the buffer is intact, but the delay is wrong.

Consequences: memory corruption in the stabilization task, a crash without error messages.

Fix: do not take off; set ez_filter_hz to no more than 255, save, reboot; check that get smith_predictor_delay shows less than 8.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
The aircraft is sluggish, “floats” behind the stick, slow oscillation in a descent

Cause: the 60 Hz cutoff and the default PIDs are designed for a heavy aircraft; a large filter delay.

Fix: raise gyro_main_lpf_hz (or ez_filter_hz) in steps of 10–20 Hz, watching the motor temperature; then P.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
High-frequency jitter, the motors are hot after 30 s of hover

Cause: the filter cutoff is too high, D or P is large.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: axisD is noisy with an amplitude comparable to axisP.

Fix: restore the cutoff, lower mc_d_* or ez_damping; check the props and the flight controller mounting.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
When ALTHOLD is enabled the aircraft sags or jumps

Cause: nav_mc_hover_thr is far from the real hover throttle; nav_mc_althold_throttle = STICK with the stick not at the hover position.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: after the mode is enabled mcVelAxisI[2] slowly moves to a large constant value.

Fix: write the hover throttle from rcCommand[3]; MID_STICK or HOVER if you prefer.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
The throttle “breathes” in ALTHOLD, RTH and a mission

Cause: nav_mc_vel_z_p or nav_mc_pos_z_p are raised, often after an upgrade from 8.x.

Fix: restore 100 and 50, then change nav_mc_vel_z_p in steps of 10–20 %.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
After the stick is released in POSHOLD the aircraft overshoots and comes back

Cause: the stop target is closer than the real braking distance.

Fix: raise nav_mc_pos_deceleration_time; or nav_user_control_mode = CRUISE and the MC BRAKING mode.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
The aircraft circles in POSHOLD, and the radius grows

Cause: a heading error — the compass, not the PIDs.

Consequences: if you continue, the spiral accelerates into a flyaway.

Fix: switch to ANGLE at once and land; check the alignment and calibration of the compass and interference from the power wires.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
In wind the aircraft drifts off the point although the angle is small

Cause: nav_mc_bank_angle or max_angle_inclination_* limit the angle; the acceleration g · tan θ is not enough.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: attitude sits at the limit, navVel does not catch up with navTgtVel.

Fix: raise the smaller of the two limits, making sure there is enough thrust to hold altitude at that angle.

Multirotor tuning in INAV: PIDs, rates, filters, EZ Tune and navigation PIDs
After a restore the VTOL has no hover mode, the second mixer profile is empty

Cause: the backup was taken with diff, not diff all — it has only the active profiles.

Fix: look for an old diff all or a Configurator auto-backup (Firmware Flasher → Open Backups); otherwise build the profile again per inav-mixer-vtol.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
Red ### ERROR: Invalid name lines on paste

Cause: the setting was renamed or removed in the new version; the file is from a different major version.

Fix: write the lines down, fix a copy of the file using the migration table, defaults, paste again. Yes, the 8 → 9 transition needs Full chip erase; red lines are not “fixed” by a repeated save.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
save answers N ERRORS WERE DETECTED and does not reboot

Cause: the file starts with batch start, and there were errors during the paste.

Fix: scroll the output to the first ### ERROR, fix the file; do not type save a second time.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
### ERROR: Unknown command, try 'help' on the line profile 1

Cause: the file is from INAV 7 or the guide is old: since 8.0 the command is called control_profile.

Fix: replace profile with control_profile (numbers from 1).

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
Configurator 9.1 restored the file without the migration window, and errors remained in the CLI

Cause: the file has no # Version: X.Y.Z line (the file was saved by hand from the CLI), and the version was not recognized.

Fix: add the line # Version: 8.0.1 at the start of the file or fix the names using the table.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
After moving the config to another board the horizon is tilted, the compass is wrong, and there are no ACC and COMPASS flags

Cause: someone else's acczero_*, accgain_*, magzero_*, maggain_* were moved together with the diff.

Fix: calibrate the accelerometer and the compass again; in a file for another board delete these lines.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
You pasted a file, changed your mind, typed exit — and some settings changed

Cause: the commands control_profile, mixer_profile, battery_profile write EEPROM themselves when the number changes.

Fix: defaults and a restore from your own backup.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
After the 8 → 9 upgrade the aircraft behaves differently although there were no errors on paste

Cause: settings that were not in the diff got the new defaults (GPS, the altitude estimator, nav_fw_pos_z_ff).

Fix: check against the table in inav-intro, add the values you need to the file explicitly.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
It does not arm, and the reason is unclear

Cause: one of the 24 flags; ARMSW appears on top of any other cause.

Fix: status, read the flags from left to right using the table, first remove the cause, then flip the switch; remember that CLI is always in the output.

INAV CLI: all commands, diff/dump and profiles, backup, restore and migration 7 → 8 → 9
On the throw the wing flips over at once

Cause: one elevon is reversed in roll, or stabilization helps the tilt.

Fix: go through the table of step 6 again, especially the ANGLE rows; reverse in smix/servo, not on the radio.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
The motor does not spin on the Outputs tab, the ESC beeps in a loop

Cause: the ESC does not understand ONESHOT125 (the default value) or is not calibrated.

Fix: motor_pwm_protocol = STANDARD, calibration per step 5.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
A servo buzzes and heats up on the ground

Cause: it stalls on the sum of roll and pitch, min/max are wider than the mechanics; servo_pwm_rate is higher than an analog servo can take.

Fix: narrow min/max, check with full diagonal sticks; set servo_pwm_rate = 50 back.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
It does not arm, status has the ROLL/PITCH flag

Cause: NAV LAUNCH is enabled (by a switch or by feature FW_LAUNCH), and the roll or pitch stick is not centered — this includes subtrim or radio trims that shift the center (fc_core.c).

Fix: release the sticks; the centers on the Receiver tab are 1500 ± 5; radio trims to zero, trimming is done with the servos.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
The servos and the motor do not respond either in MANUAL or on the Outputs tab, status has the PWMOUT flag or the outputs are silent without a flag

Cause: feature PWM_OUTPUT_ENABLE is off; a servo is on an output of a motor timer.

Fix: feature PWM_OUTPUT_ENABLE, save; the timer map and timer_output_mode — step 2 and inav-mixer-vtol.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
After autotrim the wing flies worse

Cause: trimming in a turn or in turbulence; the CG is far from the calculated one, and autotrim compensates for it with the surfaces.

Fix: restore mid from diff all, check the CG, repeat in calm air.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
During a glide at zero throttle the wing disarmed on a link loss

Cause: failsafe_throttle_low_delay is not 0.

Fix: set failsafe_throttle_low_delay = 0.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
In RTH the wing flies a circle noticeably larger than nav_fw_loiter_radius

Cause: the required bank is greater than nav_fw_bank_angle (a small radius at a high speed, or wind).

What you see in the log/on the instrument: the bank sits at the 35° limit, the distance to home is greater than set.

Fix: a larger radius or a slower cruise; tan φ = V²/(g·R).

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
The mAh in the OSD differs from the charger by 10–15 %

Cause: current_meter_scale was calculated with the proportion inverted.

Fix: scale × mAh_FC / mAh_charger; if the FC counted less — scale decreases.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
SITL starts but nothing flies

Cause: “Configurator only” mode — the simulator is not selected or not connected.

Fix: the RealFlight/X-Plane profile, RealFlight Link enabled, the chanmap matches the mixer.

INAV from scratch: a flying wing
There is HD video, but no INAV OSD at all

Cause: the MSP DisplayPort port (33554432) is not assigned or is assigned to the wrong UART; feature OSD is off; the wire “unit TX → flight controller RX” is not connected — the flight controller waits for an MSP request from the unit and sends nothing without it; the custom OSD is off in the goggles.

Fix: check serial and feature; check both signal wires for continuity; close the CLI — with the CLI open the OSD is not sent over MSP; enable Canvas Mode / the OSD type in the goggles.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
There is analog video, but no OSD

Cause: MSP DisplayPort is assigned to some port — then the MAX7456 is not initialized; the board has no chip (an HD version of the flight controller); the OSD OFF mode is active; the camera is not connected to the chip input.

Fix: remove the MSP DisplayPort function from all ports; check the Modes tab; osd_video_system = AUTO or PAL/NTSC by hand to match the camera.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
All the elements are bunched in the top left corner of the HD screen

Cause: the default layout is designed for 30×16.

Fix: after selecting osd_video_system arrange the elements again on the OSD tab.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
The elements at the right or bottom edge are not visible

Cause: osd_video_system sets a grid larger than that of the goggles (for example, DJIWTF 60×22 on an O3 with 53×20, AVATAR on HDZero).

Fix: set the value from the table; check with four corner elements.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
? characters instead of icons (DJI)

Cause: the BFHDCOMPAT / BF43COMPAT mode — the DJI font has no match for an INAV character.

Fix: if the goggles have the OSD type “INAV” — update the firmware and set DJI_NATIVE; otherwise remove such elements or hide the characters: osd_highlight_djis_missing_font_symbols = OFF.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
A DJI O4 stays at low power after arming

Cause: old O4 firmware does not recognize the arm state.

Fix: update the air unit and the goggles; if you cannot update — set enable_broken_o4_workaround = ON.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
The splash screen of the analog OSD shows “INVALID FONT” or “INVALID FONT VERSION”, garbage instead of icons

Cause: the MAX7456 still holds the font of another firmware or of an old version.

Fix: upload the font on the OSD tab of a configurator of the same version as the firmware.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
An alternate layout is empty

Cause: in layouts 1–3 all elements are hidden by default.

Fix: select the layout in the configurator and enable the elements, or copy the osd_layout 0 … lines with the first number replaced.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
Operation 32 of the framework does not change the layout

Cause: one of the OSD ALT modes is active — it has priority; or failsafe is active with osd_failsafe_switch_layout = ON.

Fix: remove OSD ALT from the switch or switch with logic only.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
The altitude alarm triggers “in the wrong place” with feet on the screen

Cause: osd_alt_alarm was entered in feet, but the firmware compares meters.

Fix: convert: feet · 0.3048.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
A custom element does not appear

Cause: the visibility condition is false; the element is not enabled in the active layout; the type number was carried over from 7.x.

Fix: temporarily set visibility 0; check osd_layout <layout> 147 …; compare the type numbers with the table.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
“stuck” characters remain on the HD OSD at the edge of the link

Cause: a packet with a cell update was lost.

Fix: keep osd_msp_displayport_fullframe_interval = 10; the value −1 disables the full redraw.

OSD in INAV: analog and HD (DJI O3/O4, Walksnail, HDZero), layouts, alarms, custom elements
The switch is pressed, there are no beeps, the value does not change

Cause: the CLI is open; the range channel is outside start…end (the end is not included); function 0; the slot is taken by a line with a lower index; the same function is in this slot once more.

Fix: adjrange in the CLI, compare the steps with the Receiver tab; spread the functions across slots, remove duplicates.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
The beeper beeps, the value stands still

Cause: the value is at the limit; function 1 or 22 (dummies); function 53 without a controllable VTX; function 59 without loaded missions.

Fix: check against the function table and the limits.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
The value runs to the limit by itself

Cause: the adjustment channel is a two-position switch or a knob; the switch is off center at power-up; one function twice in a slot (then the step runs at the frame rate of the radio link — 50…500 times a second).

Consequences: overheated motors (D), a wing losing speed in RTH (nav_fw_cruise_thr), on a multirotor with function 54 — tpa_rate above 100.

Fix: a self-centering switch; a switch position warning on the radio; remove the duplicate.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
A multirotor shakes or flips at high throttle and flies normally at low throttle; the config has an adjrange line with function 54

Cause: an adjustment took tpa_rate above 100 — the TPA factor above tpa_breakpoint became negative, and P and D work with the opposite sign (inav-mc-tuning).

Consequences: loss of control at high throttle; the value may turn out to be saved by a side-effect EEPROM write and survive a reboot.

Fix: reduce the throttle below tpa_breakpoint and land; on the ground get tpa_rate, restore the value from diff all, remove the line with function 54 or move it to a self-centering switch.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
After a reboot the adjustment is still there although Save was not given

Cause: stats = ON, autotrim, level trim with the sticks, a profile change with the sticks, with a CLI command (control_profile N, battery_profile N, mixer_profile N) or with the drop-down in the Configurator, the Save button in the Configurator.

Fix: restore the value from the diff all taken before the flight.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
A stick command does not trigger

Cause: the aircraft is armed; the CMS or the CLI is open; the channel endpoints do not reach 1100 / 1900; feature TX_PROF_SEL is not enabled for the profiles; the hold is shorter than 0.4 s (on a 50 Hz receiver hold for up to 0.8 s).

Fix: check the channels on the Receiver tab, widen the endpoints on the radio.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
Instead of saving, the flight controller beeped three times and changed the profile

Cause: with TX_PROF_SEL pitch did not reach LOW — that is the “profile 3” command.

Fix: control_profile 1 in the CLI or with the sticks; the values are not lost — the profile change wrote the config.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
You adjusted a PID, and after a VTOL transition “nothing changed”

Cause: mixer_control_profile_linking = ON, the steps went into the control profile of the other mode.

Fix: adjust in the mode the value is meant for; compare diff all for both profiles.

INAV in-flight adjustments: adjrange, stick commands, profiles, the OSD menu
After save the receiver, the GPS and the OSD are gone, and the Ports tab looks like a factory one

Cause: an invalid pair of functions on one UART or more than three ports with MSP — the firmware reset the whole table.

Fix: restore the serial lines from diff all; fix the pair by the rules of the section on ports.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
The radio has only the link sensors (1RSS, RQly), there are no RxBt, GPS, FM

Cause: feature TELEMETRY is off; the TX wire of the flight controller is not connected to the receiver; the CLI is open.

Fix: feature TELEMETRY, save; on the radio Delete all and Discover new.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
The coordinates on the radio are updated once every few seconds

Cause: the ELRS telemetry ratio Std at 250–500 Hz gives less than 10 bytes/s.

Fix: set 1:8 or a lower packet rate in the ELRS Lua; recalculate the bandwidth with the formula.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
A voice alert for the mode stopped triggering after an update to INAV 9

Cause: the FM strings changed: there is no AIR, and LOTR, WRTH, GEO, TURT appeared.

Fix: correct the conditions on the radio using the table of strings.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
SmartPort is silent on an F7 board

Cause: telemetry_halfduplex = OFF; the wire on RX instead of TX; telemetry_inverted = ON without modifying the receiver.

Fix: the wire on TX, telemetry_inverted = OFF, telemetry_halfduplex = ON.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
LTM or MAVLink on a port shared with MSP runs only in flight

Cause: that is the design: a shared port is given to telemetry while armed.

Fix: dedicate a separate UART or enable telemetry_switch and assign the TELEMETRY mode.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
A MAVLink ground station sees the aircraft but does not read parameters, does not arm and does not upload a mission

Cause: INAV does not handle parameters, COMMAND_LONG and MISSION_ITEM_INT.

Fix: configure through the Configurator over MSP; upload missions over MSP (inav-missions).

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
The ground station receives garbage or nothing

Cause: the telemetry baud rate field is 0 (AUTO): MAVLink — 57600, LTM — 19200; the ground station uses a different baud rate.

Fix: set the baud rate explicitly in the third baud rate field.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
A script sends MSP_SET_RAW_RC, and the channels do not change

Cause: the MSP RC OVERRIDE mode is not active; the channel is not in the mask or it is the channel of the mode itself; frames less often than 5 a second; less than 0.7 s has passed since the stream started.

Consequences: with a mask that includes throttle and a permanently active mode the script controls the motor.

Fix: check Modes, the mask and the frame rate (50 Hz with a margin).

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
A switch on CH13 or higher stopped working: the mode is on (or off) regardless of the radio, and on the Receiver tab the channel sits at one value

Cause: an MSP device (a companion, a script, a ground station) sent MSP2_INAV_SET_AUX_RC for this channel. The value is held without a timeout, and a zero in a frame means “do not change” — the sender cannot remove the override, even if it is already disconnected.

Consequences: if ARM, RTH or MANUAL was on such a channel, the pilot loses that switch until a reboot; the aircraft may stay armed after the device fails.

Fix: reboot the flight controller — there is no other way to clear the override; move the mode to CH5…CH12; find out which device sends the command.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
A Bluetooth module connects, but the Configurator does not see the flight controller

Cause: the baud rate of the MSP field does not equal the baud rate of the module; TX and RX are swapped; it is a fourth MSP port.

Fix: set the baud rate of the module, check the wires, remove MSP from the extra port.

INAV telemetry and links: CRSF/SmartPort/LTM/MAVLink, MSP RC, Bluetooth and ground stations
It does not arm with a mission loaded

Cause: the first waypoint is farther than nav_wp_max_safe_distance.

Fix: rewrite the mission for the current location or reset it.

INAV missions: waypoints and CLI wp
The aircraft flies to the waypoints but does not land at the end

Cause: the RTH has p1 = 0 (landing not allowed) or nav_rth_allow_landing = NEVER.

Fix: set p1 = 1 and check nav_rth_allow_landing.

INAV missions: waypoints and CLI wp
The mission altitude is wrong

Cause: bit 0 in p3 (absolute altitude) or centimeters confused with meters.

Fix: check p3 and the units of alt.

INAV missions: waypoints and CLI wp
wp shows an empty list after a reboot

Cause: wp save was not run.

Fix: save and check wp load.

INAV missions: waypoints and CLI wp
The aircraft flies past the waypoints

Cause: nav_wp_radius is too small for the speed (especially a fixed wing).

Fix: a larger radius, a lower speed at the waypoints.

INAV missions: waypoints and CLI wp
After the throw the wing dives or flips over

Cause: a reversed elevon or the wrong CG.

Fix: check the surface response to tilt in ANGLE on the ground, check the center of gravity.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
It loses altitude in a climb in navigation

Cause: a low nav_fw_cruise_thr/nav_fw_max_thr.

Fix: raise the cruise and the maximum throttle.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
Autotune changes nothing

Cause: flying in MANUAL, or too few sharp inputs, or expecting P/I to change.

Fix: ANGLE/ACRO, sharp inputs on all axes; P/I — by hand.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
Autolaunch does not start the motor

Cause: the throw was not detected (too weak) or a timeout.

Fix: throw harder, check nav_fw_launch_*.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
In a climb navigation loses speed, a stall

Cause: nav_fw_climb_angle is large with a small cruise_thr/pitch2thr.

Fix: an angle of 10–15°, a higher pitch2thr, cruise_thr from the log.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
Autolaunch triggers in the hand before the throw

Cause: nav_fw_launch_accel is low, shaking.

Fix: a higher threshold, hold it steady, test without the prop.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
In a loiter the wing “falls” into the circle

Cause: the radius is too small for the speed, the bank is limited.

Fix: a larger nav_fw_loiter_radius, nav_fw_bank_angle (35° by default) from the calculation of V_s in a turn.

Wing on INAV: mixer, auto launch, autotune
status has the GEOZONE flag and the OSD shows “NO FLY ZONE” although the aircraft is standing in a fly zone

Cause: the FZ has a non-zero min alt or sea level is enabled with a wrong altitude — on the ground the aircraft is outside the altitude range; or the takeoff point is outside the polygon, closer than 2 km to a vertex.

Fix: min alt = 0 for the takeoff zone or geozone_safehome_as_inclusive = ON; check the vertices in the editor.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
Zones are defined, but the aircraft crosses the border freely

Cause: feature GEOZONE is not enabled; the number of vertices does not match vertex count (the protection is off entirely); there was no reboot after editing; ACRO or MANUAL mode or a mission; the FZ was disabled because power was applied more than 2 km away from it.

Consequences: RTH also flies without a detour.

Fix: compare the output of geozone and geozone vertex, reboot, test in ANGLE.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
The geozone command answers as unknown

Cause: a board with 512 KB of flash (F411, F722) — USE_GEOZONE is not built.

Fix: a board on an F405, F745, H743 or AT32F435.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
Near the border the wing suddenly turns around and does not respond to the sticks

Cause: the AVOID action, the sticks are locked for 2.5 s, the maneuver lasts up to 30 s.

Fix: switch to ACRO or MANUAL — the action ends at once.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
After enabling ANGLE or HORIZON just above the FZ ceiling the wing flies straight down, the OSD shows “REACHED ZONE ALTITUDE LIMIT”, the sticks have no effect

Cause: the aircraft is above the FZ ceiling by less than geozone_safe_altitude_distance (or below a non-zero floor by the same amount); in this 9.1.0 branch the target altitude stays 0, and the maneuver lasts up to 30 s. The conclusion was drawn by reading geozoneUpdate() and was not verified in flight.

Consequences: a loss of up to 150 m of altitude in 30 s at the default nav_fw_auto_climb_rate — at low altitude that is a collision with the ground.

Fix: ACRO or MANUAL at once — the action ends; descend below the ceiling by hand and only then enable ANGLE. Raise the FZ ceiling above the working altitude.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
On RTH the aircraft flies straight through a no-fly zone

Cause: no path was found and geozone_no_way_home_action = RTH; RTH was activated outside an FZ; the detour was canceled with a double flip of the switch.

Fix: simplify the zones (sides shorter than 500 m, passages wider than 2.5 loiter radii), decide whether EMRG_LAND is acceptable.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
On RTH the wing lands in a spiral, not with an approach

Cause: fwapproach is not written in the slot of the selected safehome; both headings are 0 (north is 360); no safehome was selected because of safehome_max_distance; safehome_usage_mode = RTH_FS, and RTH was activated with the switch.

Fix: compare the number in the OSD message after arming with the fwapproach index.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
The wing loiters over the safehome endlessly

Cause: no wind estimate; both headings are negative, and the wind is a tailwind for both; the landing was aborted with a stick; nav_rth_allow_landing = NEVER; nav_rth_fs_landing_delay is running.

Consequences: the battery drains in the loiter.

Fix: make one of the headings positive; after an abort turn RTH off and on.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
Touchdown long before the safehome point

Cause: that is how the glide slope is built: the target goes underground 0.1 L before the point, and the motor is cut at nav_fw_land_glide_alt.

Fix: move the safehome deeper into the strip, reduce L or approach alt, raise nav_fw_land_glide_pitch.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
The approach is at an angle to the strip

Cause: nav_fw_wp_tracking_accuracy = 0, a large nav_wp_radius.

Fix: enable track-line tracking, nav_wp_radius down to 1000 cm.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
On a link loss the aircraft first flies away from home

Cause: trackback is taking it back along its own track.

Fix: this is the intended behavior; limit nav_rth_trackback_distance or set the mode to OFF.

Geozones, safehome, trackback and fixed-wing autoland in INAV
The copter flips on takeoff right after liftoff

Cause: the mmix lines do not match the real motor positions, the outputs are mixed up, the motors spin the wrong way or the props are on the wrong motors; props-out without motor_direction_inverted = ON.

Consequences: if only the yaw sign is wrong — not a flip but a fast spin around the vertical axis.

Fix: without props spin the motors one at a time on the Outputs tab and compare the position and the direction with the table; recalculate the line from the azimuth (roll = −sin φ, pitch = −cos φ).

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
It does not arm, status has the PWMOUT flag

Cause: there were not enough timers for the motors or the servos; a DShot motor landed on a timer without DMA.

Fix: status → the PWM output init error line; recalculate the layout from target.c, assign timer_output_mode, or change motor_pwm_protocol.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
A servo does not move although there is an smix rule

Cause: feature PWM_OUTPUT_ENABLE is not enabled; the servo is connected to a pad of a motor timer; a rule with weight 0 precedes the rule, and the table ended at it; the logic condition of the rule is false.

Fix: the Mixer tab — the output function; smix without arguments — the rules in sequence; check the condition on the Programming tab.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
A servo is connected to “its” output, but the neighboring one works

Cause: a gap in the servo numbers — the outputs are assigned consecutively from the lowest to the highest number.

Fix: renumber the servos without gaps.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
The gimbal does not respond to tilt

Cause: the CAMSTAB mode is not active — inputs 12 and 13 are zero.

Fix: assign CAMSTAB to a channel or to a permanent range; choose the weight from the µs/° of the servo.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
After pasting a diff the pusher motor disappeared

Cause: in the profile a line with throttle 0 preceded it — the mmix output stopped at it, and the motor line did not get into the backup.

Fix: write placeholders as −1.000; after a restore compare mmix in both profiles.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
The profile switch has no effect

Cause: a navigation mode is active (including ALTHOLD), sensor calibration is running after power-up, the CLI is open or the board has one profile (F411).

Fix: switch in ANGLE or ACRO without navigation; check that diff all has a mixer_profile 2 block.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
The pusher motor spins in the hover at low throttle

Cause: airmode_type = THROTTLE_THRESHOLD — the documentation forbids it for VTOL.

Fix: STICK_CENTER or STICK_CENTER_ONCE.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
On RTH the aircraft switched to the airplane and stalled

Cause: mixer_switch_trans_timer is small or 0, the pusher motor is written as a −1 placeholder.

Consequences: a loss of tens of meters of altitude; below that margin the automated transition must not be enabled.

Fix: measure the time to accelerate to a speed 20–30 % above stall in a manual transition and write it in tenths of a second.

The INAV mixer in full: mmix/smix, outputs and timers, flaperons, gimbals, mixer profiles and VTOL
A rule does not trigger

Cause: the activator is false, the wrong operand type (GVAR “value 1” versus “GVAR 1”).

Fix: watch the live state on the Programming tab, check the types.

INAV Programming Framework
Set GVAR writes to the wrong variable

Cause: operand A is “Global Variable 1” instead of “Value 1”.

Fix: the GVAR index is given as a Value.

INAV Programming Framework
After an INAV upgrade the rules broke

Cause: the operation/operand numbers changed (between 8.0.1 and 9.1.0 they did not: the new ones were appended at the end).

Fix: check against the tables of the version, rebuild the rules.

INAV Programming Framework
The logic has become unreadable

Cause: too many rules for IPF.

Fix: simplify or move to Lua/ArduPilot.

INAV Programming Framework
The strip is dark although led lines are configured

Cause: no feature LED_STRIP (or the firmware cleared it because of a timer shared with SoftSerial); the LEDS OFF mode is active; the first led line is empty; led_pin_pwm_mode is LOW or HIGH; PWM is running after ledpinpwm.

Fix: feature, led, get led_pin_pwm_mode; ledpinpwm without an argument; check the range of mode 15 in aux.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
After enabling the strip the servos or the motor are gone, arming is blocked by PWMOUT

Cause: the timer of these outputs has a channel with the LED flag (timer_output_mode … LED, or the board is laid out that way).

Fix: timer_output_mode, set AUTO back; the strip — on the stock LED pad (inav-mixer-vtol).

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The LEDs flicker, the colors are wrong

Cause: a 3.3 V data level with the strip powered from 5 V; a power sag.

Fix: per the INAV documentation — lower the strip supply to 4.7 V or less (a diode in the supply line); a separate BEC, a common ground.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
After moving a config from 8.x the arm and GPS colors went back to the old ones, and the mode_color lines gave errors

Cause: the special colors moved from mode 6 to mode 7.

Fix: replace 6 with 7 in the mode_color 6 … lines.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The arm and GPS colors set on the LED Strip tab are not applied

Cause: in Configurator 9.1.0 the special color buttons are tied to mode 6 — according to the source that is LOITER (a conclusion from reading the code, not verified on hardware).

Fix: set mode_color 7 <index> <color> in the CLI and check with the mode_color command.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The indicators on a quadcopter blink only on some of the arms

Cause: the front LEDs are not in the row y = 0, or the left ones are not in the column x = 0.

Fix: move the coordinates to the corners of the grid.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
PINIO does not respond to the switch

Cause: pinio_box does not hold the permanent id; there is no aux line; there was no reboot; the board has no PINIOn_PIN.

Fix: get pinio_box, aux, the USER mode must be highlighted on the Modes tab; check the target.h of the board.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The VTX is on when the switch is “off”, and vice versa

Cause: an inverted PINIO or a board switch with reversed logic.

Fix: measure the level in both positions, flip the aux range.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
After adding a rule with operation 38 the throttle, a stick or arming do not follow the radio: the motor spins up by itself, the aircraft cannot be disarmed with the switch or arms without it

Cause: operand A holds the number of a control channel — 1…4 (4 is throttle) or the channel of the ARM switch; or A is 0 and is constrained to channel 1. The override holds while the rule is active and does not depend on the state of the receiver.

Consequences: full throttle or arming without a pilot command; no way to disarm with the switch.

Fix: remove the propellers; disable the rule (logic N 0 … or the Programming tab); change A to a free AUX channel; check on the Receiver tab that with the rule active only the one intended channel changes.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The shutter does not fire on every interval

Cause: the window is shorter than two logic ticks at this speed; the remainder is compared with zero; the pulse is longer than the interval.

Fix: W ≥ 0.2 s · v; a pulse duration less than N / v.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The shutter does not fire at all although the rules are true

Cause: the override channel has no USER range; the CLI is open (the logic is stopped); pinio_box2 is not set.

Fix: the Programming tab — the state of the rules, the Modes tab — USER2 highlighted.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The beeper is silent on USB

Cause: the ON_USB condition is disabled with feature VBAT and there is no battery; BEEPER MUTE is active.

Fix: beeper ON_USB; check the range of mode 60.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
The beeper is weak or does not sound at all

Cause: beeper_pwm_mode does not match the type of the beeper or the board.

Fix: an active beeper — OFF; ON — only if the board supports PWM on the beeper pin.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
Arming fails, “MOTOR BEEPER ACTIVE” on the OSD

Cause: the BEEPER switch is on, the motors are beeping, and the guard pause does not expire.

Fix: turn BEEPER off, wait 0.4–1.2 s.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV
A crashed aircraft does not beep

Cause: RX_LOST is disabled; there was no arm after power-up; the battery came off.

Fix: beeper RX_LOST; with the radio connected — the BEEPER switch; only a self-powered beeper helps against a lost battery.

Lights, PINIO, the beeper and a camera trigger in INAV

Elective: PX4

QGC doesn't connect to SITL

Cause: another PX4 instance or a UDP port already in use.

Fix: close the old processes, restart SITL.

Introduction to PX4
Position won't engage

Cause: no GPS fix (in SITL — wait for initialization).

Fix: wait for “ready for takeoff” in QGC.

Introduction to PX4
The firmware won't install on an F4 board

Cause: PX4 doesn't support this hardware.

Fix: a Pixhawk-compatible FMUv5/v6, or ArduPilot.

Introduction to PX4
make fails on dependencies

Cause: ubuntu.sh hasn't been run.

Fix: run the environment setup script, log in again.

Introduction to PX4
“Preflight Fail: Compass sensor 0 missing” / “Accel inconsistent”

Cause: calibration not done or the sensor isn't detected (orientation, ribbon cable).

Fix: calibrate in order, check SENS_BOARD_ROT, the dmesg log in the console.

Introduction to PX4
Position mode won't engage

Cause: the EKF doesn't trust the GPS (EKF2_GPS_CHECK), no fix, the compass is lying.

Fix: QGC → MAVLink Console ekf2 status, the number of satellites, compass calibration in the field.

Introduction to PX4
A high vibration level in Flight Review

Cause: the mechanics, a rigid FC mount.

Fix: module “Frame and vibration”, dampers, balancing.

Introduction to PX4
ELRS приёмник светится «связь есть», а в QGC Radio каналы не двигаются

Cause: приёмник подключён к RC IN через IO (CRSF там не разбирается), порт занят MAVLink (MAV_1_CONFIG) или перепутаны TX/RX.

Fix: UART + RC_PORT_CONFIG или RC_CRSF_PRT_CFG, освободить порт, перезагрузка; в MAVLink Console — rc_input status или crsf_rc status.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
RC_CRSF_PRT_CFG нет в списке параметров

Cause: драйвер crsf_rc не собран для этой платы (до 1.17 — обычное дело; в 1.17 и 1.18 в fmu-v6x он есть, а нет уже RC_PORT_CONFIG).

Fix: rc_input на UART или своя сборка через boardconfig.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
Мотор не крутится в тесте Actuators, остальные крутятся

Cause: выход не назначен Motor N или DShot стоит на MAIN через IO.

Fix: PWM_AUX_FUNCn = 10x, протокол на группу AUX.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
При арме один мотор не запускается или стартует позже

Cause: PWM ESC без калибровки, min 1100 мкс ниже точки старта.

Fix: калибровка ESC в Power (USB, без пропов) или поднять minimum, пока мотор не начнёт едва крутиться.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
Аппарат сразу переворачивается на взлёте

Cause: порядок моторов или направление не совпадают с CA_ROTOR*, пропы перепутаны.

Consequences: сломанные пропы, риск для людей рядом.

Fix: повторить тест моторов по таблице, сверить CW/CCW.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
В полёте «Low battery», хотя АКБ полная; или наоборот — садится на 3.3 В/эл без предупреждения

Cause: не откалиброван BAT1_V_DIV, не задан BAT1_N_CELLS, COM_LOW_BAT_ACT = 0.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: battery_status.voltage_v расходится с мультиметром на постоянный процент.

Fix: калибровка по мультиметру, пороги, действие 3.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
МА·ч в логе на 7–10 % меньше, чем залила зарядка

Cause: BAT1_A_PER_V от другого силового модуля.

Fix: умножить на мА·ч_зарядка / мА·ч_лог, проверить ещё одним полётом.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
После потери RC аппарат 5 с висит и только потом идёт домой

Cause: это норма — Hold на COM_FAIL_ACT_T.

Fix: учитывать при планировании дистанции; уменьшать задержку осознанно.

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
failure rc_signal off в SITL отвечает «Timeout waiting for ack»

Cause: эта инъекция в симуляторах не обрабатывается (ответ снят на 1.15.4; в исходниках 1.17 и 1.18 обработчика тоже нет).

Fix: имитировать потерю, прекратив MANUAL_CONTROL (джойстик QGC или скрипт).

PX4 from scratch: Vehicle Setup in QGroundControl
Аппарат не армится, в QGC «Preflight Fail» или красный индикатор готовности.

Cause: одна из проверок таблицы арма; чаще всего калибровка, расхождение IMU или компасов, нет ручного управления, заряд ниже BAT_CRIT_THR, режим Return или Land.

Fix: прочитать сообщения Preflight Fail: … в QGC (или commander check, затем dmesg), найти строку в таблице проверок; проверку не отключать, пока не понята причина.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Требует нажать кнопку safety, которой нет, либо наоборот — кнопка есть, но ничего не блокирует.

Cause: CBRK_IO_SAFETY = 0 без кнопки; по умолчанию 22027 — кнопка не используется.

Fix: 22027 — без кнопки, 0 — с кнопкой; COM_PREARM_MODE = 1, если до арма нужны сервоприводы.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Моторы остановились в воздухе, в логе termination или «Kill engaged».

Cause: задет kill-тумблер; CBRK_FLIGHTTERM = 0 и крен больше FD_FAIL_R; действие Terminate у геозоны или потери связи; не failsafe вовсе — просадка питания и перезагрузка полётника.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: failsafe_flags.fd_critical_failure, actuator_armed.kill или .termination; при перезагрузке лог обрывается без дизарма.

Fix: kill — на тумблер с защитой от случайного нажатия; CBRK_FLIGHTTERM вернуть в 121212, если нет парашюта; Terminate в GF_ACTION и NAV_*_ACT не ставить без парашюта.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Дизарм в первые секунды после арма при попытке взлёта.

Cause: крен или тангаж больше 60° в первые 4 с (неверный порядок моторов или направление вращения); ESC не подтвердили арм (при FD_ACT_EN = 1; до 1.18 — FD_ESCS_EN); отрыва не было 11 с (COM_DISARM_PRFLT).

Fix: тест моторов без пропов по px4-setup-scratch; проверить телеметрию ESC.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
При потере пульта аппарат 5 с висит и только потом возвращается.

Cause: это COM_FAIL_ACT_T, штатное поведение.

Fix: менять только осознанно; при 0 перехват стиками в failsafe перестаёт работать.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Связь вернулась, а аппарат продолжает Return и не слушается стиков.

Cause: действие снимается только сменой режима; стики не перехватывают при MAN_OVERRIDE_SPD < 0, на самолёте, во время задержки Hold и при медленном движении стика — сравнивается скорость стика, а не отклонение.

Fix: переключить режим тумблером; проверить MAN_OVERRIDE_SPD (после обновления с 1.17 значение −1 означает, что COM_RC_OVERRIDE был 0).

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Return рядом с домом набирает полную высоту.

Cause: d больше RTL_MIN_DIST (10 м по умолчанию) — конус уже не действует.

Fix: поднять RTL_MIN_DIST до нужного радиуса, угол оставить.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
При пропаже телеметрии с наземной станцией ничего не происходит.

Cause: NAV_DLL_ACT = 0 по умолчанию.

Fix: для полётов по миссии без пульта — NAV_DLL_ACT = 2 и COM_DL_LOSS_T по качеству канала.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
После посадки моторы не останавливаются.

Cause: land detector не видит посадку (LNDMC_Z_VEL_MAX больше скорости посадки, вибрации); идёт незавершённая миссия; COM_DISARM_LAND ≤ 0.

Fix: дизарм тумблером; проверить vehicle_land_detected в логе.

PX4: arming, failsafe, Return and geofence — the full matrix
Трёхпозиционный тумблер переключает только два режима, средняя позиция «ничего не делает»

Cause: режим записан в COM_FLTMODE2 или COM_FLTMODE3, а центр канала попадает в слот 4.

Fix: listener manual_control_switches, посмотреть mode_slot в каждой позиции, заполнить именно эти слоты.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Тумблеры пульта не действуют, стики работают

Cause: действующий источник — джойстик MAVLink (COM_RC_IN_MODE = 3 выбрал его первым), а тумблеры обрабатываются только при источнике «пульт».

Fix: COM_RC_IN_MODE = 0 или 5, выключить виртуальный джойстик в QGC, перезагрузить.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Арм отклоняется, в QGC «Kill engaged» или аппарат сам уходит в Return

Cause: неверный знак или величина порога RC_*_TH, либо перевёрнутый канал (RCn_REV).

Fix: посчитать p = 0.5·v + 0.5 для обеих позиций и сравнить с порогом; зеркало 0.75 — это −0.25.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Моторы выключились в полёте в Stabilized или Acro

Cause: задет арм-тумблер — в режимах с ручным газом дизарм с пульта в воздухе разрешён (в 1.18 всегда; до 1.18 — при COM_DISARM_MAN = 1); либо kill.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: «Disarmed by RC switch» или «Kill engaged».

Fix: вернуть тумблер сразу — повторный арм в течение 5 с идёт без проверок; перенести арм на тумблер, который не задевается; для съёмочного аппарата — арм жестом.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
После назначения арм-тумблера перестал работать арм стиками

Cause: прошивка сама записала MAN_ARM_GESTURE = 0.

Fix: это штатно; если тумблер убран (RC_MAP_ARM_SW = 0), верни MAN_ARM_GESTURE = 1 вручную.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Ручка подстройки не меняет параметр

Cause: после перезагрузки потеряна привязка; параметр целочисленный; RC_MAP_PARAMn = 0; управление идёт с джойстика.

Fix: заново Set RC to Param, проверить тип параметра и канал ручки в listener rc_channels.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Параметр прыгнул сразу после привязки, аппарат раскачало

Cause: ручка стояла не в центре, параметр ушёл к value0 + scale·rc.

Consequences: при большом scale — потеря устойчивости.

Fix: ручку в центр до привязки, scale не больше трети value0, min и max заданы всегда.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Аппарат не перехватывается стиками из Mission

Cause: стик двигают медленно — MAN_OVERRIDE_SPD сравнивается со скоростью движения (умолчание 1 /с строже прежнего порога 0.3 /с); либо MAN_OVERRIDE_SPD < 0 (так же переносится старый COM_RC_OVERRIDE = 0); на самолёте перехвата нет.

Fix: резкое движение стика или смена режима тумблером.

PX4: modes on switches, AUX channels and in-flight tuning (RC_MAP_PARAM)
Серва греется, гудит или дёргается сразу после назначения функции.

Cause: группа осталась на 400 Гц (в ней уже была функция) или стоит в DShot.

Fix: PWM_AUX_TIMn группы = 50, перезагрузка; мотор и серву развести по разным группам.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
После включения bidirectional DShot моторы 5 и 6 молчат или нет оборотов в esc_status.

Cause: в 1.18 BDShot выбран только у одной группы (PWM_AUX_TIMn = −6…−8 нужен у каждой группы моторов); в 1.16–1.17 bidirectional работал только на таймере с индексом 0 (первые четыре выхода FMU на H7); в 1.15 его нет; у группы нет DMA; ESC не поддерживает.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: esc_status.esc_rpm = 0 при вращении, dshot status показывает BDShot не на всех группах.

Fix: в 1.18 — −6/−7/−8 в TIMn каждой группы моторов, перезагрузка; в 1.16–1.17 — моторы на первые четыре выхода FMU, для гекса и окта — телеметрия по UART (DSHOT_TEL_CFG); версию прошивки сверить с таблицей в px4-intro.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
QGC на слайдере мотора пишет «command denied».

Cause: аппарат заармлен; кнопка safety есть и не нажата; в 1.17 и раньше ещё COM_MOT_TEST_EN = 0 (в 1.18 параметра нет).

Fix: дизарм, нажать safety (или CBRK_IO_SAFETY = 22027 на плате без кнопки); из консоли — actuator_test set -m 1 -v 0.1 -t 1.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
На земле рули не реагируют на стики.

Cause: COM_PREARM_MODE = 0 — без арма выход стоит в DIS.

Fix: COM_PREARM_MODE = 2 (или 1 с кнопкой safety); проверять слайдерами.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
Самолёт в Stabilized раскачивается сильнее, чем в Manual, или уходит в крен при взятии ручки на себя.

Cause: поверхность идёт против правила знаков, а исправлено знаком TRQ вместо REV; у элевонов перепутан знак TRQ_R.

Fix: слайдер вверх → кромка вверх на каждой поверхности; знаки — по шагу 3.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
После обновления на 1.17 пропал trim поверхности, в QGC предупреждение про TRIM is reset.

Cause: задан хотя бы один CENTn — прошивка обнулила все CA_SV_CSn_TRIM.

Fix: перенести trim в CENTn по формуле MIN + (MAX − MIN)/2 · (1 + TRIM) для каждой сервы на FMU.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
DShot-ESC подключён к MAIN на Pixhawk с IO, моторы не инициализируются.

Cause: IO умеет только PWM и OneShot.

Fix: перенести ESC на AUX (FMU PWM OUT) и переназначить функции.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
Обороты в esc_status не сходятся с расчётом по KV, динамический notch не помогает.

Cause: DSHOT_MOT_POLn этого мотора (до 1.18 — MOT_POLE_COUNT) не равен числу магнитов.

Fix: посчитать магниты на колоколе; 12 полюсов вместо 14 дают ошибку в 7/6.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
Выход Peripheral via Actuator Set не реагирует на команду.

Cause: param7 (индекс) не 0; значение в микросекундах вместо −1…1; выход в группе, занятой триггером или DShot.

Fix: param1…6 в диапазоне −1…1, param7 = 0; проверить группу таймера.

PX4 outputs: functions, timers, servos, control surfaces, relays
The controller doesn't respond to a setpoint

Cause: the topic isn't published or is published at zero rate.

Fix: uorb top, listener <topic> — is there any data.

PX4 architecture: modules and uORB
A module won't start

Cause: it isn't built for this board or depends on a missing driver.

Fix: check the board config and the startup output.

PX4 architecture: modules and uORB
The topic you need isn't in the log

Cause: it isn't included in the logging profile (SDLOG_PROFILE).

Fix: enable the profile, repeat the flight.

PX4 architecture: modules and uORB
Параметр есть в документации, в списке QGC его нет.

Cause: модуль не собран для платы; параметр не используется (ведущий параметр выключен или модуль другого типа аппарата).

Fix: param find <имя>; not found — собирать прошивку через boardconfig; найден — включить ведущий параметр и перезагрузить, либо param touch <имя> и обновить список в QGC.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
После param import всё настроено, после перезагрузки вернулись старые значения.

Cause: импорт не запускает автосохранение.

Fix: после param import или param load набрать param save, проверить last auto save и storage array в param status.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
После обновления прошивки аппарат летает иначе, параметры «никто не трогал».

Cause: значения, равные старому умолчанию, не хранились и получили новые умолчания.

Fix: сравнить .params до и после; таблица изменений — в px4-intro.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
top, dmesg или reboot отвечают command not found.

Cause: это SITL — команд нет в сборке px4/sitl.

Fix: listener cpuload, work_queue status; вывод модулей виден прямо в терминале pxh>.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
listener печатает данные датчика, а QGC сообщает о его потере.

Cause: без -n печатается последнее сообщение, каким бы старым оно ни было.

Fix: listener <топик> -n 5; при Giving up — <драйвер> status и dmesg.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
После правки файлов на SD плата не выходит на связь по USB и телеметрии.

Cause: на карте остался etc/rc.txt или ошибка в config.txt / extras.txt; окончания строк Windows.

Fix: вынуть карту и убрать файлы; для разбора — отладочный UART, 57600 бод.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
При включении мелодия ошибки, в dmesg строка param import failed.

Cause: основной файл параметров повреждён; параметры взяты из копии.

Consequences: если копии не было, борт на умолчаниях — без калибровок и failsafe.

Fix: param status, сверить param show -c с последним бэкапом; файл param_import_fail.bson сохранить для разбора.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
На плате с параметрами во flash изменения, сделанные в полёте, пропали после посадки с отключением питания.

Cause: запись во flash откладывается до дизарма.

Fix: после дизарма подождать 2 с или набрать param save.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
После загрузки чужого .params горизонт завален, курс уходит, проверки молчат.

Cause: чужие CAL_* на плате той же модели.

Fix: QGC → калибровки заново; впредь переносить только show-for-airframe.

PX4 console and parameters: nsh/pxh, param, listener, status, backup
Устройство подключено, параметра его драйвера нет в списке QGC.

Cause: драйвер не собран для этой платы (default.px4board).

Fix: проверить колонку «v6x» в таблицах или файл платы; собрать прошивку с драйвером через boardconfig.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Параметр порта выставлен, драйвер на status отвечает «not running».

Cause: не было перезагрузки; порт уже занят другим драйвером.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: dmesg — строка Conflicting config for /dev/ttyS….

Fix: найти второй параметр с тем же номером порта (чаще всего MAV_1_CONFIG или UXRCE_DDS_CFG на TELEM2), развести, перезагрузить.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
GPS не определяется, gps status показывает перебор скоростей.

Cause: перепутаны TX и RX; приёмник не u-blox при GPS_1_PROTOCOL = 1.

Fix: проверить провода; GPS_1_PROTOCOL = 0 (авто) или 6 для NMEA.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
На 1.16 и раньше мультиротор «отстаёт» по GPS при резких манёврах.

Cause: airframe поставил GPS_UBX_DYNMODEL = 6 (airborne < 1 g).

Fix: GPS_UBX_DYNMODEL = 7, перезагрузка; в 1.17 это умолчание.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
«Compass inconsistent», в Position аппарат ходит кругами.

Cause: неверный CAL_MAGn_ROT внешнего компаса; внутренний компас рядом с силовыми проводами.

Fix: перекалибровать с SENS_MAG_AUTOROT = 1; внутреннему CAL_MAGn_PRIO = 0; при помехах от тока — CAL_MAG_COMP_TYP.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Дальномер есть в listener distance_sensor, высота над землёй не используется.

Cause: датчик смотрит не вниз, а драйвер считает, что вниз; оценка высоты по дальномеру не включена.

Fix: проверить поле orientation в топике; EKF2_RNG_CTRL — по модулю nav-terrain.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Самолёт не армится: «Preflight Fail: Airspeed invalid».

Cause: SYS_HAS_NUM_ASPD = 1, а драйвер датчика не включён (SENS_EN_*) или выключен UAVCAN_SUB_ASPD; датчик не откалиброван.

Fix: включить драйвер и перезагрузить; если датчика нет — SYS_HAS_NUM_ASPD = 0 и FW_USE_AIRSPD = 0.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Телеметрия в QGC обновляется рывками, ATTITUDE 5–7 Гц.

Cause: набор потоков больше MAV_0_RATE; модем сообщает малый txbuf.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: mavlink status — tx rate mult заметно ниже 1.

Fix: поднять MAV_0_RATE до бод / 20 (значение 0) или снизить частоты командой mavlink stream; поднять air rate модема.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
QGC пишет о потере связи («data link lost»), хотя модем на связи.

Cause: на радиоканале режим с высокими частотами (MAV_n_MODE = 2) или MAV_n_RATE больше, чем пропускает модем: его буфер переполнен, пакеты теряются.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: mavlink status — малый txbuf, tx rate mult у нижней границы.

Fix: режим 0 или 13 (Low Bandwidth) для радио, Onboard — только для проводного companion; MAV_n_RATE не выше бод / 20.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
В полёте светодиод был красным, потом снова стал зелёным или синим, пищалка батареи замолчала.

Cause: батарея прошла уровень Critical и дошла до Emergency; при COM_LOW_BAT_ACT = 0 для этого уровня нет ни цвета, ни мелодии.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: battery_status.warning = 3; в QGC — сообщение об опасно низком заряде.

Consequences: отключение ESC по напряжению и падение.

Fix: садиться немедленно; на земле задать COM_LOW_BAT_ACT = 2, 3 или 5, чтобы уровень Emergency запускал посадку.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Узел DroneCAN не появляется в uavcan status.

Cause: UAVCAN_ENABLE = 0; разный битрейт; нет терминатора; конфликт номеров при значении 1.

Fix: UAVCAN_ENABLE = 2, UAVCAN_BITRATE как у узла, терминаторы 120 Ом на концах; для ESC — значение 3.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
OSD пустой.

Cause: перепутаны TX и RX; msp_osd нет в сборке платы; видеосистема ждёт не DisplayPort (прошивка 1.15).

Fix: msp_osd status; проверить версию PX4 и сборку; порт VTX — 115200.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
Пищалка молчит при загрузке и арме.

Cause: CBRK_BUZZER = 782097; пищалка стоит в модуле GPS, а он не подключён.

Fix: CBRK_BUZZER = 0, перезагрузка; tune_control play -t 1.

PX4 peripherals: GPS/compass, rangefinder, airspeed, telemetry, DroneCAN, OSD, LED and buzzer
gimbal status отвечает «not running», подвес мёртв.

Cause: MNT_MODE_IN = −1 (значение по умолчанию) или плата не перезагружена после смены.

Fix: выставить 0, 1 или 4, перезагрузить, повторить gimbal status.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
gimbal test pitch -45 принят, а серва стоит.

Cause: аппарат без арма и prearm выключен — выход в DIS; функция 421 не назначена; серва в группе таймера с DShot.

Fix: COM_PREARM_MODE, экран Actuators, группы таймеров по px4-outputs-servos. Перед проверкой убрать руки из зоны хода.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Подвес не доходит до надира, останавливается на −45°.

Cause: MNT_MIN_PITCH = −45 (по умолчанию; до 1.18 — MNT_RANGE_PITCH = 90) — команда обрезается до −45°.

Fix: MNT_MIN_PITCH = −90 и пересчитать соответствие импульсов нижней половины хода; верхний предел можно оставить.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Ручка пульта подвес не двигает.

Cause: RC_MAP_AUXn = 0; в режиме Auto ход ручки меньше 0.25 по шкале −1…+1 (12.5 % полного хода); MNT_MODE_IN = 4.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: manual_control_setpoint.aux2 постоянно 0 либо меняется, а gimbal_controls — нет.

Fix: назначить канал, сдвинуть ручку на большой ход, проверить режим входа.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Сервоподвес с MNT_DO_STAB = 1 и ручкой в режиме скорости (MNT_RC_IN_MODE = 1) при наклонённой раме уходит в упор.

Cause: не подтверждено; по чтению output.cpp 1.17.0 вычитание наклона рамы в этом сочетании могло накапливаться от цикла к циклу (возможный дефект прошивки). В 1.18 этот путь переписан (set_last_valid_setpoint возвращает угол рамы в запомненную уставку); с наклонённой рамой ни 1.17, ни 1.18 не запускались.

Fix: прежде чем полагаться на это сочетание, проверь на столе — наклони раму на 10°: камера должна вернуться к горизонту и остановиться, а не уехать в упор; уходит — MNT_RC_IN_MODE = 0.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
После обновления на 1.18 ручка в режиме скорости почти не двигает подвес (градус в секунду вместо десятков).

Cause: MNT_TAU = 0.3 (умолчание 1.18) — фильтр внутри цикла накопления пропускает около 1/30 каждой прибавки.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: manual_control_setpoint.aux2 держится, gimbal_controls растёт медленно и равномерно.

Fix: MNT_TAU = 0 или режим угла (MNT_RC_IN_MODE = 0, при MNT_DO_STAB ≠ 0).

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
На 1.18 gimbal test pitch …, ручка в режиме угла или команда наземки с флагом lock не двигают сервоподвес, а ручка в режиме скорости двигает.

Cause: MNT_DO_STAB = 0 — в 1.18 уставка в мировой системе на выход AUX не проходит (проверено в SITL v1.18.0).

Fix: сервоподвес без контроллера — MNT_DO_STAB = 1 (или 3 для тангажа); подвес со своим IMU — режим скорости ручки и MNT_TAU = 0, проверка на столе.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Companion получает DENIED на DO_GIMBAL_MANAGER_PITCHYAW.

Cause: primary control у другого компонента (пульт, наземка, автопилот после миссии).

Fix: сначала DO_GIMBAL_MANAGER_CONFIGURE со своими id; проверить gimbal status и GIMBAL_MANAGER_STATUS.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
MAVLink-подвес молчит, в статусе «gimbal device compid not found».

Cause: подвес не шлёт GIMBAL_DEVICE_INFORMATION (не поддерживает роль device v2), перепутаны TX и RX, не та скорость порта, system id подвеса не равен MAV_SYS_ID.

Fix: mavlink status на порту, проверить проводку и скорость; не умеет v2 — MNT_MODE_OUT = 1.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Кадров на карте вдвое меньше плана, шаг удвоен.

Cause: TRIG_MIN_INTERVA больше интервала D / v — драйвер пропускает спуски.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: интервалы между записями camera_trigger около 2 · D / v.

Fix: TRIG_MIN_INTERVA = цикл камеры, скорость не выше D / цикл.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
В логе все команды на месте, а кадров меньше.

Cause: камера не успевает (интервал меньше её цикла), короткий TRIG_ACT_TIME, неверная полярность.

Fix: снизить скорость или увеличить шаг; TRIG_ACT_TIME по паспорту камеры; поставить обратную связь, чтобы пропуски были видны в camera_capture.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Камера строчит без остановки сразу после начала участка Survey.

Cause: TRIG_MODE = 1 или 2 — команда миссии запустила таймер с TRIG_INTERVAL = 40 мс.

Fix: TRIG_MODE = 4, перезагрузка.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
Ортофотоплан сдвинут вдоль галсов, соседние галсы «гребёнкой».

Cause: не учтена задержка затвора; галсы летят навстречу друг другу, сдвиг v · τ имеет разный знак.

Consequences: без контрольных точек ошибка удваивается на стыке галсов.

Fix: поправка времени на τ при геотеггинге либо обратная связь с башмака.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
После включения CAM_CAP_FBACK топик camera_capture пуст.

Cause: функция 2032 не назначена («No capture channel configured»), неверный CAM_CAP_EDGE, нет сигнала с башмака, TRIG_MODE = 0.

Fix: camera_capture status — строки «Capture enabled» и «Frame sequence»; проверить сигнал осциллографом.

PX4: gimbal, camera trigger and geotags
В QGC «Invalid configuration: FW_AIRSPD_STALL higher FW_AIRSPD_MIN».

Cause: FW_AIRSPD_STALL > FW_AIRSPD_MIN, часто после пересчёта V_s без пересчёта MIN.

Fix: MIN = 1.25 V_s, TRIM и VT_ARSP_TRANS — по тем же правилам.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
«Quad-chute triggered due to transition timeout» на каждом переходе.

Cause: с датчиком скорость не дошла до VT_ARSP_BLEND за VT_F_TR_OL_TM; без датчика VT_F_TR_OL_TM > VT_TRANS_TIMEOUT; на высокогорье все времена выросли в (ρ₀/ρ)^1.5 раз.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: airspeed_validated.calibrated_airspeed_m_s растёт медленно, газ тянущего мотора на VT_F_TRANS_THR.

Fix: поднять VT_F_TRANS_THR или VT_F_TR_OL_TM, сохранить VT_TRANS_TIMEOUT ≥ OL + 3 с, проверить датчик скорости.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
При посадке или возврате (Land, RTL) аппарат внезапно разгоняет тянущий мотор и начинает переход вперёд.

Cause: тумблер перехода переведён (или оставлен) в FW: запрос тумблером в Land и RTL не блокируется, отклоняется только команда MAVLink; отмена при RTL срабатывает лишь в момент входа в RTL.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: vtol_vehicle_status уходит в переход при vehicle_status.nav_state Land или RTL; manual_control_switches.transition_switch = FW.

Consequences: разгон у земли, выход из зоны посадки, quad-chute на малой высоте.

Fix: вернуть тумблер в MC; впредь держать его в MC всё время, пока аппарат в Land, RTL или Takeoff.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
Без датчика аппарат проседает сразу после начала перехода.

Cause: VT_TRANS_MIN_TM = 2 с по умолчанию — подъёмные моторы теряют вес к 2 с, а скорости ещё нет.

Consequences: при VT_QC_T_ALT_LOSS = 0 проседание не ловится ничем.

Fix: VT_TRANS_MIN_TM по опорному полёту + 30 %.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
«Quad-chute triggered due to maximum pitch angle exceeded» при переходе назад.

Cause: тангаж торможения выше VT_FW_QC_P — проверка действует и в переходе назад.

Fix: VT_FW_QC_P выше максимального тангажа торможения в логе на 10°; или уменьшить VT_B_DEC_I.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
После quad-chute на земле не армится: «VTOL fixed-wing system failure detected».

Cause: флаг fixed_wing_system_failure держится до нового запроса перехода в FW.

Fix: найти причину в логе (сообщение называет её), перезагрузить аппарат.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
В Position после quad-chute аппарат висит на месте, Return не начался.

Cause: COM_QC_ACT применяется только в Mission, Hold, Takeoff и VTOL Takeoff.

Fix: это поведение кода; в ручных режимах пилот сам выбирает Return.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
В висении рули не двигаются при наклоне.

Cause: VT_ELEV_MC_LOCK = 1 — так и должно быть.

Fix: проверять рули в самолётном режиме (переход на столе без арма).

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
При броске мотор не запускается; или запускается сразу при арме в руках.

Cause: FW_LAUN_AC_THLD выше реального броска; или FW_LAUN_DETCN_ON = 0.

What you see in the log/on the instrument: launch_detection_status.launch_detection_state остаётся в ожидании.

Fix: детектор включить; порог не ниже 2 g, чтобы встряхивание не запускало мотор.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
Миссия VTOL отклонена при загрузке.

Cause: нет посадки при MIS_TKO_LAND_REQ = 2; угол глиссады больше FW_LND_ANG.

Fix: добавить посадку или отодвинуть точку LOITER_TO_ALT.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
VTOL проскакивает точку посадки в переходе назад.

Cause: реальное торможение меньше VT_B_DEC_MSS; попутный ветер.

Fix: по логу посчитать среднее торможение, поставить его в VT_B_DEC_MSS; заходить против ветра.

PX4 from scratch: plane and VTOL
Fast oscillations after autotune

Cause: the noise isn't filtered, and autotune picked gains for a noisy signal.

Fix: filters, autotune again.

PX4 control loops and tuning
In Position the vehicle slowly “sways”

Cause: the outer loop (MPC_*) is too aggressive with a weak inner loop.

Fix: rate/attitude first, then position.

PX4 control loops and tuning
Hot motors

Cause: high D on a noisy gyro.

Fix: lower D, increase filtering, check the mechanics.

PX4 control loops and tuning
A motor spins the wrong way in the test

Cause: the geometry or direction in Actuators.

Fix: fix CA_ROTOR<n>_KM/the direction, recheck with props off.

PX4 control loops and tuning
Autotune aborts

Cause: wind, vibration, a starting tune that is too low, not enough altitude.

Fix: calm air, spectrum and notch before autotune, a lower MC_ROLLRATE_K, altitude 5+ m.

PX4 control loops and tuning
Slow position oscillations in Position mode

Cause: MPC_XY_P/MPC_XY_VEL_* higher than GPS quality/latency allows.

Fix: check the EKF (innovations), lower MPC_XY_VEL_P_ACC, then MPC_XY_P.

PX4 control loops and tuning
ros2 topic list is empty

Cause: the agent isn't running, wrong port, px4_msgs version mismatch.

Fix: start the agent on 8888, check the px4_msgs branch.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
It doesn't switch to Offboard

Cause: OffboardControlMode wasn't streamed before the command.

Fix: send the stream at ≥ 2 Hz in advance, then the mode.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
It gets kicked out of Offboard in flight

Cause: the OffboardControlMode rate dropped below ~2 Hz.

Fix: the node's timer, check the companion's load.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
It flies up instead of down

Cause: the sign of Z in NED.

Fix: Z is positive down, an altitude of 3 m is Z = −3.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
PX4 won't enter Offboard
