Cause: setpoints weren't sent for ≥ 1 s before the command, rate < 2 Hz, wrong QoS.

Fix: stream before the mode change, 10+ Hz, BEST_EFFORT QoS; commander status.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
Takeoff “into the ground” in SITL

Cause: the sign of z (NED).

Fix: z is negative up; check the ENU/NED conversion.

PX4 and ROS 2: uXRCE-DDS and offboard
Permission denied: /dev/ttyTHS*

Cause: the user isn't in dialout, or there is a console on the port.

Fix: usermod -aG dialout, log in again, systemctl disable --now nvgetty.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS
The port opens, but there is no data

Cause: the wrong ttyTHS, TX/RX not crossed, no common ground, MAVLink not enabled on TELEM2.

Fix: the loopback from step 2, check the wiring, MAV_1_CONFIG.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS
MAVLink works, uXRCE-DDS doesn't

Cause: MAVLink is still on the same port, the agent runs at a different baud rate, px4_msgs is a different version.

Fix: MAV_1_CONFIG = Disabled, the same baud, px4_msgs from the branch that matches the firmware.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS
The topics are there, but ros2 topic echo is silent

Cause: a QoS mismatch (PX4 publishes best effort).

Fix: a subscriber with qos_profile_sensor_data, ros2 topic echo --qos-reliability best_effort.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS
Detection latency grows after 10 minutes of flight

Cause: Jetson throttling.

Fix: airflow, the nvpmodel mode, tegrastats in the flight log.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS
The Jetson reboots under throttle

Cause: the shared BEC sags.

Fix: a separate DC-DC with headroom, a capacitor on the input, measure the voltage with an oscilloscope under load.

PX4 + Jetson Orin: companion over UART, MAVLink and uXRCE-DDS

Elective: Mathematical foundations

After conversion to ROS the drone “flies south” and the camera points down and backward

Cause: both substitutions NED→ENU and FRD→FLU weren't made, or only one was.

Fix: check the signs of y and z separately for position and for attitude; yaw_enu = 90° − yaw_ned.

Vectors, matrices and rotations: the language of attitude
A quaternion from MAVLink gives a rotation that is “almost right, but mirrored”

Cause: (w,x,y,z) order versus (x,y,z,w).

Fix: check that the scalar part ≈ 1 at zero angles; reorder at the boundary.

Vectors, matrices and rotations: the language of attitude
The attitude estimate slowly “stretches”, the angles drift while stationary

Cause: the quaternion isn't normalized after the update.

Fix: normalize after every multiplication; monitor |q| − 1.

Vectors, matrices and rotations: the language of attitude
Euler angles jump by ±180° in the log

Cause: not a bug — yaw range wrap-around or pitch passing through 90°.

Fix: use the quaternion/matrix for analysis, angles only for display.

Vectors, matrices and rotations: the language of attitude
When you raise P, the loop oscillates at a frequency that doesn't depend on P

Cause: it's delay, not inertia: ω·L ≈ π/2; the frequency is set by the total delay.

Fix: reduce the delay (filters, RC smoothing) instead of cranking P.

Differential equations and dynamics: from the motor to the loop
Slow (1–2 Hz) wobbles with a good fast response

Cause: too much I — the third pole has moved to the right.

Fix: lower I or add anti-windup/an I limit.

Differential equations and dynamics: from the motor to the loop
The numpy model is unstable even though the analysis says “stable”

Cause: the integration step is too large (forward Euler) relative to τ.

Fix: T_s ≤ τ/10 or an implicit scheme.

Differential equations and dynamics: from the motor to the loop
The D-term is noisy, the motors are hot, and the response is no sharper

Cause: D amplifies frequencies above 1/τ_m that the motor can't follow.

Fix: a D-term filter at ~1/(2πτ_m)·3, less D, an RPM filter.

Differential equations and dynamics: from the motor to the loop
In the EKF logs the normalized innovations are always small, but the position “floats”

Cause: *_NSE are set too high — the filter barely trusts the measurements, the gates are wide.

Fix: lower the measurement noise to the real σ (from a recording at rest).

Probability and estimation: noise, Gaussians, fusion
Frequent GPS rejections by the gates despite good HDOP

Cause: POSNE_M_NSE is set too low, or vibration has spoiled the prediction (large P).

Fix: a realistic GPS σ is 0.5–1.5 m; deal with vibration first.

Probability and estimation: noise, Gaussians, fusion
Allan deviation has no plateau and rises at large τ

Cause: temperature drift during the recording.

Fix: warm the sensor up for 10 min, record at a stable temperature; account for temp-cal.

Probability and estimation: noise, Gaussians, fusion
“EKF variance” right after takeoff with no GPS problems

Cause: the position σ grows as t^1.5 (the variance P as t³) when there are no position measurements; the source isn't configured.

Fix: check EK3_SRC1_POSXY and that the source actually delivers data.

Probability and estimation: noise, Gaussians, fusion

Elective: ROS 2 on board

ros2 topic echo /fmu/out/... is silent, even though topic list shows it

Cause: QoS — a RELIABLE subscription to a BEST_EFFORT publisher; or the _v1 version suffix is missing.

Fix: --qos-reliability best_effort; check the exact topic name.

ROS 2 onboard: nodes, topics, QoS, tf2, bag
A laptop on the same network “interferes” with the vehicle's topics

Cause: a shared ROS_DOMAIN_ID and open discovery.

Fix: your own domain, localhost-only on board, an explicit bridge to the outside.

ROS 2 onboard: nodes, topics, QoS, tf2, bag
Tf2 “extrapolation into the future” when replaying a bag

Cause: the nodes run on system time, the bag on recording time.

Fix: ros2 bag play --clock and use_sim_time:=true on all nodes.

ROS 2 onboard: nodes, topics, QoS, tf2, bag
The vehicle flies “sideways” after the coordinate conversion

Cause: ENU/NED or FLU/FRD mixed up, or the quaternion component order.

Fix: conversion tests (frames.py), a check on a stationary vehicle with a known heading.

ROS 2 onboard: nodes, topics, QoS, tf2, bag
PX4 refuses to switch to Offboard

Cause: OffboardControlMode was not streaming before the mode request or is sent at less than 2 Hz; no valid local position.

Fix: STREAM_SETPOINTS ≥ 1 s; a 10–20 Hz timer; check xy_valid/z_valid.

ROS 2 + PX4: uXRCE-DDS, an offboard node as a state machine
The node doesn't see /fmu/out/vehicle_status

Cause: the version suffix (_v1) or RELIABLE QoS.

Fix: the name from MESSAGE_VERSION; the BEST_EFFORT profile.

ROS 2 + PX4: uXRCE-DDS, an offboard node as a state machine
The vehicle goes up and down “the wrong way round”

Cause: altitude given as positive in NED.

Fix: z = −altitude; test the conversions.

ROS 2 + PX4: uXRCE-DDS, an offboard node as a state machine
After the pilot switches to Position, the vehicle “jerks” back into Offboard

Cause: the node keeps sending the mode request.

Fix: detect the intervention and stop sending commands (the DONE state).

ROS 2 + PX4: uXRCE-DDS, an offboard node as a state machine
No /ap/* topics

Cause: SITL was built without --enable-DDS, DDS_ENABLE = 0, the agent is on a different port or in a different domain.

Fix: build with DDS; port 2019; the same ROS_DOMAIN_ID/DDS_DOMAIN_ID.

ROS 2 + ArduPilot: AP_DDS and MAVROS, one node, two routes
arm_motors returns false

Cause: the mode doesn't allow arming or the pre-arm checks haven't passed.

Fix: GUIDED first; /ap/prearm_check; STATUSTEXT in the GCS.

ROS 2 + ArduPilot: AP_DDS and MAVROS, one node, two routes
cmd_vel setpoints are ignored on the ground

Cause: takeoff in GUIDED wasn't performed.

Fix: the takeoff service, wait for the altitude, then the velocities.

ROS 2 + ArduPilot: AP_DDS and MAVROS, one node, two routes
The vehicle flies “east instead of north”

Cause: an ENU setpoint was sent as NED (or vice versa), wrong frame_id.

Fix: frame_id map for world ENU; convert only at the boundary.

ROS 2 + ArduPilot: AP_DDS and MAVROS, one node, two routes
When the neural network hangs, the vehicle “freezes” with its setpoints

Cause: inference and the setpoint timer are on the same executor thread.

Fix: separate callback groups, MultiThreadedExecutor, or perception as a separate process.

Architecture of an onboard ROS 2 application: nodes, lifecycle, watchdog
Tracking switches on and off several times a second

Cause: no hysteresis on input recovery.

Fix: recover_s ≥ 0.5–1 s.

Architecture of an onboard ROS 2 application: nodes, lifecycle, watchdog
After the companion reboots, the application doesn't start

Cause: the service starts before the serial port/network appears.

Fix: systemd dependencies (After/BindsTo), connection retries in the bridge.

Architecture of an onboard ROS 2 application: nodes, lifecycle, watchdog
The bag and the autopilot log don't line up in time

Cause: the companion's clock isn't synchronized.

Fix: chrony, use the autopilot's timestamps in the messages.

Architecture of an onboard ROS 2 application: nodes, lifecycle, watchdog
The path runs right next to the obstacle, the vehicle clips it

Cause: no inflation, or the radius is smaller than the vehicle size + position error.

Fix: inflation = radius + 2σ of position + margin.

Navigation and obstacle avoidance: maps, planners, setpoints
The vehicle “jerks”, replanning every 100 ms changes the path

Cause: the global planner runs at the local planner's rate, a noisy map.

Fix: global plan at 1 Hz, hysteresis on path changes, a map filter (log-odds).

Navigation and obstacle avoidance: maps, planners, setpoints
The vehicle flies into an unknown area and loses the obstacle from its field of view

Cause: unknown is treated as free; the sensor isn't looking where you're flying.

Fix: unknown = occupied; yaw along the direction of travel.

Navigation and obstacle avoidance: maps, planners, setpoints
The setpoints along the path lead the wrong way

Cause: an ENU path was sent to PX4 as NED.

Fix: convert at the boundary (frames.py), test on a known point.

Navigation and obstacle avoidance: maps, planners, setpoints
The ported node “hangs” on a service call

Cause: a synchronous call in a callback of a single-threaded executor.

Fix: call_async + handling the future, or a separate callback group.

From ROS 1 to ROS 2: reading old projects and porting them
The node doesn't receive MAVROS 2 telemetry

Cause: QoS (sensor data on the publisher) or a different topic name.

Fix: ros2 topic info -v, the subscription profile.

From ROS 1 to ROS 2: reading old projects and porting them
Parameters “don't apply”

Cause: in ROS 2 a parameter must be declared (declare_parameter) and passed to a specific node.

Fix: declare it in the constructor, YAML with the node name in launch.

From ROS 1 to ROS 2: reading old projects and porting them
Ros1_bridge doesn't pass your custom message type

Cause: the bridge was built without this type or the definitions differ.

Fix: build the bridge from source with both workspaces; better yet — port.
